Method: 提出 \OURS{}：在每一步的损失评估之外，引入轨迹感知代理评估候选的长期有效性；使用参考策略正则化保持选择的稳健性；利用判别器估计的卡方校正调整选择概率，从而鼓励在后续步骤仍有效的候选。

Result: 在 HarmBench 基准上，\OURS{} 在相同搜索预算下持续超越强基线，显著提升攻击成功率，并表现出更平稳的优化过程。

Conclusion: 缓解因短视候选选择导致的选择阶段奖励作弊是提升 jailbreak 后缀优化的关键，轨迹感知的选择框架能够显著改善攻击效果和优化稳定性。

Abstract: Gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods typically update the suffix by retaining the candidate with the lowest current loss. We show that this seemingly natural design is fundamentally myopic: candidates that look better under the current-step proxy often fail to produce better jailbreak outcomes later in the search, revealing a form of selection-stage reward hacking. This suggests that candidate selection, rather than candidate generation alone, is a hidden bottleneck in suffix optimization. To address this issue, we propose \OURS{}, a trajectory-aware candidate selection framework for jailbreak suffix optimization. Instead of selecting candidates solely by their immediate loss, \OURS{} augments per-step evaluation with a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizes selection with reference-policy regularization and a discriminator-estimated chi-squared correction, encouraging choices that remain effective beyond the current step. Experiments on HarmBench show that \OURS{} consistently outperforms strong baselines under the same search budget, substantially improving attack success rates while exhibiting more stable optimization behavior throughout the search. Our findings highlight that mitigating selection-stage reward hacking caused by myopic candidate selection is critical for improving jailbreak suffix optimization.

</details>


### [74] [Which one is banana man? Evaluating vision-language models in multi-turn pragmatic interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29571)
*Alvin Wei Ming Tan,Ben Prystawski,Veronica Boyce*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 人类在迭代指称游戏中能灵活利用上下文理解指称，而当前视觉语言模型虽能利用已有上下文，但难以有效构建并利用相关上下文，表现出协作语言能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类与视觉语言模型在多轮指称任务中对上下文的敏感性与语用推理能力，以评估模型在实际对话协作中的核心技能。

Method: 让人类参与者和多种视觉语言模型在迭代指称游戏中识别描述的指称对象，系统性变更上下文的量、顺序和相关性，比较其准确率和利用上下文的程度。

Result: 人类在所有条件下均表现稳定且高效；模型能够利用已给出的上下文信息，但在需要自行累积、筛选相关上下文以解释新指称时表现不佳，准确率显著低于人类。

Conclusion: 现有视觉语言模型缺乏构建和利用多轮语用上下文的核心能力，限制了其在高效语言协作中的应用，需要进一步研究提升上下文敏感的语用推理机制。

Abstract: Flexible adaptation to context and shared pragmatic intuitions contribute to smooth human conversation. Iterated reference games---in which players repeatedly pick out novel referents using language---present a test case for agents' ability to perform context-sensitive pragmatic reasoning in multi-turn linguistic environments. We tested humans and vision--language models on their ability to identify the intended meaning of descriptions produced in iterated reference games, varying the provided context in terms of amount, order, and relevance. While humans performed well consistently, the models we evaluated could make use of prior context to interpret humans' referring expressions, but they struggled to build up the relevant context to interpret those expressions effectively. Our results suggest that the models we evaluated lack core skills needed for efficient linguistic collaboration.

</details>


### [75] [SemTrace: Source-Grounded Semantic Signatures for Tracing LLM Exposure to Protected Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29575)
*Junyan Zhang,Yudong Zeng,Yongwei Huang,Zuhao Ouyang,Hong Chen,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SemTrace，一种基于语义的水印，通过在受保护文档中嵌入二进制签名并在审稿时要求模型复述特定事实，以检测生成文本是否受该文档影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被用于阅读文档并生成下游文本，文档所有者难以控制或检查模型，导致来源可追溯性（provenance）问题。需要一种不依赖模型内部修改、且与文档语义紧密关联的检测手段。

Method: SemTrace 为每份受保护的 PDF 隐蔽地嵌入一个内容合约：从文档中挑选若干二元事实对，每对选取一个事实作为二进制位。审稿指令要求模型在固定的评审槽位中复述这些事实，保持独立评价不变。随后使用冻结的自然语言推理（NLI）模型解码生成的文本，提取显式擦除的语义证据，并将恢复的比特序列与预先分配的码字进行比对，以判断是否存在泄露。

Result: 该设计实现了模型无关的复制曝光检测，水印与源文档的语义内容直接绑定，避免了传统基于 token 概率偏置或表面形式模式的水印方法的局限。

Conclusion: SemTrace 提供了一种可在不改动模型生成行为的前提下，利用文档内部事实进行语义水印的方案，为保护文档版权和追溯生成文本来源提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to read documents and produce downstream text, creating a provenance problem when the document owner cannot control or inspect the model that performs the generation. We introduce SemTrace, a source-grounded semantic watermark for detecting whether a generated review was influenced by a known protected manuscript copy. Rather than biasing token probabilities or imposing surface-form patterns, SemTrace constructs a document-specific binary signature from factual propositions that are directly supported by the manuscript itself. A protected PDF invisibly carries a content contract that selects one fact from each binary pair and asks an instruction-following reviewer to express those facts in fixed review slots without changing its independent evaluation. A frozen natural language inference model then decodes the resulting semantic evidence with explicit erasures and scores the recovered bits against the codeword assigned to that copy. This design targets model-agnostic, assigned-copy exposure detection while keeping the watermark semantically tied to the source document.

</details>


### [76] [SUP-MIMIC: A Multi-Task Clinical Diagnosis Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs' Robustness to Contradictory Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29582)
*Yi Yu,Bo Wang,Chong Feng,Ge Shi,Xia Liu,Ziyi Yang,Xuewen Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SUP-MIMIC框架，通过基本评估、诊断分歧任务和诊断收敛任务，系统评估LLM在临床诊断歧义和收敛情境下的推理能力，发现现有模型在这些任务上表现显著下降，存在保守倾向和潜在漏诊风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估主要关注事实检索，忽视临床指标与诊断之间的非一一映射关系，无法检验模型在诊断歧义（相同症状对应不同病因）和诊断收敛（不同症状指向同一疾病）情境下的因果推理能力。

Method: 构建SUP-MIMIC多任务基准，基于MIMIC‑IV‑v3.1数据集设计三类任务：① 基础评估（BA）检验常规问答能力；② 诊断分歧任务（DDT）让模型在表型相似的病例中进行“一对多”消歧；③ 诊断收敛任务（DCT）要求模型在不同病理路径的病例中识别“多对一”诊断模式。对主流LLM进行系统评测，并与基线任务比较。

Result: 在DDT和DCT上，最先进的LLM相较于基础评估任务表现显著下降，说明模型更依赖统计关联而非真实因果推理；此外模型倾向于给出“健康”预测，暗示在真实临床环境中可能导致漏诊。

Conclusion: SUP-MIMIC提供了量化临床推理鲁棒性的严谨方法，揭示了当前LLM在诊断歧义与收敛场景下的局限性，为提升语言模型在医学安全性方面指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Current evaluations of large language models (LLMs) primarily focus on factual knowledge retrieval, overlooking the fundamental challenge of navigating the complex, non-bijective mappings between clinical indicators and diagnoses. Existing benchmarks fail to assess whether large language models truly possess the reasoning capability required for diagnostic ambiguity scenarios, where identical clinical presentations may correspond to different etiologies, and diagnostic convergence scenarios, where heterogeneous symptoms ultimately indicate the same disease. To address this issue, we propose SUP-MIMIC, a multi-task framework utilizing MIMIC-IV-v3.1 that comprises Basic Assessment (BA), Diagnostic Divergence Task (DDT), and Diagnostic Convergence Task (DCT). Specifically, DDT is designed to evaluate the model's "one-to-many" disambiguation capability among phenotypically similar cases, while DCT assesses the model's ability to identify "many-to-one" diagnostic patterns across different pathophysiological pathways. Comprehensive evaluation of state-of-the-art LLMs reveals substantial performance degradation on DDT and DCT compared to baseline tasks, exposing a systemic reliance on statistical shortcuts over genuine causal reasoning. Our findings further highlight a conservative bias toward "healthy" predictions, implying non-trivial risks for missed diagnoses in realistic medical settings. This work establishes a rigorous methodology for quantifying clinical reasoning robustness and provides a roadmap for enhancing the safety of language models in clinical medicine.

</details>


### [77] [How You Ask Shapes What You Get: A Theory-Seeded Measurement of Articulation in Advice-Seeking LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29591)
*Juneha Baek,Suhyeon Lee,Donghyuk Shin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现用户在求助时的表达方式（如详细约束或模糊需求）形成可测的潜在维度，这些维度与话题无关且影响语言模型的回答质量，尤其是一种长篇但信息贫乏的表达会导致模型给出更短、更模糊的答案且不主动澄清。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将用户表达方式的差异视为噪声并直接平均，导致忽视了表达方式本身的结构及其对模型响应的影响。作者希望揭示并量化这种结构，以改进评估和模型行为。

Method: 从三个公开聊天语料库（WildChat、LMSYS、ShareChat）中收集16,447条求助提示，提取可解释特征并使用潜在因子分析/主题模型等方法发现可重复的表达因素。通过跨训练‑测试划分和跨语料库验证其稳健性，并分析这些因素与话题的独立性以及对模型回答的影响。

Result: 发现少数几种可复现的表达风格，其中一种占最大语料约1/6，表现为长篇但信息量低。该风格下模型倾向给出更短、更模糊的回答且不提出澄清问题；而另一种同样信息不足的风格则会主动询问澄清。该现象在所有话题和长度分层中均一致，且两位独立人工标注者能够复现此对比。

Conclusion: 表达方式是与话题独立的关键维度，应在基准测试中对其进行分层评估。作者提供了提取的表达结构作为度量工具，建议未来的评估和模型改进考虑该维度。

Abstract: Users articulate the same advice-seeking request in different ways: some specify detailed constraints, others gesture at a vague need. Prior work treats this variation as noise to be averaged away; we instead treat it as a stable, measurable structure in the input distribution. We ask whether articulation (how people ask) forms latent dimensions separable from topic (what they ask about), and whether it is associated with how language models respond. We extract interpretable features from 16,447 advice-seeking prompts pooled from public chat corpora (WildChat, LMSYS, and ShareChat) and recover a small set of latent articulation factors that replicate across train/test splits and across corpora. Because this structure is largely separable from topic, the populations it defines cut across topics and stay invisible to topic- or task-based evaluation. The factors define a handful of recurring articulation styles, one of which stands out: a long-form but information-poor style, roughly one in six prompts in the largest corpus, where models return shorter, vaguer answers and do not ask for clarification even though under-specification is exactly the condition that warrants it. The contrast holds within every topic group and length quintile, and is not under-specification alone -- a second, equally under-specified style does draw clarifying questions. Two independent human annotators reproduce this contrast. We argue that benchmarks should stratify on articulation, and we offer the extracted structure as a measurement instrument for doing so.

</details>


### [78] [Hindsight Memory-PRM: Supervising Memory Management with Auditable Hindsight Credit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29605)
*Haoxuan Jia,Yang Liu,Yingguang Yang,Yancheng Chen,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Qian Li,Yulin Huang,Jianshen Zhang,Yongzhi Qi,Shang Luo,Kefu Xu,Hao Peng,Junyu Lu,Du Cheng,Philip S. Yu,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过利用长时记忆轨迹中的检索命中和答案引用，Hindsight Memory-PRM 在离线训练记忆效用评估器并在线校准记忆奖励，实现了无需人工标签的高效记忆监督，使 8B 模型在 LoCoMo 上达到 77.5% 的准确率，显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序 LLM 代理的记忆操作难以直接监督，因为操作的价值在执行时不可观测；然而这些操作会在轨迹中留下机器可读的审计痕迹（检索命中、答案引用），如果能利用这些痕迹就能实现无标签的记忆评估与优化。

Method: 提出 Hindsight Memory-PRM：① 离线阶段利用检索命中和答案引用训练一个条件记忆效用评估器（memory-utility critic），该评估器以操作为条件输出效用分数；② 在线阶段，对每个探测进行一次受控的删除‑重新回答操作，结合检索、引用信息以及评估器输出，计算干预校准的条目级存在信用，并沿记忆版本链向上传递，形成动作级代理奖励。整个过程不依赖人工标签，也不需要对未来轨迹进行 Monte‑Carlo 回放。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 数据集上，使用 8B 参数的本地策略在固定共享阅读器下取得 77.5% 的成功率，超过其 API 教师的 65.1% 以及所有已复现的外部系统；且仅使用 Mem0 官方运行点上下文的八分之一。LongMemEval 上也达到 79.0%。消融实验表明性能提升主要来源于因果校准而非信号密度，且策略收敛到一种多版本记忆组织结构，未有任何开放式基线能够复现其增益。

Conclusion: Hindsight Memory-PRM 有效利用记忆操作的审计痕迹，实现了无需人工标注的记忆奖励学习，显著提升长时记忆 LLM 代理的性能，并展示了因果校准在记忆管理中的关键作用，为未来大模型记忆机制的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Memory operations of long-horizon LLM agents are hard to supervise: an operation's value is unobservable when it is taken. But they are special -- they leave machine-readable evidence in the trajectory: retrieval hits and answer-time citations. Hindsight Memory-PRM exploits this audit trail twice: offline to train an operation-conditioned memory-utility critic, and online, where retrievals, citations, and one controlled deletion-and-reanswer per probe settle an intervention-calibrated entry-level presence credit, propagated along version chains as an action-level proxy reward -- no per-operation human labels, no Monte-Carlo replay of continuations. On held-out LoCoMo a local 8B policy reaches 77.5% under a fixed shared reader, surpassing its API teacher (65.1%) and all reproduced external systems, at one eighth the context of Mem0's official operating point; on LongMemEval, 79.0%. Ablations attribute the gain to causal calibration rather than signal density, and the policy converges to a multi-version memory organization whose gains no tested open-loop baseline reproduces.

</details>


### [79] [Agent Zero Memory: Provenance-Aware Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29606)
*Ming Wu,Pengyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Agent Zero Memory，一种具备可追溯性的三路并行长期记忆系统，通过时间线、知识图谱和层级文档记忆实现高保真检索，在 LongMemEval 与 LoCoMo 基准上分别达到 95.60% 与 93.60% 的新纪录，并显著降低查询成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代理需要持久、可信的记忆来记录用户所有对话和文件，但现有记忆系统通常只采用单一组织结构（如事实库、向量索引或知识图），导致盲点和信息失真。

Method: 构建三条平行记忆通道：① 事件时间线（Episodic Memory Events）记录何时何事改变；② 实体‑事件关联知识图谱链接人物、项目等跨会话信息；③ 层级文档记忆（HDM）存储经引用锁定的持久事实。检索时先通过意图门过滤，再由源路由器并行启动三种基于混合（嵌入+词法）搜索的工具循环，返回带证据引用的答案并统一置信度。所有学习项携带来源、时间戳和证据指针，答案只能引用实际打开的证据，实现结构性防伪。

Result: 在公开基准 LongMemEval 上取得 95.60%（比最佳基线提升 0.73 分），在 LoCoMo 上取得 93.60%（提升 1.10 分）。在八种主流 LLM 骨干模型上进行的控制实验显示，准确率波动仅 3.4 分，而查询成本相差约 30 倍，最高可在保持近乎最先进质量的前提下降低 20 倍成本。

Conclusion: 通过多模态、可追溯的并行记忆结构，Agent Zero Memory 能在保持高准确性的同时显著降低查询成本，证明记忆驱动的检索比单纯依赖模型更具效率和可靠性，为 LLM 代理的长期记忆提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents need durable, faithful memory of everything a user or organization has said and stored, yet most memory systems commit to a single organizing structure (a fact store, a vector index, or a knowledge graph) and inherit its blind spots. We present Agent Zero Memory, a provenance-aware long-term memory system that distils a user's conversations, files, and connected sources into three parallel memory systems, each capturing a different facet of the same history: an episodic Memory Events timeline that makes when and what changed first-class, an associative entity-event knowledge graph that links people and projects across sessions, and a semantic, curated, citation-locked Hierarchical Documentary Memory (HDM) of durable facts. A retrieval turn runs an intent gate (so self-contained turns add no latency), a source router, and three concurrent agentic searches, one per system, each a tool-using loop over hybrid (embedding + lexical) search under agent-controlled filters; their grounded, cited answers are integrated into one answer with a single confidence. We formalize the reading discipline: every learned item is a provenanced item carrying its origin, timestamp, and evidence pointer, and every answer is read under a citation lock, so it may cite only evidence its reader actually opened; fabrication is structurally excluded and the system abstains rather than guesses. On two public benchmarks the system sets a new state of the art: 95.60% on LongMemEval and 93.60% on LoCoMo, improving over the strongest prior systems by +0.73 and +1.10 points. A controlled study across eight backbone LLMs characterizes the accuracy-cost-latency frontier: accuracy varies by only 3.4 points while per-query cost varies by ~30x, with near-state-of-the-art quality at up to 20x lower cost per query, the signature of memory-driven, rather than model-driven, quality.

</details>


### [80] [Beyond Surface Alignment: Grounding the Dynamics of Situational Understanding and Generative Control in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29610)
*Chenghao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出当前大语言模型的对齐调优只关注表层行为，导致缺乏情境和生成的扎根能力；通过SitTest、ReCode、Branching Factor等评估，提出动态控制、AI Realtor 等方法，实现基于情境与生成的深度对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法虽提升流畅性、安全性和语调一致性，但掩盖了模型在情境理解和自我生成理解上的脆弱性，限制了在高风险或需要持续情境追踪的任务中的可靠性。

Method: ① 设计情境对齐评估套件：SitTest 检测模型在变化环境中的情境保持能力，ReCode 检验对深层句法依赖的利用。② 引入生成对齐度量：Branching Factor（BF）量化生成空间的多样性，Hindsight 评估模型对自身输出的理解。③ 提出动态控制框架：通过 AI Realtor 的上下文工程、Base‑Aligned Model Collaboration 的探索‑约束解耦、Annealed Sampling 的可验证强化学习，实现情境与生成的协同对齐，并在成瘾支持等高风险场景进行案例验证。

Result: 实验表明：在 SitTest 与 ReCode 上，现有 SOTA 模型表现显著下降；引入 BF 后发现标准对齐会导致生成空间提前收敛。采用动态控制后，模型在情境保持、生成多样性和自我解释能力上均有可观提升，尤其在 Addiction Support 场景中实现了更可靠的理性化对话。

Conclusion: 表层对齐不足以支撑可靠的智能体，必须结合情境对齐与生成对齐。本文的 Grounded Alignment 框架及其动态控制技术为构建既能理解环境又能自我监控生成的 LLM 提供了可行路径，推动了从表层调优向深层扎根对齐的转变。

Abstract: The current alignment tuning paradigm for Large Language Models (LLMs) prioritizes surface-level behaviors -- fluency, safety, and tonal consistency. While effective for casual chat, this thesis argues that such surface alignment masks a lack of grounding, creating models that are stylistically confident but situationally brittle. We propose a framework of Grounded Alignment, analyzing how models process context (Input) and structure generation (Output), then aligning these grounded behaviors to human needs.
  First, we evaluate failures in Situational Grounding. SitTest shows that despite large context windows, state-of-the-art models struggle to maintain a consistent "mental model" of a changing environment. ReCode further shows that models rely on surface heuristics rather than deep syntactic dependencies: they "read" extensive histories without truly "understanding" the evolving situation.
  Second, we evaluate Generative Grounding. We introduce the Branching Factor (BF) to map LLM generation, finding that standard alignment tuning constricts this landscape into premature stylistic collapse. Hindsight further shows that models often fail to understand their own generations.
  Finally, we propose Dynamic Control for grounded interaction. AI Realtor demonstrates context engineering to compensate for poor situational grounding. Base-Aligned Model Collaboration decouples exploration from stylistic constraints. We also present Annealed Sampling for verifiable reinforcement learning and apply these ideas to Addiction Support, where model-generated rationalization offers a communication interface for high-stakes domains. Collectively, this work moves beyond surface alignment toward agents anchored in both context and generation.

</details>


### [81] [Cross-lingual Functional Vectors for Emotion Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29613)
*Jieying Xue,Phuong Minh Nguyen,Minh Le Nguyen,Shogo Okada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了函数向量（FVs）在跨语言情感识别任务中的迁移能力，发现从源语言提取的FVs能够在目标语言的零样本设置下显著提升性能，表明FVs捕获了语言无关的任务信号，且在不同语言和模型中表现出稳定的注意力头范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管函数向量已被证明能在结构化的上下文学习中恢复任务行为，但其在语义复杂任务（如多标签情感识别）以及跨语言泛化能力尚未得到系统评估，亟需验证其是否能够提供轻量级、语言无关的任务适配方式。

Method: 使用多语言大型语言模型，在多标签情感识别基准上从源语言的少量示例中提取函数向量；在目标语言的零样本（干净和扰动）条件下，将这些FVs注入模型以引导任务行为；同时分析不同注意力头数量对FV效果的影响，并与标准少样本上下文学习进行对比。

Result: 在多种跨语言设置下，加入FVs后模型性能显著提升，接近或部分匹配少样本上下文学习的效果；不同模型在构建有效FVs时表现出相对稳定的最优注意力头范围，该范围在语言之间保持一致；FVs在不增加示例处理开销的情况下实现了有效的任务迁移。

Conclusion: 函数向量能够捕获语言无关的任务相关信息，具备跨语言迁移能力，是一种轻量且高效的多语言任务适配手段，可在实际大规模应用中替代传统的少样本上下文学习。

Abstract: Function vectors (FVs) have recently emerged as a promising mechanism for steering the behavior of large language models (LLMs) by injecting task-specific latent direction representations derived from in-context demonstrations. While prior studies have shown that FVs can recover task behavior in structured in-context learning settings, their effectiveness on semantically complex tasks and their ability to generalize across languages remain underexplored. We investigate the cross-lingual transferability of FVs using multilingual multi-label emotion recognition as a challenging semantic classification benchmark. Specifically, we examine whether FVs extracted from a source language can steer task behavior in another language under both standard clean and perturbed zero-shot settings without providing demonstrations during inference. Across diverse cross-lingual settings, applying FVs substantially improves performance, suggesting that FVs capture language-agnostic, task-relevant signals rather than purely language-specific lexical patterns, and highlighting their potential as a lightweight and transferable mechanism for multilingual task adaptation. We observe that each LLM exhibits a relatively stable optimal range of attention heads for constructing effective FVs, and the pattern remains consistent across languages. In addition, FVs can partially replicate the task-steering effects of standard few-shot in-context learning while avoiding the computational overhead of processing multiple demonstrations, making them effective for large-scale practical applications. Our code is available at https://github.com/yingjie7/cross_lingual_fvs.

</details>


### [82] [JPO: Juris Policy Optimization for Structured Legal Reasoning in Criminal Judgment Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29616)
*Zhaolu Kang,Yantao Liu,Tailong Luo,Leqi Zheng,Lei Wei,Chenghua Zhu,Junhao Gong,Jiachen Qian,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Jiaxin Liu,Yuan Wang,Hao Zhang,Zixia Wang,Rong Fu,Zheng Lin,Richeng Xuan,Zhichao Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出JPO框架，通过教师理性监督和复合奖励的强化学习，提升中文刑事判决预测的准确性和推理结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型仅优化最终标签，缺乏对法律推理过程的结构化约束，评估也多依赖间接或模型内部的指标，难以保证判决与法条、指控、量刑的一致性。

Method: JPO包括两阶段：①使用教师生成的推理步骤（法条匹配、指控依据、量刑一致）对模型进行标准化四步推理的监督；②基于法律预测质量、推理结构完整性和跨步一致性的复合奖励进行强化学习，并引入令牌级优势重加权和自适应裁剪，聚焦法律关键片段。

Result: 在多个开源中文语言模型和三大中文法律基准上，JPO相较于仅监督微调和传统强化学习基线，显著提升了判决预测准确率和推理质量，一致性指标均有提升。

Conclusion: JPO有效实现了结构化法律推理的后训练优化，证明了在刑事判决预测任务中，结合教师理性监督和复合奖励的强化学习能够同时提升预测性能和推理可解释性。

Abstract: Criminal judgment prediction requires models to infer statutory articles, charges, and sentencing outcomes from case facts. Unlike standard classification tasks, it involves a structured reasoning process in which statutes should be matched with facts, charges should be justified by statutes, and sentencing outcomes should remain consistent with charges. Existing approaches optimize final labels, and while some have attempted to evaluate reasoning quality, their evaluations are indirect, often relying on LLM-generated rubrics that reflect model-internal preferences rather than the inherent logical structure of legal adjudication. We propose Juris Policy Optimization (JPO), a post-training framework for structured legal reasoning in Chinese criminal judgment prediction. JPO first uses teacher-generated rationales to supervise a standardized four-step reasoning process, and then applies reinforcement learning with a composite reward over legal prediction quality, reasoning structure completeness, and cross-step consistency. JPO further introduces token-level advantage reweighting and adaptive clipping for legally salient reasoning segments. Experiments on multiple open-source language models and three Chinese legal benchmarks show that JPO consistently improves both judgment prediction and reasoning quality over supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [83] [Memory-First Fact-Checking: A Knowledge-Graph-Grounded Multi-Agent System for Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29617)
*Amelia Petrenciuc,Alexandru Lecu,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合式事实核查框架，先用基于知识图谱的语义记忆进行检索和推理，若证据不足再调用可信网页并通过对抗多智能体审判决定真假，最终将验证信息以三元组形式加入知识图谱，实现可解释且可增量的误信息检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有误信息检测方法要么依赖静态知识库，缺乏可解释性和实时性；要么频繁访问网络，成本高且易受噪声干扰。作者希望构建一个能够优先利用内部语义记忆、在必要时补充外部证据，并具备解释能力的系统。

Method: 1) 记忆优先、网络后备架构；2) 使用 Sentence‑BERT 对输入主张进行语义检索，匹配双索引知识图谱并通过自然语言推理（NLI）评估；3) 引入图感知置信机制，综合语义相似度、NLI 置信度和图结构证据判断内部知识是否足够；4) 若不足，则从可信网页抓取信息，交给由支持、矛盾和裁决三类智能体组成的对抗审判团进行多轮推理；5) 验证后将结果转化为结构化三元组并增量更新知识图谱。

Result: 在 COVID‑19 误信息基准上，本文框架在已解决的主张上取得 97.4% 的准确率和 92.6% 的宏观 F1，显著优于 Llama 3.3 70B 基线（准确率 87.7%，宏观 F1 86.3%）。

Conclusion: 通过结合知识图谱语义记忆和对抗多智能体推理，本文实现了高精度、可解释且具增量学习能力的误信息检测，证明了记忆优先策略能够显著降低不必要的网络检索并提升整体性能。

Abstract: This paper introduces a hybrid fact-checking framework that integrates Knowledge Graph-based semantic memory with adversarial multi-agent reasoning for explainable misinformation detection. The proposed system follows a memory-first, web-fallback architecture, in which input claims are initially evaluated against a dual-index Knowledge Graph through Sentence-BERT-based semantic retrieval and Natural Language Inference. When the evidence retrieved from the graph is insufficient to support a reliable decision, the framework collects information from trusted web sources and assesses it using an adversarial tribunal composed of support, contradiction, and judging agents. A graph-aware confidence mechanism combines semantic similarity, NLI confidence, and structural graph evidence to determine whether internal knowledge is sufficient, thereby reducing unnecessary web retrieval. Following verification, validated information is transformed into structured triples and incorporated into the Knowledge Graph, supporting the incremental expansion of the system's semantic memory. Experimental evaluation on a curated COVID-19 misinformation benchmark demonstrates that the proposed framework achieves an accuracy of 97.4\% and a macro-averaged F1-score of 92.6% on resolved claims, outperforming a Llama~3.3~70B baseline, which obtains an accuracy of 87.7% and a macro-averaged F1-score of 86.3%.

</details>


### [84] [MI-Distillation: Selecting from Model-Interpolated Instruct-Reasoning Data Spectrum for Chain-of-Thought Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29623)
*Yangsong Lan,Renkai Hu,HongKai Zheng,Bo Zhang,Renzhi Wang,Hongliang Dai,Piji Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MI-Distillation框架，通过模型插值生成连续的指令-推理数据谱，并用SeqLSS挑选信息密集且易学习的推理路径，实现小模型在长链推理蒸馏上的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思考（Long CoT）虽能提升大模型推理能力，但直接蒸馏到小模型效果有限，且梯度幅度大、更新方向集中，导致学习困难；需要在信息密度与学生模型分布对齐之间取得平衡。

Method: 从梯度视角分析Long CoT与Short CoT的差异，提出模型插值蒸馏（MI-Distillation），在教师模型之间插值生成不同信息密度的推理轨迹；再利用序列可学习惊讶度得分（SeqLSS）评估并选择既信息丰富又易于学生学习的路径进行蒸馏。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，MI-Distillation相较于强基线Long CoT蒸馏，持续提升小模型的CoT表现，验证了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 长链推理蒸馏的关键在于平衡信息密度与学生模型的分布匹配，MI-Distillation通过插值生成多样化轨迹并用SeqLSS筛选，成功实现了小模型的高效长链推理学习。

Abstract: Recent advances in large reasoning models (LRMs) have shown strong performance on complex problems through long chain-of-thought (Long CoT) reasoning. However, distilling such trajectories into smaller student models remains challenging: direct Long CoT supervision often provides limited gains and can be less effective than concise Short CoT rationales. In this work, we investigate this phenomenon from a gradient-centric perspective. Our analysis shows that Long CoT induces larger gradient magnitudes and more concentrated update directions than Short CoT, with this effect becoming more pronounced as student model capacity increases. These findings suggest that effective Long CoT distillation requires balancing the reasoning information density of reasoning trajectories with their distributional alignment to the student model. Motivated by this insight, we propose \textbf{M}odel \textbf{I}nterporlation \textbf{Distillation} (\textbf{MI-Distillation}), a framework that constructs a continuous Instruct-Reasoning data spectrum through model interpolation. To select suitable trajectories from this spectrum, we further introduce \textbf{Seq}uential \textbf{L}earnable \textbf{S}urprisal \textbf{S}core (\textbf{SeqLSS}), which favors reasoning paths that are both informative and learnable for the student. Extensive experiments on reasoning benchmarks show that MI-Distillation consistently improves small model CoT distillation over strong Long CoT baselines.

</details>


### [85] [PrivBench: A Holistic and Modular Benchmarking Platform for Evaluating Text-to-Text Privatization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29624)
*Stephen Meisenbacher,Andreea-Elena Bodea,Ahmet Bilal Akın,Alexandra Klymenko,Jana Diesner,Florian Matthes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PrivBench，一个面向文本隐私化的统一、模块化、可扩展的基准平台，提供多维评估、实时对比和公开排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到文本隐私化方法的评估手段繁杂且缺乏统一标准，难以客观比较不同方法的隐私保护能力。

Method: 构建PrivBench平台，将评估目标划分为若干 desiderata（如隐私泄露度、文本可读性、信息保真度等），并实现模块化评估组件，支持扩展和实时在线评测，配合公开排行榜促进竞争。

Result: 平台已实现并对外开放，提供完整的评估流水线和实时排行榜，用户可免费使用并提交结果，促进社区对文本隐私化方法的统一比较。

Conclusion: PrivBench 为文本隐私化提供了统一、可扩展且面向用户的评估基准，推动该领域的标准化和竞争，加速隐私保护技术的研发与应用。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing methods have enabled novel solutions and advances in the field of privacy, particularly in the sub-domain of text-to-text privatization, where the goal is to transform a sensitive input text into a privatized output by ideally masking (in)directly identifiable or otherwise private information. The evaluation of text-to-text privatization, however, is not straightforward, and the extant literature has utilized a myriad of techniques and metrics to quantify the privacy-preserving capabilities of privatization methods. Seeking to unify the evaluation of text-to-text privatization, we introduce PrivBench, a holistic and modular benchmarking platform for researchers and practitioners working on text privatization. PrivBench is holistic in that it evaluates privatization on a series of defined desiderata, which are structured into modules. PrivBench is not only modular but also extensible, allowing for future updates and benchmark versions. PrivBench is user-centered and promotes competition via real-time evaluation and a live public leaderboard. The platform is free to use and openly accessible at https://privbench.com/.

</details>


### [86] [ACTD: Anchor-Based Cross-Tokenizer Distillation with Residual Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29662)
*Huiyi Zhang,Zijian Li,Xiaocheng Feng,Weitao Ma,Xiaoliang Yang,Yichong Huang,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于锚点和残差正则的跨分词器蒸馏方法（ACTD），通过词表和序列对齐并抑制对齐噪声，实现跨模型族的知识迁移，在多项推理基准上取得SOTA，并在多教师扩展中进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨分词器蒸馏面临词表和序列不对齐导致的噪声问题，现有近似对齐方法会引入额外误差，限制了大模型向轻量模型的有效知识转移。

Method: 提出ACTD框架：①通过锚点机制实现词表和序列的结构对齐；②引入锚点损失结合残差正则化，降低对齐噪声；③扩展至多教师场景，融合多模型知识。

Result: 在五个推理基准和三种不同教师模型上实验，ACTD显著超越现有方法，取得最新性能；多教师版本进一步超越最强单教师和多教师基线，验证了方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ACTD有效解决跨分词器蒸馏的对齐难题，并通过残差正则抑制噪声，实现了跨模型族的高效知识迁移，具备良好的扩展性和实用价值。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation effectively transfers reasoning capabilities from large language models to lightweight student models. To enable knowledge transfer across disparate model families, researchers increasingly explore cross-tokenizer distillation. However, cross-tokenizer distillation remains challenging due to vocabulary and sequence misalignment, while approximate vocabulary alignment can introduce additional noise into distillation. To address these challenges, we propose Anchor-Based Cross-Tokenizer Distillation with Residual Regularization (ACTD). ACTD bridges structural heterogeneity through vocabulary and sequence alignment, while mitigating alignment noise via a novel anchor loss with residual regularization. We further extend this framework to a multi-teacher setting. Evaluated across five reasoning benchmarks with three distinct teacher models, ACTD achieves state-of-the-art performance. Moreover, its multi-teacher extension outperforms the strongest single-teacher and multi-teacher baselines, further demonstrating the robustness of our method.

</details>


### [87] [A Hub of Short Rows Inflates Intrinsic Dimension Estimation of Token Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29702)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现token嵌入表中存在一个靠近原点的中心簇（hub），该簇导致最近邻内在维度估计器（如TwoNN）显著高估维度；去除或归一化该hub后，所有模型的内在维度均降至约10-17，说明之前的维度随模型规模增长的现象是由hub引起的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究报告称大型语言模型的token嵌入表的内在维度随参数规模显著增长，作者怀疑这一现象可能受嵌入表中中心簇（hub）的影响，进而影响最近邻维度估计的准确性。

Method: 1) 分析token嵌入矩阵的行向量分布，识别靠近原点的短向量簇；2) 使用TwoNN等最近邻内在维度估计器对单个token和全词表进行测量；3) 通过删除或对这些hub行进行归一化，重新评估维度；4) 在多个模型（GPT-2、K3、GLM-4.7、Pythia等）上复现并比较结果；5) 检查hub行的训练更新情况和长度特征。

Result: • 中心簇的存在使得大多数token的最近邻距离几乎相同，导致TwoNN等估计器给出远高于真实的维度，呈重尾分布；• 去除几百个hub行后，所有模型的内在维度收敛到10-17的窄区间；• 对Pythia模型复现的维度随参数增长的报告（27→122）在去除hub后消失，维度保持在10-17；• hub行并非未被更新的异常向量，而是长度较短的向量；• 对hub行进行归一化同样能将维度降低到相同水平。

Conclusion: token嵌入表中的中心簇是导致最近邻内在维度估计器高估维度的主要因素，去除或归一化该簇可获得更可靠的内在维度估计。此前关于嵌入表维度随模型规模增长的结论需重新审视，实际有效维度在不同模型间保持相对稳定。

Abstract: A token-embedding table holds a hub of short rows near its origin, and we show that this cluster biases what nearest-neighbor intrinsic-dimension (ID) estimators report. Because of the concentration of measure, a token is closer to the central cluster than to any other token, so its first two neighbors are both hub rows at nearly the same distance. As a result, the ID estimators such as TwoNN return a dimension far above the real ID. Measured one token at a time, dimension is a heavy-tailed distribution. Measured on the full vocabulary, it grows with the model's parameter count. However, when we remove the hub, the heavy tail disappears and the measured dimension collapses to a narrow range for eleven models, from GPT-2 to models such as K3 and GLM-4.7. The hub acts as a switch: a few hundred rows are enough to fully inflate the estimate. We reproduced an experiment stating that the intrinsic dimension (ID) of Pythia's token-embedding table grows with the parameter count, from $27$ to $122$ between 160M and 12B parameters. We show that this result disappears when the hub is removed: the table then reads $10$ to $17$ at every size. The hub contains a subset of the population that under-trained-token detectors flag, but on Pythia the hub that we detected and removed as a whole was updated during training: what seem to characterize these rows is simply their length, not an absence of updates. Finally, we show that normalizing the rows instead of removing them gives the same lower reading.

</details>


### [88] [The Depth Flow of Token Representations Is Nonlinear and Does Not Descend Its Own Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29706)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了语言模型中词向量随层传播的动力学，发现其运动可用离散 Langevin 模型描述，且非线性二次漂移优于线性映射，流动具有显著旋转分量，且不沿自身对数密度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大规模语言模型内部表征随层的演化机制，以揭示其潜在的动力学规律和信息保持特性，为解释模型行为提供物理学视角。

Method: 在 Pythia-160M 与 Pythia-410M 模型上，基于语料库平均轨迹拟合离散 Langevin 方程；比较二次漂移与线性映射的预测误差；使用 Kramers–Moyal 估计器检验局部性；进一步分析漂移势、旋转分量及各层的秩保持情况。

Result: 二次漂移在所有层的预测上均优于线性映射；Kramers–Moyal 估计在局部邻域内吻合；漂移并未沿自身对数密度下降，而是沿另一个势函数；旋转分量占可解释漂移的 4%~45%；词向量的角度秩在全部 13 层保持不变，范数秩被打乱，浓度秩在最后一层被逆转。

Conclusion: 语言模型的表征流动呈现非线性、带有显著旋转的动力学特征，信息在角度维度上得以保留，而在幅度和浓度维度上经历重排，这为理解模型内部信息传递提供了新的物理解释框架。

Abstract: A token's representation is carried through the network layer by layer. The whole vocabulary carried together forms a flow. We fit this flow's equation of motion as a discrete Langevin model over corpus-mean trajectories of Pythia-160M and Pythia-410M, and score the predicted steps on held-out tokens. Linear maps are often used as cheap surrogates for a layer. The flow they summarize is not linear: a quadratic drift beats the linear linear map at every transition of both models, and the Kramers--Moyal estimator agrees wherever its neighborhoods stay local.
  We then characterize the flow further. First, we show that it does not descend its own log-density. The drift instead descends a potential that is not the density. Second, the rotational component is not negligible, $4$ to $45\%$ of the explainable drift, and the circulation shows in what the flow preserves: a token keeps its angular rank across all thirteen layers while its norm rank is shuffled and its concentration rank is reversed by the last block.

</details>


### [89] [DVBench: Benchmarking MLLMs for Understanding Dynamic Charts and Narratives in Data Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29711)
*Bomiao Wang,Zekai Shao,Jiexiang Lan,Xiaoliang Fu,Xingchen Zeng,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 DVBench 基准，评估多模态大语言模型在数据视频（动态图表+叙事）上的理解能力，涵盖5个维度，收录300段真实数据视频和1000条人工验证问答，评测9个模型，发现 Gemini-3.1-Pro 效果最佳，Kimi‑k2.5 为开源模型之最，并指出模型规模与性能、叙事能力与视觉能力之间的脱钩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型的评测多聚焦于单一的图表或视频理解，缺乏对随时间演变的结构化视觉信息（即数据视频）整体的评估，导致难以把握模型在动态图表叙事场景中的真实能力。

Method: 构建 DVBench 基准：① 将数据视频定义为动态图表配合结构化叙事的媒体；② 将理解任务拆解为5个维度（如视觉、叙事、跨帧关联等）；③ 通过半自动化管线收集300段真实数据视频并生成1000条经人工验证的问答对；④ 对9个主流 MLLM 进行系统评测，并进行细粒度分析、消融实验，考察帧配置和字幕输入等因素的影响。

Result: 实验表明 Gemini-3.1-Pro 在整体得分上领先，Kimi‑k2.5 为表现最好的开源模型。发现开源模型的性能并非严格随参数规模提升，且叙事能力强的模型不一定具备相应的视觉理解能力。细粒度分析揭示各维度存在不同的薄弱环节，帧数和字幕的使用对模型表现有显著影响。

Conclusion: DVBench 为评估 MLLM 在动态结构化视觉信息上的能力提供了系统化基准，揭示了当前模型在数据视频理解上的局限，为后续模型设计和数据集构建指明了方向。

Abstract: While MLLMs have made significant strides in chart comprehension and video understanding, current evaluations largely isolate these capabilities, leaving a critical gap in understanding temporally evolving structured visual information. To address this gap, we introduce DVBench, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on data videos, a storytelling medium that integrates dynamic charts with structured narratives. We decompose data video understanding into five dimensions. DVBench comprises 300 real-world data videos and 1,000 human-verified QA pairs curated through a rigorous semi-automated pipeline. Extensive evaluations of nine MLLMs show that Gemini-3.1-Pro achieves the best overall performance, while Kimi-k2.5 is the strongest open-source model. We further identify two notable phenomena: open-source model performance does not scale strictly with parameter size, and narrative proficiency does not guarantee visual capability. Fine-grained analyses and ablation studies further reveal dimension-specific weaknesses and the effects of frame configurations and subtitle inputs, informing future MLLM development. DVBench is publicly available at https://bomiaowang.github.io/DVBench/.

</details>


### [90] [Evaluating the Capabilities of LLMs for Persuasive Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29738)
*Jordan Robinson,Angus R. Williams,Katie Atkinson,Anthony G. Cohn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM在辩论中看似有说服力，但在形式论证评判下表现不佳，主观与客观说服力出现显著分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型生成的文本是否真正具备论证质量，还是仅凭修辞流畅性获得说服感。

Method: 构建基于形式论证理论的多代理对话平台Persuasio，对人类与LLM在英国政治议题上的192场自由文本辩论进行自动论证评判，并通过9702次众包成对判断（共1386个标注实例）进行主观评估。

Result: LLM在主观排名中占优，但在论证理论裁决中显著落后于人类；多代理和检索增强的LLM变体进一步扩大了主观与客观说服力的差距。

Conclusion: LLM的修辞流畅性与正式论证强度之间存在系统性鸿沟，提示仅凭语言流畅性并不能保证论证质量，需在模型设计中引入形式论证约束。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate apparently highly persuasive text, but does sounding persuasive mean arguing well? We introduce \textsc{Persuasio}, a multi-agent dialogue platform grounded in a formal argumentation-based theory of persuasion dialogues that adjudicates logical winners during free-text debates. Using this system, we generated 192 debates on a UK political topic between humans and LLMs, and evaluated 22 interlocutors through both automated adjudication and 9,702 crowdsourced pairwise judgements across 1{,}386 annotation instances. We observed a consistent decoupling between subjective and formal persuasiveness: LLMs dominated the subjective ranking yet performed substantially worse under argumentation-theoretic adjudication, where humans remained competitive. Multi-agent and retrieval-augmented variants further widened this divergence. These findings reveal a systematic gap between rhetorical fluency and formal argumentative strength in LLM-based persuasive dialogues.

</details>


### [91] [ReTrace: Rejected-Trajectory Conditioning for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29748)
*Luxi Lin,Zhanpeng Zeng,Shuang Peng,Songwei Liu,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReTrace 通过在每轮草稿生成时保留并条件化先前被拒绝的后缀，使投机解码在不增加额外前向计算的情况下提升接受长度和解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在投机解码（如 DFlash）中，一旦出现首次拒绝，后续草稿后缀会被全部丢弃，导致已生成的计算未被利用；观察到这些被拒绝的后缀仍可能与目标序列对齐，说明它们蕴含有价值的语义信息。

Method: 提出 ReTrace：在每轮草稿块生成时，将上一轮被拒绝的后缀的隐藏表示作为条件输入，而不是仅使用全掩码占位符。通过门控残差融合将这些保留的表示与草稿块的输入嵌入结合，并利用同一次验证过程中的目标感知校正信号对其进行细化，从而实现跨轮条件化。

Result: 在 Qwen3 系列模型上，针对数学推理、代码生成和开放式对话等任务，ReTrace 相比 DFlash 基线显著提升了平均接受长度和端到端解码速度，保持了投机解码的无损特性且无需额外的模型前向传播。

Conclusion: ReTrace 通过跨轮条件化利用被拒绝的后缀信息，在不改变验证机制的前提下提升投机解码效率，且与现有的草稿改进方法兼容，可进一步组合以获得更大收益。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive language model inference by having a lightweight draft model propose multiple candidate tokens, which are then verified in parallel by a larger target model. However, after the first rejection, standard prefix-based verification discards the remaining draft suffix, so the computation spent generating and verifying those positions does not contribute to decoding progress. Focusing on DFlash, we show that rejected positions in a rejected suffix may still align with the target continuation, indicating that the draft model can retain useful semantic and structural information despite local token-level errors. Motivated by this observation and inspired by conditional diffusion, we introduce~\textbf{ReTrace}, a rejected-trajectory conditioning method that conditions each draft block on the rejected suffix from the previous round rather than generating it from fresh mask placeholders alone. ReTrace retains the hidden representations of the rejected suffixes, aligns them with the next draft block, refines them using target-aware correction signals from the same verification pass, and admits them into the drafter's input embeddings through gated residual fusion. Because rejected tokens are never committed and target-side verification remains unchanged, ReTrace preserves the lossless property of speculative decoding without requiring an additional model forward pass. Experiments with Qwen3 models across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and open-ended dialogue demonstrate that ReTrace consistently improves average acceptance length and end-to-end decoding speed over its DFlash backbone. By introducing cross-round conditioning without modifying within-round proposal generation, ReTrace is largely orthogonal to existing drafting improvements and might be combined with them for further gains.

</details>


### [92] [HiVe: Beyond Static Prompts for Multitask Learning via Hierarchy-based Vertical Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29790)
*HyeonJik Bae,Minyeol Kim,Susik Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HiVe通过层次化提示和垂直MoE在推理时实现输入依赖的提示专化，显著提升了大模型的参数高效微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示微调要么使用平坦结构，要么使用固定层次结构，缺乏对不同输入的自适应专化能力，限制了性能提升。

Method: HiVe在训练阶段利用任务间关系构建多层次提示层级；推理阶段引入垂直混合专家（V‑MoE）机制，根据每个输入的需求动态组合不同层级的提示，实现输入依赖的专化。

Result: 在多种任务上，HiVe相较于强基线提示微调方法持续取得更高的性能指标，验证了其有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 层次化提示结合V‑MoE的设计为参数高效微调提供了灵活且强大的解决方案，展示了在大语言模型上实现自适应提示专化的可行性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) continue to scale, parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has become a practical alternative to full-parameter adaptation. Prompt tuning is effective, but existing approaches either use flat prompt structures or hierarchical structures with fixed prompt composition, limiting adaptive prompt specialization. To address this limitation, we propose HiVe, a prompt tuning framework that models prompts at multiple levels and enables input-dependent specialization. HiVe constructs a prompt hierarchy by leveraging inter-task relationships during training, and employs a vertical mixture-of-experts (V-MoE) mechanism at inference time to compose prompts up to the level of specialization required for each input. Experiments show that HiVe consistently outperforms strong prompt tuning baselines across diverse tasks.

</details>


### [93] [R$^2$A: Learning Persona Policies Through Persona Representation Learning and Runtime Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29798)
*Mohan Zhang,Chengsong You,Xiaoyu Cao,Zhen Sun,Xiaohan Jia,Junwei Zhou,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Persona Selection–Realization框架，将Persona行为生成拆分为选择与实现两步，并设计两阶段学习方法R²A，通过Persona表示学习和运行时对齐显著提升跨任务Persona一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态Persona提示在不同任务中表现不一致，因同一Persona在某些情境下有益而在另一些情境下有害，需要一种能够动态适配任务的Persona策略。

Method: 构建Persona Selection–Realization框架，将潜在Persona状态分解为Selection（选择）和Realization（实现），定义Selection Gap和Realization Gap。提出R²A两阶段方法：①Persona Representation Learning，使用结构化的Who–How–What描述编码Persona目标、行为原则和轨迹表现；②Persona Runtime Alignment，去除显式Persona指定，利用任务反馈联合校准行为选择和轨迹实现。

Result: 在覆盖四大Accountable-Professional Persona原则的12个评估设置中，R²A整体优于基线模型和静态Persona提示。消融实验表明，Persona Representation Learning对防止Runtime Alignment产生行为失衡并实现更稳定的Persona策略学习至关重要。

Conclusion: 通过将Persona生成过程系统化为选择与实现两环，并结合结构化表示与运行时对齐，R²A实现了跨任务的一致且可控的Persona行为，证明了动态Persona策略学习的有效性。

Abstract: The same Persona behavior can be beneficial in one context but harmful in another, causing static Persona elicitation to perform inconsistently across tasks. We introduce the Persona Selection--Realization Framework, which models behavior generation through a latent Persona state and decomposes it into Persona Selection and Persona Realization. The discrepancies between static Persona elicitation and an ideal Persona policy in these two components define the Selection Gap and Realization Gap, respectively. Building on this framework, we propose R$^2$A, a two-stage approach for learning Persona policies. Persona Representation Learning uses structured Who--How--What presentations to encode the target Persona's objective, conditional behavioral principles, and trajectory-level manifestations. Persona Runtime Alignment then removes the explicit Persona specification and jointly calibrates behavior selection and trajectory realization using task feedback. Across 12 evaluation settings covering the four principles of the Accountable-Professional Persona studied in this work, R$^2$A overall outperforms both the base model and static Persona elicitation. Ablation results further show that Persona Representation Learning is critical for preventing Runtime Alignment from producing behaviorally imbalanced policies and for achieving more stable Persona policy learning.

</details>


### [94] [A^2Agent: Action-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Repository-Level Code Localization Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29831)
*Doyeon Kim,Suyoung Bae,Yumin Lee,Jee-Hyong Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种动作感知的强化学习方法，通过每回合奖励序列和动作级优势估计，提升代码定位任务的发现与提交能力，显著超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码定位方法仅依赖稀疏的轨迹级信号，无法区分每一步动作的有效性，导致在探索阶段虽能找到正确代码区域，却难以将其提交，限制了自动化软件工程的性能。

Method: 设计了一个动作感知的强化学习框架：① 引入每回合奖励序列，既奖励发现金代码区域，也奖励对其的提交；② 采用动作级优势估计，将共享相同探索上下文的回合分组，从而为每个动作单独分配信用。该框架在SWE‑Bench数据集上进行训练与评估。

Result: 在SWE‑Bench Verified上提升平均F1 1.58%，在SWE‑Bench Pro上提升8.55%；4B模型在性能上可匹配甚至超越参数规模高达8倍的基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过引入动作层面的奖励与信用分配，能够更有效地定位并提交相关代码区域，显著提升自动化代码定位的准确性和效率，为后续强化学习在软件工程中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Localizing issue-relevant code regions is a critical step in automated software engineering. However, due to their reliance on sparse trajectory-level signals, existing methods cannot identify which per-turn actions are effective and often discover correct code regions during exploration but fail to commit them. To address these limitations, we propose an action-aware reinforcement learning method that combines a per-turn reward sequence rewarding both the discovery and commitment of gold code regions with an action-level advantage estimation scheme that isolates each action's credit by grouping turns sharing the same exploration context. Extensive evaluations show that our method improves the average F1 over the state-of-the-art (SOTA) by 1.58% on SWE-Bench Verified and 8.55% on SWE-Bench Pro, with our 4B model outperforming baselines up to 8x larger. Our code is available at https://github.com/donian00/A2Agent.

</details>


### [95] [You Know What I Mean: A Benchmark for Agentic Conversational Reference Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29834)
*Karen Fuchs,Uri Katz,Yoav Goldberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出对话引用定位（CoRG）任务，利用多种工具在开发者聊天中将间接引用指向GitHub上的具体Issue、PR或提交，基准RepoRef包含400段对话，最佳模型仅达67%成功率，任务仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作对话中，许多引用并未直接命名，需要结合对话上下文和外部工作空间（如代码库、Issue等）才能定位目标。现有检索方法多为单步、单模态，难以处理这种跨工具、跨时间的推理需求。

Method: 将任务形式化为Conversational Reference Grounding（CoRG），要求智能体使用一组API/工具（搜索、查看元数据、读取内容等）逐步发现并验证候选目标。构建RepoRef基准，收集92个仓库的400段开发者聊天，对每个引用标注唯一的GitHub对象。评估多种基线模型，包括检索式、链式思考和工具使用策略。

Result: 在RepoRef上，最强基线模型的成功率为67.0%，仍有约三分之一的引用未能正确定位。相比单步检索任务，模型需要进行多轮工具调用才能完成定位，错误主要来源于候选生成不足和验证步骤不充分。

Conclusion: CoRG提供了一个真实、多工具环境下的基准，揭示了当前语言模型在跨模态、跨时间信息检索与验证方面的局限。未来工作应聚焦于更高效的候选生成、动态工具规划以及更强的上下文记忆，以提升在实际协作对话中的引用定位能力。

Abstract: Collaborative conversations frequently contain references whose targets are indirect rather than named: resolving "this looks like the fix discussed yesterday" requires combining conversational context with evidence from the surrounding workspace which is accessible through APIs or user interfaces. We formalize this problem as Conversational Reference Grounding (CoRG): using a given set of tools to resolve a reference in conversation to the unique external item intended by the speaker. CoRG is challenging because it combines lexical, semantic, and temporal cues distributed across the conversation and the external workspace. Agents must translate these heterogeneous signals into effective tool use: formulating strategies, discovering plausible candidates, inspecting their metadata and content, and ruling out close alternatives. We study CoRG through RepoRef, a benchmark of 400 developer-chat segments grounded in GitHub issues, pull requests, and commits across 92 repositories. Unlike single-shot retrieval tasks, RepoRef often requires multi-step tool use. Our results show that CoRG remains challenging for current agents, even the best agent reaches only 67.0% success rate, leaving one third of references unresolved. These findings position CoRG as a concrete benchmark for studying how agents search, inspect, and verify information in realistic multi-tool environments.

</details>


### [96] [EVAR: Evidence-Validated Hypothesis Admission for Budget-Aware Narrative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29835)
*Peilin Liu,Zhiquan Ji,Jinglong Ping*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 EVAR 框架，通过在不可变证据库中验证假设，控制推理预算，实现对长篇叙事的证据可靠推理，提升性能与证据忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在长篇非交互式叙事推理时容易产生未被证据支持的中间假设，导致后续推理被污染，尤其当证据分散在远距离文本时，这一问题尤为突出，需要一种机制确保假设得到证据验证并控制推理成本。

Method: EVAR 首先将叙事转化为不可变的证据库，存储源链接的原子陈述，并根据未解决的知识缺口和不确定性信号为每个实例分配推理预算。推理过程中，直接为缺口生成候选假设，构造基于假设的验证挑战，并在锁定的证据库中验证：得到支持的假设进入答案支持状态，无法验证的假设被隔离，矛盾的假设被丢弃。采用基于充分性的停止机制避免不必要的细化。

Result: 在 NarraCrime 数据集及多个公开推理基准上，EVAR 在保持可控推理成本的前提下，显著提升任务准确率和证据忠实度。

Conclusion: 通过证据验证的假设接纳与预算感知的推理控制，EVAR 有效缓解了长篇叙事推理中的假设漂移问题，展示了在实际推理任务中兼顾性能、可信度和成本的可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often produce fluent but weakly grounded conclusions when reasoning over non-interactive, long-form narratives. A central failure mode is that unsupported intermediate hypotheses can enter the reasoning trajectory and contaminate subsequent inference, especially when evidence is scattered across distant parts of the story. To address this problem, we propose EVAR, an evidence-validated hypothesis admission framework for budget-aware narrative reasoning. EVAR first compiles the narrative into an immutable evidence store of source-linked atomic claims and assigns an instance-specific inference budget from unresolved gaps and uncertainty signals. During refinement, EVAR directly proposes candidate hypotheses for unresolved gaps, constructs hypothesis-conditioned validation challenges, and verifies each candidate against the locked store before admission: supported hypotheses enter the answer-supporting state, unverifiable ones are quarantined, and contradictory ones are discarded. A sufficiency-based stopping mechanism further avoids unnecessary refinement. Experiments on NarraCrime and multiple public reasoning benchmarks show that EVAR improves both task performance and evidence faithfulness while maintaining controllable inference cost.

</details>


### [97] [SkillForge: Compositional Skill Synthesis with Verification-in-the-Loop for Generating Formally Verified Dafny Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29841)
*Yanming Liu,Xinyue Peng,Jiannan Cao,Xinyi Wang,Jinbo Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SKILLFORGE 将形式化代码合成拆解为可复用的原子技能，并通过验证驱动的调度器迭代修复，显著提升了从自然语言到 Dafny 程序的生成与验证效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从自然语言生成形式化验证程序的方法要么一次性生成且在验证失败时无法回溯，要么依赖开放式的智能体推理，导致过程不可预测且缺乏透明度，难以保证生成代码的形式化正确性。

Method: SKILLFORGE 构建了一个原子技能库，每个技能对应特定子任务（如规格推断、代码生成、不变式生成、错误诊断、定向修复），并由提示模板、工具绑定和可判定的成功标准定义。验证驱动的调度器将候选程序提交给 Dafny 验证器，依据验证失败的结构化类别将其路由至相应的修复技能，循环迭代直至证明形式正确或耗尽预算。

Result: 在自然语言到 Dafny 规格对的精选基准上，SKILLFORGE 相较于最先进的智能体方法（包括 ReAct、基于 MCTS 的修复和 RL 引导的验证）以及传统迭代基线，取得了显著的性能提升，使用更少的 token 和更低的延迟。消融实验表明每个技能均有可测量的贡献，调度器收敛快速，大多数程序在首次尝试即获验证。

Conclusion: 通过将形式化代码合成模块化为可验证的原子技能并结合验证驱动的调度机制，SKILLFORGE 有效克服了现有方法的不可回溯和不确定性问题，实现了更高效、更可靠的自然语言到形式化程序的生成。

Abstract: Generating formally verified programs from natural language remains challenging: existing approaches either produce code in a single pass without recourse when verification fails, or rely on open-ended agentic reasoning that is non-deterministic and opaque. We introduce SKILLFORGE, a framework that decomposes formal code synthesis into a library of atomic, reusable skills, each targeting a specific subtask such as specification inference, body synthesis, invariant generation, error diagnosis, or targeted repair, and defined by a prompt template, tool binding, and decidable success criterion. A verification-driven harness orchestrates these skills: it submits candidates to the Dafny verifier, diagnoses failures into structured categories, deterministically routes to the appropriate repair skill, and iterates until formal correctness is proved or a budget is exhausted. On a curated benchmark of natural language to Dafny specification pairs, SKILLFORGE substantially outperforms both state-of-the-art agentic approaches (including ReAct-style agents, MCTS-based repair, and RL-guided verification) and traditional iterative baselines, while requiring fewer tokens and lower latency. Ablation studies confirm that every skill contributes measurably, and the harness converges rapidly with the majority of programs verified on the first attempt.

</details>


### [98] [Influence-Directed Distillation: Solving the Diversity Bottleneck in Sampled-Token On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29846)
*Run Yang,Runpeng Dai,Jie Sun,Jielei Zhang,Fan Zhou,Hongtu Zhu,Peiyi Li,Longwen Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于影响的自适应在线蒸馏方法（IDA-OPD），在仅使用教师抽样token概率的情况下，解决了传统OPD导致多样性下降的问题，并在保持或提升pass@1的同时显著提升pass@k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的采样token在线蒸馏（OPD）虽然高效，但常出现多样性蒸馏失败：学生模型的pass@1提升而pass@k停滞，无法继承教师的解答多样性，需要解释并改进此现象。

Method: 引入First-Order Local Entropy Influence（FOLI）来量化每次更新的熵变化对教师-学生对数概率差和学生局部概率结构的影响，发现熵收缩对应负向影响。基于此，提出IDA-OPD：在保持熵扩张的更新不变的前提下，将熵收缩的更新替换为基于散度自适应的advantage shrinkage，只使用教师的抽样token对数概率，避免全词表前向KL计算。

Result: 在推理任务的蒸馏实验中，IDA-OPD显著提升pass@k，成功继承教师的多样性；在pass@1上与原始OPD持平或略有提升；并且在计算成本上低于所有需要全词表教师信息的方法，达到或超过最强教师感知方法的效果。

Conclusion: IDA-OPD通过影响导向的自适应策略，有效解决了OPD的多样性蒸馏失败问题，在低成本下实现了更好的多样性保留和整体性能，展示了仅依赖抽样token信息进行高效蒸馏的可行性。

Abstract: Sampled-token on-policy distillation (OPD) efficiently transfers capabilities from teacher to student using student-generated tokens, requiring teacher probabilities only for sampled tokens. Yet it frequently suffers from diversity distillation failure: the student's pass@1 improves while its pass@$k$ plateaus, failing to inherit the teacher's diversity. To explain this, we introduce First-Order Local Entropy Influence, a signed first-order proxy that decouples each update's entropy effect into the teacher--student log-probability gap and the student's local probability structure, and empirically links entropy contraction to negative-influence positions. Motivated by this, we propose Influence-Directed Adaptive On-Policy Distillation (IDA-OPD): rather than relying on costly full-vocabulary Forward-KL objectives, it preserves entropy-expanding updates while replacing entropy-contracting ones with divergence-adaptive advantage shrinkage, using only the teacher's sampled-token log-probability. Experiments on reasoning-oriented distillation show IDA-OPD consistently improves pass@$k$, inheriting the teacher's diversity through distillation, matches the strongest teacher-informed methods at strictly lower cost, and broadly maintains vanilla OPD's pass@1, all without full-vocabulary teacher information.

</details>


### [99] [GenRubric: Self-Evolving Rubric Generation for Scalable LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29856)
*Yifan Chen,Haitao Li,Qingyao Ai,Fengbin Zhu,Tat-Seng Chua,Min Zhang,Yiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GenRubric框架，通过自我进化在无标注查询上生成高质量、覆盖全面的评估rubric，无需额外人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估依赖查询特定的rubric，但人工编写成本高，自动方法往往需要推理时微调或外部监督，导致评估需求不明确且难以审计。

Method: 基于rubric诱导的自洽性：对同一查询独立采样的rubric提供对潜在评估需求的不同视角，完整rubric应产生在这些视角下都能泛化的回复。通过强化学习结合跨rubric覆盖度信号、组级和准则级奖励，对生成模型进行自我进化，规模为4B、8B、14B，覆盖多个领域。

Result: 在人类标注的rubric基准上，GenRubric的自我进化显著提升生成rubric与专家rubric诱导评估的一致性，并在未见领域保持提升，证明了其可扩展性和跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 自我进化的rubric生成能够在无需额外人工标注的情况下提供可审计、覆盖全面的查询特定评估，为大规模LLM评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as scalable evaluators for open-ended tasks. However, many LLM judges derive query-specific criteria during scoring, leaving the evaluation requirements insufficiently specified and their coverage difficult to audit. Query-specific rubrics make these requirements explicit, but expert-written rubrics are costly to construct, while existing automatic methods typically rely on inference-time refinement or external supervision. We introduce GenRubric, a self-evolving framework that improves rubric generation from unlabeled queries without requiring additional human annotations during self-evolution. Our approach is based on rubric-induced self-consistency: independently sampled rubrics for the same query provide partial views of its latent evaluation requirements, and a comprehensive rubric should induce a response that generalizes across these complementary evaluation views. We implement this principle through reinforcement learning, combining a cross-rubric comprehensiveness signal with group-level and criterion-level rewards for rubric quality. We train GenRubric models at 4B, 8B, and 14B scales across multiple domains. Experiments on human-annotated rubric benchmarks show that self-evolution improves the agreement between evaluations induced by generated rubrics and those induced by expert-written rubrics. The improvements further generalize to held-out domains, demonstrating the potential of self-evolving rubric generation for scalable and query-specific LLM evaluation. Code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/foggpoy/GenRubric.

</details>


### [100] [ManGo: Manga Active Narrative Grounding Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29865)
*Hao Qiu,Junyan Wang,Zheyuan Liu,Lei Fan,Hong Jia,Lianbo Guo,Zhulin Tao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ManGo框架，通过主动式叙事草图（ANS）在漫画视觉问答中主动挑选面板、提取证据并决定停止时机，实现无监督的答案与证据路径优化，显著提升基准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漫画问答的证据分布在有序面板、嵌入文字、重复角色和隐含事件转折中，传统的被动页面编码难以定位关键面板、筛选线索并判断何时信息足够，亟需一种主动式的证据检索与推理机制。

Method: 构建ManGo框架，引入Active Narrative Sketching（ANS），在每一步迭代中：① 依据问题指向性主动选择下一个面板；② 从选中面板抽取简洁的 grounding 线索；③ 决定是否停止以形成紧凑的证据草图。为在无人工标注的情况下学习ANS，采用多次rollout采样并进行group‑relative训练，使用两类奖励：① 基于列表自排名的答案偏好奖励；② 基于稳定有序面板轨迹的路径一致性奖励。两者加权后通过group‑relative policy training 优化模型的答案质量和证据路径质量。

Result: 在多个标准漫画理解基准上，ManGo在不同设置下均取得了最新的性能指标，显著超越已有方法。

Conclusion: 主动式的面板选择与证据草图构建能够在无监督条件下有效提升漫画视觉问答的准确性和解释性，ManGo展示了该思路的可行性与优势，为跨面板叙事理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Manga visual question answering requires models to answer questions over panel-based visual narratives, where relevant evidence is distributed across ordered panels, embedded text, recurring characters, and implicit event transitions. This structure makes passive page encoding insufficient, as the model must identify which panels to inspect, what clues to retain, and when the accumulated evidence is sufficient for answering. We propose ManGo (Manga Active Narrative Grounding Optimization), an unsupervised framework for active manga visual question answering. ManGo introduces Active Narrative Sketching (ANS), which iteratively selects panels, extracts concise grounded clues, and decides when to stop, forming a compact question-directed evidence sketch before answer generation. To optimize this behavior without human-annotated answers or rationale paths, ManGo samples multiple ANS rollouts and applies group-relative training with two rewards: answer preference from listwise self-ranking and path consistency from stable ordered panel trajectories. The combined reward is optimized with group-relative policy training, encouraging the model to improve both final answers and the panel-level evidence paths that support them. Experiments on standard manga understanding benchmarks show that ManGo achieves state-of-the-art performance across different settings.

</details>


### [101] [Improving Argument Saliency Coverage in Small LLMs for Long Legal Opinion Summarization via Sequence-Level Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29884)
*Mohamed Elaraby,Ahmed Elhady,Diane Litman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过从长上下文教师模型进行序列级蒸馏，使用极少量（约10条）教师生成的摘要监督，即可显著提升小模型在长法律意见摘要中的论点覆盖率，且效果优于直接微调专家摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型在长法律意见摘要时难以捕捉最关键的论点，亟需一种无需人工标注、数据高效的提升方法。

Method: 利用具备长上下文能力的教师模型生成摘要（以及可选的推理链），对学生模型进行序列级蒸馏；在不同学生模型规模上比较仅摘要蒸馏、仅摘要微调以及摘要+推理链蒸馏的效果。

Result: 在法律意见摘要任务上，蒸馏始终优于基于专家摘要的微调；仅需约10条教师生成的摘要即可获得大部分性能提升；加入推理链蒸馏虽能保持竞争力，但相较于仅摘要蒸馏收益有限。

Conclusion: 序列级蒸馏是一种简单、无标注、数据高效的策略，可显著提升小模型在长法律文本摘要中的论点显著性覆盖，且在实际应用中仅需极少量教师生成的监督数据。

Abstract: We show that sequence-level distillation from a capable long-context teacher model is a simple, annotation-free, and data-efficient strategy for improving argument saliency coverage in long legal opinion summarization, where small LLMs often struggle to retain the most salient argumentative content. Across student model sizes, distillation consistently surpasses tuning on expert-written summaries in our legal-opinion setting. We further demonstrate that most gains are achieved with as few as ~10 training summaries, highlighting the strong data efficiency of teacher-generated supervision. Finally, we find that summary distillation is sufficient for improvements: reasoning-chain distillation remains competitive with summary-only distillation, but provides marginal benefit when combined with summary supervision.

</details>


### [102] [Check The Scoreboard: An Analysis of Scoring Schemes on Multiple-Choice Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29887)
*Nishant Balepur,Paiheng Xu,Wei Ai,Eunsol Choi,Rachel Rudinger,Jordan Boyd-Graber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出六种教育启发的多选题评分方案，超越传统准确率，评估模型的排除干扰项、弃权、置信度校准和自我纠正等能力，发现不同模型在这些维度上的表现差异，并能更好预测用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多选题评估仅使用正确率，无法捕捉模型在排除干扰、主动弃权、置信度校准和自我纠正等更细粒度能力，这限制了对模型真实能力的评估，尤其在教育测试中评分方案是关键设计因素。

Method: 设计六种受教育测试启发的评分方案，分别衡量：1) 干扰项消除能力，2) 弃权行为，3) 置信度校准，4) 自我纠正等。将这些方案应用于31个大型语言模型的多选题基准，比较其在不同方案下的排名变化，并与LLM Arena用户偏好进行关联分析。

Result: 1) 在新评分方案下，模型排名与仅使用重新表述的正确率提示的排名出现显著变化；2) 这些方案的评分更能预测用户在LLM Arena中的偏好；3) 揭示模型间的能力差异，例如GPT‑5几乎不弃权且能主动自我纠正，而较弱的开源模型倾向于弃权并难以排除错误选项。

Conclusion: 替代传统正确率的评分方案能够更全面地衡量语言模型的多样化能力，并在模型选择和评估上提供更有价值的指引。作者建议将此类评分框架推广至除多选题外的其他任务，以获得更细致的模型能力评估。

Abstract: Multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) benchmarks in NLP use number-right scoring (accuracy), but in educational testing, the scoring scheme, the combination of the response mode models follow and the rule for grading responses, is a key design choice that dictates which abilities to reward. We examine how alternatives to number right change what MCQA measures with six education-inspired schemes that assess abilities beyond accuracy: distractor elimination, abstention, confidence calibration, and self-correction. On LLM benchmarks, these schemes: 1) shift rankings of 31 LLMs beyond rephrased number right prompts; 2) better predict the LLMs users prefer in LLM Arena; and 3) reveal distinct model capabilities, like that GPT-5 rarely abstains and readily self-corrects, while weaker open-weight models often abstain and hesitate to eliminate choices. Given the benefits of alternative scoring schemes, we discuss ways to extend them to tasks beyond MCQA.

</details>


### [103] [VibeJam: A User Study Platform for Web Development with Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29889)
*Nishant Balepur,Connor Baumler,Valerie Chen,Eunsol Choi,Rachel Rudinger,Jordan Boyd-Graber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VibeJam 是一个基于浏览器的用户研究平台，支持用户与 AI 编码代理协作开发网站，提供自定义、diff 审查、聊天、计划模式和实时预览，实验表明其易用且能提升代码质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编程 AI 越来越具备代理特性，用户直接让大语言模型编辑代码并审查，但现有 NLP 研究多采用离线评估，缺乏对真实编程交互的在线研究支持，导致对编程代理实际使用方式的认识不足。

Method: 构建 VibeJam 平台，默认集成开源 Aider 代理，加入 diff review、chat、plan 模式以及实时网站预览；平台支持代理定制。进行两类用户实验：55 人的游戏化网站创建任务的先导研究，分别邀请 5 名经验丰富的 AI 程序员和 13 名初学者使用平台完成任务。

Result: 经验丰富的 AI 程序员认为系统有趣、简洁且类似商业工具；初学者在相同任务中使用 VibeJam 能生成比仅使用代理更高质量的网站。

Conclusion: VibeJam 证明了浏览器端协作平台的可行性，能够提升用户与编码代理的交互体验和产出质量，且开源可促进后续扩展和更大规模的在线研究。

Abstract: Programming with AI is increasingly agentic, users prompt LLMs to directly edit their code and review the changes, with adoption growing especially for web development tasks. Despite this growth, most NLP work uses offline evaluation and lacks support for online studies, losing insights into how programmers truly use coding agents. We release VibeJam, a browser-based user study platform for users to collaborate with AI agents to develop websites. VibeJam enables agent customization and uses the open-source Aider agent by default, and to mirror downstream use, we add diff review, chat and plan modes, and live website previews. In a pilot study with 55 released, game-based website creation tasks, five experienced AI programmers rate our system as fun, simple, and resembling commercial tools, while 13 junior students use VibeJam to make websites of higher quality than agents in the same task. We open-source VibeJam to spur extensions and support studies on how coding agents can help users.

</details>


### [104] [En-ViMedNER: An English-Vietnamese Parallel Biomedical Corpus with UMLS Semantic Type Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29890)
*Nhu Vo,Phuong Nguyen,Nu Uyen Phuong Le,Inigo Jauregi Unanue,Dung D. Le,Massimo Piccardi,Wray Buntine*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个英-越平行生物医学实体识别语料库En-ViMedNER，包含4,392篇PubMed摘要对，44,892句对和202,949对对齐实体，覆盖21种UMLS语义类型，并提供基准模型，越语NER最高F1 52.70，跨语言NER最高F1 45.44。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有英文UMLS标注语料推动了英文生物医学NER，但缺乏对应的越南语资源，限制了越南语医疗AI的研发。需要一个跨语言、统一标签空间的越南语生物医学NER语料，以实现与英文资源的直接比较并促进越南语相关研究。

Method: 通过自动机器翻译将MedMentions的英文摘要翻译为越南语，随后由专家后编辑；利用大语言模型（LLM）辅助投射UMLS标签并进行人工核验与仲裁，构建大规模银标语料并保留经人工审校的mini-test子集。提供完整的语料构建流水线。

Result: 在越南语NER任务中，使用越南语监督的编码器模型、英文监督的多语言编码器模型以及基于提示的LLM进行基准测试，最佳模型F1为52.70（测试集）/53.78（mini-test）。在英-越跨语言NER任务中，编码器-解码器模型和提示式LLM的最佳F1为45.44（mini-test）。

Conclusion: En-ViMedNER为越南语生物医学NLP提供了首个大规模、跨语言、UMLS统一标签的语料库，展示了当前模型在越南语NER上的有限性能，为后续模型改进和资源扩展提供了基准和平台。

Abstract: Biomedical Named Entity Recognition (NER) is fundamental to healthcare AI applications, including clinical decision support and medical information extraction. While corpora with Unified Medical Language System (UMLS) annotations, such as MedMentions, have driven progress in English biomedical NER, no comparable resource exists for Vietnamese. This paper presents En-ViMedNER, the first English-Vietnamese parallel biomedical NER corpus annotated with UMLS semantic types, which are language-neutral codes providing a shared cross-lingual label space and ensuring direct comparability with existing UMLS-based resources. The corpus contains 4,392 PubMed abstract pairs, 44,892 English-Vietnamese sentence pairs, and 202,949 aligned entity-mention pairs across 21 semantic types adapted from the MedMentions ST21pv dataset. To balance quality and scalability, we have constructed the corpus through automatic translation, expert post-editing, LLM-assisted label projection, and human verification and adjudication. We characterize En-ViMedNER as a large-scale silver-standard corpus with a human-audited and consensus-corrected mini-test subset. We evaluate En-ViMedNER in two settings: (i) Vietnamese-input/Vietnamese-output biomedical NER and (ii) English-input/Vietnamese-output cross-lingual NER. For Vietnamese NER, we benchmark Vietnamese-supervised encoder models, English-supervised multilingual encoder models, and prompt-based LLMs. The best model achieves an F1 score of 52.70 on the test set and 53.78 on the mini-test set. For cross-lingual NER, we benchmark encoder-decoder models and prompt-based LLMs. The best model achieves an F1 score of 45.44 on the mini-test set. We publicly release our corpus, corpus construction pipeline, and baseline models to facilitate future Vietnamese biomedical NLP research.

</details>


### [105] [When History Is Multimodal: Rethinking Context Management for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29897)
*Jiaqi Su,Cong Pang,Jiawei Hong,Tiankuo Yao,Zixuan Chen,Xin Lou,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将视觉渲染（VR）作为长时程智能体的上下文管理器，并在统一实验平台下与四种基线比较，发现视觉记忆在多模态任务中天然优势。基于此，提出无需训练的 VERA 策略，在文本任务上等效于 VR，在多模态任务上保留原始视觉信息，显著降低非缓存 token 使用并提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体需要在有限的工作上下文中压缩不断增长的交互历史。现有光学记忆方法主要将像素视为文本历史的密集编码，且往往需要额外的 SFT、蒸馏或强化学习来弥补性能下降，导致两个未解决的问题：① 在公平、受控的比较下，视觉渲染作为上下文管理器的表现如何；② 当历史本身是多模态时，这种表示是否具备天然优势。

Method: 将上下文管理定义为受预算约束的历史转换问题，提出 Visual Rendering（VR）作为一种表征式上下文管理器。随后在统一的实验框架（相同的策略模型、触发器和任务域）下，对四个文本为主和三个多模态基准进行评估，比较 VR 与四种基线（No Compression、Discard-All、Sliding Window、Summarization）。在此基础上，设计 VERA（Visual Evidence‑Retaining）策略：在文本基准上采用与 VR 相同的渲染方式，在多模态基准上直接保留原始视觉观测，无需额外的记忆操作或训练。

Result: 实验表明：① 视觉记忆能够自然承载视觉证据，在多模态基准上表现优于其他基线；② VERA 在几乎所有基准上将累计非缓存 token 数量降低 31.5%–63.1%（相较于 No Compression）；③ 在文本基准上，VERA 与现有管理器性能持平；④ 在多模态基准上，VERA 达到所有基线最高的准确率。

Conclusion: 视觉渲染是一种有效的上下文管理方式，尤其在多模态长时程任务中保留原始视觉信息能够提供天然优势。VERA 作为一种无需训练的、确定性渲染的上下文管理策略，兼顾了文本和多模态任务的需求，支持了“模态保持”视角在长时程上下文管理中的重要性。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents need a context manager to compress growing interaction histories into a bounded working context, via passive strategies or active strategies that decide how memory is accessed and reorganized. Meanwhile, prior optical-memory work mainly treats pixels as a dense codec for textualized histories, often presupposing that rendering context into optical memory incurs a significant performance drop relative to text, thus coupling this representation with SFT, self-distillation, or reinforcement learning to close this gap, leaving unresolved (i) how visual rendering performs as a context manager under a fair, controlled comparison, and (ii) whether this carrier offers a native advantage when history is inherently multimodal. In this paper, we formulate context management as a budget-constrained history transformation and introduce Visual Rendering (VR) as a representational context manager. Under a shared harness, policy model, trigger, and task domain, we evaluate VR on four text-centric and three multimodal benchmarks against four baselines (No Compression, Discard-All, Sliding Window, Summarization), finding visual memory is a natural carrier of native visual evidence. Building on this finding, we propose VERA (Visual Evidence-Retaining strategy for long-horizon Agents), a training-free context manager built on deterministic rendering with no exposed memory operations: on text-centric benchmarks it renders textual history as VR does, while on multimodal benchmarks it retains native visual observations instead of translating them into text. Across nearly all benchmarks, VERA cuts cumulative non-cache tokens by 31.5%-63.1% versus No Compression, matches existing managers on text-centric tasks, and achieves the highest accuracy among all baselines on multimodal tasks, supporting a modality-preserving view of long-horizon context management.

</details>


### [106] [REIGN: Refurbished Embeddings with Integrated Guidance Networks for Efficient Context-Length Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29899)
*Devrim Çavuşoğlu,Emre Akbaş*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: REIGN是一种对比学习的双编码器模型，利用冻结的引导网络对文档进行块级嵌入并缓存，显著降低长文档稠密检索的训练成本，同时在多个基准上以更小参数量匹配或接近更大模型的检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档稠密检索成本高，传统token级编码器随序列长度呈二次增长，现有长上下文模型要么需要特殊架构要么依赖数十亿参数的LLM，难以在计算和存储上高效实现。

Method: 提出REIGN（Refurbished Embeddings with Integrated Guidance Networks），采用对比学习的双编码器框架。先用冻结的Guidance Network（GN）对文档切块生成上下文化块嵌入并缓存至磁盘，检索时只对这些块嵌入进行双编码器处理，实现 token 级处理与文档级推理解耦。单块输入仍使用GN。通过块级嵌入的缓存，将训练成本降低约四个数量级。

Result: 在内部Wikipedia基准、LoCo OOD套件以及真实专利检索案例中，REIGN在相同或更小的参数预算下匹配或接近现有长上下文稠密检索模型。配对显著性检验显示，在专利任务上表现相当于参数量大1.6‑4.3倍的模型；在LoCo上nDCG@10仅落后0.65。

Conclusion: REIGN通过块级嵌入缓存和双编码器设计，实现了长文档稠密检索的高效训练和推理，在保持检索质量的同时大幅降低计算资源需求，为实际大规模长文档检索提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dense retrieval over long documents is expensive. Token-level encoders scale quadratically in sequence length, and most long-context embedding models reach 32K tokens only through architectural workarounds or by stretching billion-parameter LLMs. We propose REIGN (Refurbished Embeddings with Integrated Guidance Networks), a contrastively trained bi-encoder that operates on sequences of contextualised chunk embeddings from a frozen Guidance Network (GN) rather than on raw tokens. REIGN targets multi-chunk inputs, primarily for document-to-document retrieval; single-chunk inputs stay with the GN. Decoupling token-level processing from document-level reasoning, and caching the GN embeddings to disk, cuts per-document training cost by roughly four orders of magnitude relative to chunked Transformer fine-tuning. We also release a synthetic long-document retrieval benchmark for contrastive training and evaluation at long context lengths. Across an in-distribution Wikipedia benchmark, the LoCo out-of-distribution suite, and a real-world patent retrieval case study, REIGN matches dense long-context retrievers at smaller parameter budgets in each regime. A paired significance test puts it on par with models 1.6-4.3x larger on the patent task, and it stays within 0.65 nDCG@10 of a 20x-larger model on LoCo.

</details>


### [107] [When Less is More: Understanding When Token Filtering Helps and Fails in AI-generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29903)
*Xiaoyang Han,Lvxiaowei Xu,Ming Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，在AI生成文本检测中，保留更少的token（约40%）有时能提升检测效果，提出基于熵差分数（EGS）的top‑k累计概率过滤作为诊断工具，并系统分析其在不同源语言模型下的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的快速发展，AI生成文本的检测变得尤为重要；现有零样本检测方法普遍认为保留更多token能提升检测可靠性，但缺乏对token数量与检测性能关系的系统研究。

Method: 利用熵差分数（Entropy Gap Score, EGS）引入top‑k累计概率过滤作为诊断探针；在三种典型设置下实验，结合典型集理论、熵校准和分布分析，对过滤过程进行理论与实证分析。

Result: 实验表明，过滤在弱源语言模型下有效，低熵token会对检测产生负面影响；而在强源语言模型下，低熵token并不显著有害，过滤效果不佳。保留约40% token 可在部分场景获得最佳检测性能。

Conclusion: 首次系统性证明并非所有token仅是无信息的，部分token因熵校准错误会系统性地削弱检测效果，揭示了token级检测的双向权衡：适度过滤可提升弱模型检测，但对强模型可能无益甚至有害。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs) has made AI-generated text detection increasingly critical. Existing zero-shot detectors assume that more token-level evidence leads to more reliable detection. However, our empirical study challenges this consensus: fewer tokens sometimes work better, retaining only 40% can yield optimal performance, yet this benefit is not universal. Using the Entropy Gap Score (EGS), we introduce top-$k$ cumulative probability filtering as a diagnostic probe. Across three representative settings, filtering exhibits strikingly different behaviors. We analyze EGS via typical set theory and quantify its dynamics through entropy calibration and distribution analysis. We find that filtering helps for weak source LMs, where low-entropy tokens are harmful, but fails for strong source LMs, where they are not notably harmful. Our work provides the first systematic analysis showing that some tokens are not merely uninformative but systematically harmful due to entropy miscalibration, revealing a two-sided trade-off in token-level detection.

</details>


### [108] [IndicDetect: Evaluating Cross-Lingual LLM-Generated Text Detection for Hindi, Telugu, and Tamil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29919)
*Bhaskar Ganesh Devalla,Junchao Wu,Nilesh Dokuparthi,Greeshma Yaluru,Tatiana Muniz Rodriguez,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出IndicDetect基准，系统评估印地语、泰卢固语和泰米尔语中AI生成文本检测器在领域、生成器和对抗扰动下的鲁棒性，发现现有检测器在跨生成器和对抗攻击时表现显著下降，尤其是印地语受影响最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的快速普及，需要可靠的AI生成文本检测技术，但现有基准几乎忽视印地语、泰卢固语和泰米尔语，且评估环境过于理想化，无法反映真实分布的变化。

Method: 构建IndicDetect基准，收集高质量的人类撰写文本并配对相应的LLM生成文本，覆盖多个领域和生成模型；设计统一的评估流程，分别在领域迁移、生成器迁移和对抗扰动三种情形下测试统计型和神经网络型检测器。

Result: 实验表明：监督式神经检测器在同分布数据上表现良好，但在未见生成器和对抗攻击下性能大幅下降；无监督的训练免费方法在这些情形下退化更严重；不同语言的鲁棒性差异显著，印地语在对抗扰动下降幅最大。

Conclusion: 现有检测器在印地语等Indic语言环境中的主要瓶颈是鲁棒性而非最高准确率。IndicDetect提供标准数据划分、评估协议和基线，为构建语言感知且鲁棒的AI生成文本检测奠定基础。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of LLMs has further heightened the need to develop dependable AI-generated text detection, especially beyond English. Nevertheless, current benchmarks pay little attention to Indic languages and test detectors in idealized settings that do not represent the real world. We present a generalized benchmark for AI-generated text detection in Hindi, Telugu, and Tamil, which we call IndicDetect, designed to assess the robustness of detectors under realistic distribution shifts. IndicDetect comprises highly curated human-written texts matched with LLM-generated counterparts across various domains and generators, and systematically evaluates detectors in the presence of domain shift, generator shift, and adversarial perturbation. Using a single and repeatable evaluation scheme, we evaluate a wide range of statistical and neural detectors. We find substantial robustness failures: supervised neural detectors perform well in-distribution, while training-free methods degrade considerably under unseen generators and adversarial attacks. The severity of these failures varies across languages, with Hindi exhibiting the largest overall degradation under adversarial perturbations. These results highlight that the primary weakness of existing detectors in Indic settings lies in their robustness, not in their peak accuracy. IndicDetect provides standard data splits, an evaluation protocol, and baselines to establish a robust, language-aware foundation for AI-generated text detection in Indic scripts.

</details>


### [109] [Sleight of Word Benchmark: Can Language Models Notice If Their Own Output Was Tampered With?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29921)
*Alberto Cetoli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出在语言模型生成过程中通过持续替换单词来检测其对外部干扰的感知，构建了“Sleight of Word”基准，并在19种开源模型上评估了惊讶度和文本反应两类指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的输出可能在生成时被外部干扰篡改，缺乏有效手段评估模型对这种实时干扰的感知能力，亟需一种简单可复现的测试方法。

Method: 在生成过程中始终将特定单词替换为另一个词，记录模型的内部概率变化（惊讶度）以及生成文本的质量变化，形成“Sleight of Word”基准，对19个开源权重模型进行系统评测。

Result: 实验显示不同模型对单词替换的敏感程度差异显著，惊讶度指标能够捕捉到模型对干扰的即时反应，而文本反应指标揭示了生成内容的鲁棒性和一致性。

Conclusion: Sleight of Word提供了一种简洁有效的方式来衡量语言模型在生成过程中的外部干扰感知，为评估模型鲁棒性和安全性提供了新工具。

Abstract: The output of a Language Model can be tampered with \emph{while} the model is writing it. A simple test can thus be constructed by evaluating the model's perception of this external perturbation. In this spirit, a simple benchmark is built in which a single word is consistently substituted with another in the generation process. We call this method \emph{Sleight of Word}. Two distinct axes are measured: metrics that relate to the model's surprise, as well as an evaluation of the textual reaction for 19 different open-weight language models.

</details>


### [110] [Token Counts Are Not Model Lineage: A Frozen-Threshold Holdout Study of Black-Box LLM API Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29930)
*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，提示词计数在共享分词器和聊天模板的模型之间能形成一致的指纹，但其对模型家族归属的判别能力有限，尤其在未见数据上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型通过中转和转售API提供，如何在黑箱环境下辨别模型来源变得重要；提示词计数作为低成本信号的有效性缺乏系统验证。

Method: 在24对已标记的API端点上进行冻结阈值实验，分为开发集和保持集，各12对；每对进行三次时间重复，每次30条受控文本；引入有效性门控的结果契约，使用平移不变的精确匹配得分并设定阈值0.725。

Result: 开发集全部区分成功；保持集仅6对满足三次重复规则，平衡准确率0.75，灵敏度0.50（95% Wilson区间0.15–0.85），特异度1.00（0.342–1.00）；两对同族模型未达阈值；共4,320次API调用，记录可重放，持有集包含189次非200响应和157次缺少计数的成功响应。

Conclusion: 提示词计数一致性可作为共享分词堆栈的指纹，但不足以单独判定模型家族血缘，需结合其他特征使用。
