Method: AIA²框架包括：①利用潜在语义分布自动发现数据中的子群不平衡；②从中抽取学习难度大且子群不平衡的切片；③调用大语言模型生成子群感知的增强样本，以平衡各子群分布。整个过程无需事先的子群标签。

Result: 在5个包含丰富属性的主流语料库上实验，AIA²在最差子群的表现上显著提升，并相较于多种竞争基线实现了稳定的整体增益。消融实验表明各组件均对性能提升有贡献，进一步分析证实该方法在最差子群鲁棒性方面具备实用性和一致性。

Conclusion: AIA²提供了一种实用且一致的方式，在缺乏显式子群标注的情况下提升模型对数据子群不平衡的鲁棒性，尤其对最差子群表现有显著改善。

Abstract: Attributes describing data content and context can induce diverse imbalance patterns that go beyond label imbalance alone. However, existing studies primarily address label imbalance while overlooking data attributes, such as topics and demographics, which can induce meaningful subgroup structure while causing model degradation on underrepresented subgroups. We propose Attribute-Agnostic Imbalance Augmentation (AIA$^{2}$), a framework for improving model robustness under varying subgroup imbalances without explicit subgroup annotations. AIA$^{2}$ automatically discovers varying imbalances via latent semantic distributions, obtains slices with both learning difficulty and subgroup imbalance deficits, and deploys a large language model (LLM) for subgroup-aware imbalance augmentation. We have evaluated AIA$^{2}$ on 5 popular corpora with rich domains and their attribute values, covering social issues and diverse topics. Results show improved performance on the lowest-performing subgroups and consistent gains over competitive baselines. Ablation studies confirm complementary contributions from each component, and additional analyses show that AIA$^{2}$ provides a practical and consistent way to improve worst-group robustness under data subgroup imbalance. Code is available at https://github.com/trust-nlp/AIA2-Subgroup-Robustness.

</details>


### [148] [Lazy Grounding: Attacking Search Agents with Factual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30303)
*Yulin Zhang,Yukun Huang,Sanxing Chen,Tianyi Lin,Ziang Yang,Xunjian Yin,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 搜索代理在检索到的真实文档中也会出现‘懒惰落地’问题：当相邻问题的证据被错误地用于当前问题时，模型会采纳邻近答案，导致准确率下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然检索增强可以降低幻觉，但检索系统的攻击面不仅包括恶意或错误信息，还可能因合理但不相关的证据被误用而导致错误答案，亟需研究这种潜在风险。

Method: 构造基准问题的答案改变改写，使得每个改写问题都有对应的真实支持文档。将这些文档检索到原始问题上，评估模型在12组模型-基准组合上的表现，分析证据出现位置和答案形状对误导程度的影响。

Result: 在12个模型-基准组合中，邻近证据平均使准确率下降5.9个百分点，最大下降17.3个百分点；所有设置均出现邻近答案采纳。证据出现得更晚或更具答案特征时，误导效果更强。

Conclusion: 搜索代理必须防御的不仅是错误信息，还要防止对真实但不相关证据的误用，即‘懒惰落地’现象。需要设计更鲁棒的检索与证据匹配机制。

Abstract: Search agents reduce hallucination by grounding answers in retrieved web evidence. Yet reliance on retrieval also creates an attack surface: poisoned corpora with false or malicious documents can cause agents to reproduce misinformation. We show that falsehood is not necessary -- a search agent can be misled by factual evidence for a nearby question, adopting that nearby answer even when it does not answer the current question. We call this failure lazy grounding. We expose lazy grounding using nearby evidence from answer-changing rewrites of benchmark questions. Each document truthfully supports a neighboring rewritten question, but is surfaced for the original question. Across 12 model-benchmark pairs, nearby evidence reduces accuracy by 5.9 points on average and by up to 17.3 points, while inducing nearby-answer adoption in every setting. The effect is stronger when nearby evidence appears later or is more answer-shaped. Our results show that robust search agents must defend against not only misinformation but also the misapplication of factual evidence. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/frankyzha/lazy-grounding.

</details>


### [149] [Beyond Token-Level Guidance: Inference-Time Alignment of Specialized LLMs via Cross-Family Representation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30319)
*Jin Gan,Xin Li,Jun Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CREST，一种在推理阶段通过安全方向引导隐藏表征的对齐方法，解决专用模型与通用指导模型能力正交导致的安全性下降问题，在保持领域能力的同时提升安全性，安全基准提升最高22.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用领域微调的LLM在安全性上会退化，现有推理时对齐方法要么无法提升安全性，要么会破坏领域能力，根本原因在于专用基模型与通用指导模型的能力正交，导致指导信号不可靠，尤其表现为停止标记干扰。

Method: CREST在推理阶段不在token层面进行干预，而是利用任意家族的指导模型提取安全方向，直接在基模型的隐藏表征上施加引导，从而避免停止标记冲突并实现安全对齐。

Result: 在多个安全基准上，CREST相较于现有基线提升最高22.2%，同时保持或提升了专用领域的生成能力，对已对齐模型也不产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 通过在隐藏表征层面的安全方向引导，CREST提供了一种高效、即插即用的推理时对齐方案，成功解决了专用模型安全退化的问题，为大模型在专业领域的安全部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) finetuned for specialized domains represent crucial high-impact applications. Inference-time alignment improves safety degraded from specialization finetuning without requiring substantial computational resources, complementing finetuning-based methods with an easy-to-use, plug-and-play solution. However, existing inference-time methods fail to reliably improve safety without disrupting domain capability. We identify the root cause as complementary expertise orthogonality: specialized base models and general-domain guidance models have orthogonal competencies, making the guidance signal unreliable for specialized generation. This primarily manifests as stop token interference, where the guidance model's tendency toward continuation overrides the base model's decision to stop, burying correct answers under guidance-induced continuation. To address this problem, we propose CREST, an inference-time alignment method that steers base model hidden representations using safety directions extracted from a guidance model of any family, avoiding token-level structural limitations entirely. CREST improves safety where specialization has weakened it while preserving both domain-specific capability and the safety of already well-aligned models, outperforming baselines by up to 22.2\% on safety benchmarks. Our code is available at: https://github.com/DecayingSeart/CREST.

</details>


### [150] [On the Design of Qwen3.8-Next Architecture: Evaluation, Efficiency, and Training Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30320)
*Zihan Qiu,Zekun Wang,Xiao Li,Yanpeng Li,Yang Xu,Yixuan Wang,Huaqing Zhang,Rui Men,Bochao Mao,Chengruidong Zhang,Fan Zhou,Hao Luo,Haofeng Huang,Haoran Lian,Haoyan Huang,Hongqing Chen,Jianwei Zhang,Jing Xu,Junjie Wang,Langshi Chen,Liangyu Wang,Linlang Jiang,Man Yuan,Minmin Sun,Peng Jin,Siqi Zhang,Siyu Wang,Xingzhang Ren,Yakai Wang,Yi Zhang,Yiming Dong,Yizhong Cao,Yubo Ma,Yunfei Mao,Bo Zheng,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Qwen3.8-Flash-Next，一种 125B 参数的稀疏混合专家模型，激活 6B 参数/标记，配合 51B 参数的 n-gram 嵌入，实现 1/3 参数、1/3 训练标记、约 1/9 FLOPs 的高效预训练，在 14 项基准中 8 项领先前代模型，且在下游任务上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型预训练中，计算资源和训练成本是主要瓶颈。作者希望在显著降低激活参数量、训练标记数和 FLOPs 的前提下，保持或提升模型的下游性能和训练稳定性。

Method: 设计了层级混合的 Token Mixing（每四层一个全局注意力，其余使用 Gated DeltaNet），在继续预训练阶段将全注意力层替换为 Qwen Sparse Attention（微块粒度评分的轻量索引器）。残差流扩展为四分支并通过元素级门控（Gated Residual）。在主干之外加入单层 n‑gram 嵌入表，表格在主机内存中预取。使用 Muon 优化器并系统评估每项改动的损失、下游基准、训练/推理成本及超参数影响。

Result: 在 14 项预训练基准中，Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 在 8 项上超越 397B‑A17B 前代模型，其他 6 项仅落后最多 2.6 分。相较于前代，激活参数、训练标记数分别降低至约 1/3，训练 FLOPs 降至约 1/9。n‑gram 词表扩大可持续降低损失但下游精度饱和。Muon 优化器提升了最佳学习率和批量大小，免除批量大小预热，显著增强了训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过联合考虑损失、下游性能、效率和稳定性，作者提出的架构与 Muon 优化器实现了更高效、更强大且更稳健的大模型训练方案，为稀疏混合专家模型的设计提供了可复用的经验。

Abstract: We describe the architecture and ablations of Qwen3.8-Flash-Next, a sparse mixture-of-experts model with 125B parameters, 6B activated per token, and additional 51B parameters of n-gram embedding tables held off the accelerator. On fourteen pre-training benchmarks the model leads the 397B-A17B predecessor on eight and trails it on the rest by at most 2.6 points, at 1/3 the activated parameters, 1/3 the training tokens, and roughly 1/9 the training FLOPs. Token mixing uses a layer-wise hybrid of Gated DeltaNet (GDN) and global attention, with one full-attention layer in every four; at continued-pretraining time those full-attention layers are replaced by Qwen Sparse Attention (QSA), which scores context at micro-block granularity with a compressed lightweight indexer. The residual stream is widened to four branches and read through an elementwise gate, a design we call the Gated Residual (GR). Capacity is added outside the backbone by a single n-gram embedding layer whose tables are prefetched from host memory. We evaluate every candidate change along three axes: loss together with downstream benchmarks; the cost of the change in training, prefill and decode; and its effect on the optimal hyperparameters and training stability. Loss and downstream accuracy do not always move together: enlarging the n-gram vocabulary lowers loss monotonically while downstream accuracy saturates. The architecture and the Muon optimizer together shift the optimal learning rate and batch size upwards, render batch-size warmup unnecessary, and substantially improve stability under stress tests. Loss, benchmarks, efficiency and stability form one design problem. Solved jointly, they yield a recipe that is simultaneously more efficient, more capable and more stable.

</details>


### [151] [Sequential Trajectories and Simultaneous Blending: Multi-Emotion Modeling for Instruction-Following TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30325)
*Yan Zhou,Yun Hong,Yang Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HybridEmo是一种后训练框架，通过先用监督微调再用混合奖励的组相对策略优化，实现了情感轨迹和情感混合两类多情感TTS任务的显著提升，兼顾情感完整性、强度和说话人相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感TTS系统仅能控制单一的句子级情感，缺乏对跨句子情感变化（情感轨迹）和同句子内多情感共存（情感混合）的建模能力，且监督微调未直接评估情感特征，单情感奖励也无法提供结构化或配对式的反馈。

Method: HybridEmo先进行监督微调（SFT）得到初始模型，然后采用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）进行后训练。针对情感轨迹，使用段对齐一致性奖励，将平均证据与最弱阶段证据结合，保证阶段正确性与完整性；针对情感混合，构建基于GMM的奖励，利用离线情感空间中目标情感锚点的帧级支持并加入句子级弱目标间距。两条分支共享ASR奖励，并在统一策略中路由。

Result: 在MultiEmo-Test数据集上，HybridEmo显著提升了情感轨迹的正确率和情感混合的强度，且说话人相似度几乎无下降。人工评测显示，HybridEmo优于CosyVoice 3和EmoVoice‑0.5B，且与Qwen3‑TTS的偏好基本持平。

Conclusion: 通过后训练的混合奖励和策略优化，HybridEmo成功弥补了监督微调与单情感奖励的不足，实现了多情感TTS的高质量控制，为情感语音合成的细粒度调控提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Natural-language instructions enable flexible control of synthesized speech, yet emotional TTS systems primarily model a single utterance-level affect, leaving multi-emotion control underexplored. We study two complementary multi-emotion TTS tasks: emotion trajectory, which spans several ordered affective stages, and emotion blending, in which multiple emotions coexist throughout an utterance. These tasks expose a supervision mismatch: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) does not explicitly evaluate emotion features, while single-emotion rewards provide neither structure-aware feedback for trajectory completion nor pair-aware feedback for blending. We introduce HybridEmo, a post-training framework that initializes both tasks with SFT and then aligns the speech-token policy through Group Relative Policy Optimization using a sample-aware hybrid reward. For trajectory samples, segment-aligned consistency combines average and weakest-stage evidence to preserve the correctness and completeness of prescribed stages. For blending samples, a GMM-based reward combines frame-level support from the union of target-emotion anchors in an offline emotion space with an utterance-level weaker-target margin. Both branches share an ASR reward and are routed within a unified policy. On MultiEmo-Test, HybridEmo significantly improves trajectory correctness and blending intensity, without a noticeable degradation in speaker similarity. Human evaluation prefers HybridEmo to CosyVoice 3 and EmoVoice-0.5B, with nearly balanced preferences against Qwen3-TTS.

</details>


### [152] [Do Small Models Use the Law You Give Them? Measuring Context Use on a Bilingual Bangladesh Legal Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30327)
*Moniruzzaman Mahadi,Abrar Mohammed Tanzim Alam,Sayma Siddika Monalisa,Mir Mohammad Asif Abdullah,Swakkhar Shatabda,Md Adnan Arefeen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 微调提升了孟加拉法律问答的整体准确率，但并未增强模型对上下文中提供的法律条文的依赖；通过控制选项评分、循环选项和删除关键法条的实验，发现精调主要提升了答案评分和检索能力，而对法条使用的依赖并未增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在法律问答任务中，模型的高准确率并不一定意味着其真正理解并利用了提供的法律条文。作者希望区分微调带来的整体性能提升与模型对法条使用的真实改进，特别是在双语孟加拉法律场景下。

Method: 构建了层级保持的法典语料库、2,165 条双语微调样本和 150 条控制式供给法条。对六种指令微调模型（Llama‑3.2‑1B/3B、Qwen3.5‑0.8/2/4B、Gemma‑4‑E2B）进行三组 LoRA 随机种子实验。通过约束选项字母评分、循环选项顺序以及有/无关键法条的对照实验，分别评估答案评分、检索和法条使用的影响。

Result: 在 398 条律师协会输出中，Qwen3.5‑2B seed‑42 适配器的整体准确率提升 50.0%，但仅有 3.0% 来自选项评分；Gemma‑4‑E2B 两种评分方法得出不同结论。当保证关键法条出现时，六个基准模型的四序准则准确率提升 14.7%‑19.3%；删除法条后模型准确率下降 8.0%‑15.3%，适配器下降 13.8%‑14.9%。差分‑差分分析显示微调后模型对关键法条的依赖并未提升。

Conclusion: 法律领域的微调效果必须区分评分、检索和法条使用三类因素；单纯的准确率提升并不等同于模型对法律条文的更好利用。研究强调在评估法律适配时需使用控制实验以避免误判。

Abstract: Fine-tuning can improve legal question-answering accuracy without improving how models use law supplied in context. We study this distinction in bilingual Bangladeshi legal QA, where observed errors can arise from answer scoring, retrieval, or failure to use relevant law. We construct a hierarchy-preserving statutory corpus, 2,165 reviewed bilingual fine-tuning examples, and a 150-item supplied-law control. We evaluate six instruction-tuned models: Llama-3.2-1B, Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen3.5-0.8B, Qwen3.5-2B, Qwen3.5-4B, and Gemma-4-E2B, with three LoRA seeds per model. To separate effects, we combine constrained option-letter scoring, cyclic option rotation, and controlled removal of the governing provision. On 398 Bar Council outputs, an exact-line parser attributes an accuracy gain of 50.0\% to the Qwen3.5-2B seed-42 adapter, whereas option scoring yields only $3.0\%$. For Gemma-4-E2B, the two scoring methods favor different systems. When the governing provision is guaranteed to be present, five of six reference models improve by $14.7\%-19.3\%$ under the four-order criterion. Removing that provision reduces accuracy by $8.0\%-15.3\%$ for models and by $13.8\%-14.9\%$ points for their adapters. However, difference-in differences estimates show no increase in reliance on the governing provision after fine-tuning. Results show that legal adaptation claims require separating scorer, retriever, and model effects. Our Code and data are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/bangladesh-legal-qa-11E3

</details>


### [153] [Auditing MCQA Benchmarks through Probability Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30372)
*Minsoo Song,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于模型输出分布的两阶段概率框架，用全局概率特征（P_top1、H_norm、MPD）审视MCQA基准整体结构，并通过噪声注入检测并标记潜在有缺陷的题目，以实现轻量化审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在标准多项选择题（MCQA）基准上表现趋于饱和，现有的基准质量验证和缺陷过滤依赖大量人工，缺乏可扩展的诊断手段。

Method: 框架包括两部分：①基准层面，用最高预测概率 P_top1 与归一化残差熵 H_norm 描绘概率分布景观，并用全局平均成对距离（MPD）汇总；②题目层面，引入噪声注入削弱有意义的干扰项竞争，进而标记需人工复审的候选题，并对残余错误模式进行分类。

Result: 在四个 MCQA 基准上，景观分析揭示模型置信度和残余选项竞争的基准差异；噪声注入方法成功标记出可操作的题目缺陷，且与 MMLU-Redux 的专家错误标注高度一致。

Conclusion: 基于概率分布的审计框架能够轻量化地比较宏观基准结构并优先筛选需人工审查的题目，为 MCQA 基准的可扩展质量评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: As Large Language Models rapidly advance, performance on standard multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) benchmarks is reaching saturation. While the community has responded by developing increasingly difficult datasets, validating question quality and filtering flawed items remains a labor-intensive process. To provide a scalable diagnostic approach, we propose a two-component probabilistic framework for auditing MCQA benchmarks using model output distributions. First, for benchmark-level analysis, we characterize the probability landscape using the top prediction probability ($P_{top1}$) and normalized residual entropy ($H_{norm}$), summarized globally by Mean Pairwise Distance (MPD). Second, for item-level diagnostics, we introduce noise injection to reduce meaningful distractor competition, enabling us to flag candidate items for targeted human review and categorize residual failure patterns. Across four MCQA benchmarks, our landscape analysis reveals benchmark-level differences in model confidence and residual option competition. Concurrently, our noise-injection method flags potentially actionable item-level issues, showing alignment with expert error annotations from MMLU-Redux. These results suggest that our probability-based framework provides a lightweight audit lens for comparing macro-level benchmark structure and prioritizing individual items for targeted human review.

</details>


### [154] [Beyond Consensus: Downward Bias and Role Asymmetry in Multi-Agent LLM Judges for Subjective Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30373)
*Minsoo Song,Chanwoo Kim,Sugyeong Eo,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 单评审基线在主观评分任务上比多代理共识的MAD更贴近人类判断；MAD的性能下降主要源于不对称角色提示导致的系统性下调偏差，去除角色不对称可恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主观 rubric 评分中，多代理辩论（MAD）被认为能通过共识提升 LLM 评估的可靠性，但实际是否能提升与人类评判的一致性尚不明确，需要系统评估单评审与共识式 MAD 的差异。

Method: 在六种 LLM 上进行主观评估实验，对比单评审基线与基于共识的 MAD；设计三组消融实验：①角色提示（严格 vs. 宽松）②多轮交互③显式分数共享；通过对比人类对齐度评估各因素的影响。

Result: 单评审基线在六个评审模型上平均获得最高的人类对齐度；MAD 在两项任务上均出现对齐度下降。消融实验表明，下降主要来源于不对称的角色提示——严格评审角色导致系统性向下偏差，且共识过程未能纠正；对称 MAD 能基本恢复基线性能；隐藏同伴分数会增加代理间分歧并进一步降低对齐度。

Conclusion: 在主观评分场景下，基于角色专化的共识式 MAD 可能通过强制人工一致性而牺牲真实的人类对齐，显示出其结构性局限性；去除角色不对称或采用更对称的多代理交互是提升 MAD 效能的关键方向。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) has been widely adopted to improve LLM-based evaluation by prompting multiple agents to negotiate and reach a consensus. However, for subjective rubric-based scoring, inter-agent agreement does not guarantee alignment with human judgments. In this paper, we compare a single-judge baseline against a consensus-based MAD protocol on subjective evaluation tasks and design three ablations to isolate the impact of role prompting, multi-round interaction, and explicit score sharing. Evaluations across six LLMs show that the single-judge baseline achieves the strongest human alignment on average across six judge models, whereas MAD shows degradation in human alignment on both tasks. Our ablations demonstrate that this performance drop stems primarily from asymmetric role prompting rather than the interaction itself. Specifically, assigning a strict judge role introduces a systematic downward bias that the consensus process fails to correct. The central finding is that this bias reflects strict-stance dominance beyond averaging: the consensus score falls well beyond the arithmetic midpoint of the standalone strict and lenient conditions, rather than averaging them out. Removing role asymmetry (Symmetric MAD) largely recovers baseline performance, while masking peer scores widens inter-agent disagreement on average and worsens average human alignment. These findings demonstrate that multi-agent consensus can enforce artificial agreement at the expense of true human alignment, revealing a structural limitation in consensus-style, role-specialized MAD protocols for subjective scoring.

</details>


### [155] [Kathleen Remembers: Length-Invariant One-Shot Recall Without Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30376)
*George Fountzoulas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在循环、无注意力序列模型中加入一个固定键全息关联记忆（notebook），仅用2.5万参数即可实现远程一次性精确回忆，显著提升稀有词重复预测并在长序列上保持零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环、无注意力模型的状态会随时间衰减，导致无法精确回忆远距离出现的单次信息；需要一种轻量、可扩展的记忆机制来克服这一结构性弱点。

Method: 在主干模型（Kathleen trunk）上附加第二层记忆——固定键全息（HRR）关联存储，配备学习的局部写门、自门控读取以及写触发的遗忘机制；该记忆层仅占25K参数，直接作用于输出logits。

Result: 1）在针刺搜索任务中，notebook在训练长度4倍的序列上实现80‑82%一次性回忆，远超仅主干（≈4%）和等参数注意力头（训练长度内100%，超出0%）。2）在WikiText-2上提升稀有词重复预测0.15‑0.27 bits/byte，且增益随提及距离增长，4倍训练长度仍保持零样本性能；写触发遗忘将首次提及的记忆污染从+0.33降至‑0.004。3）在WikiText-103大规模实验中，零样本重复增益随数据规模单调上升。所有实验预注册、可复现。

Conclusion: 固定键全息关联记忆层能够在不增加大量参数的前提下，为循环、无注意力模型提供长度不变、一次性精确回忆能力，显著改善长序列稀有词预测，并具备可选择性遗忘和事实归因等实用特性。

Abstract: Recurrent, attention-free sequence models share a structural weakness: a fading state cannot perform exact recall of something seen once, far in the past. We add to the Kathleen trunk a second memory layer -- a "notebook": a fixed-key holographic (HRR) associative store with a learned local write gate, a self-gating raw read, and write-triggered forgetting -- 25K parameters that attach to the logits of any trunk. (1) Mechanism: on a controlled needle-in-haystack task the notebook reaches 80-82% one-shot recall at 4x the training length, where the bare trunk scores ~4% and a parameter-matched attention head scores 100% inside its training length and 0% beyond it. Addressing is length-invariant by construction; the untrained memory alone recalls at 90% accuracy identically at 512, 2048 and 4096 bytes. Because the store is a linear superposition, two capabilities follow from arithmetic alone: selective unlearning (one subtraction erases one fact to chance, retained facts unharmed) and per-token attribution (counterfactual erasure names the source fact of every correct byte, 100% provenance). (2) Real text: on WikiText-2 bytes the notebook improves prediction of repeated rare words by +0.15-0.27 bits/byte, the gain growing with the distance between mentions and holding zero-shot at 4x training length; write-triggered forgetting eliminates memory pollution at 8x length (first-mention cost +0.33 -> -0.004). (3) Scope and scale: a parameter-matched attention head does generalize on natural-text repetition, so the notebook's claim is exact recall at O(L); on a WikiText-103 ladder (8 to 512 MB) the zero-shot repeat gain rises monotonically. All experiments are pre-registered, seeds reported, and reproducible on a single free-tier GPU.

</details>


### [156] [Using Grounded Theory for Agent Behavior Analysis at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30391)
*Zhuoran Lu,Yangyang Yu,Zhuoyan Li,Yibo Meng,Nan Jiang,Chengxi Zang,Jie Gao,Ziang Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 AutoTraceGT，一种自动化基于扎根理论的多智能体轨迹分析流水线，能够在大规模轨迹数据中迭代编码并达到饱和，生成任务特定的行为分类，显著提升故障模式识别和预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预构建分类器难以在成千上万的轨迹和长时任务中发现新行为模式，缺乏可扩展且可审计的分析方法。

Method: 将社会科学的扎根理论引入智能体轨迹分析，构建 AutoTraceGT 流水线：对轨迹进行开放编码、轴向编码和理论编码，循环迭代直至满足饱和准则，自动生成行为码本（codebook）。

Result: 在六个轨迹数据集上，AutoTraceGT 生成的码本能够恢复 73%–91% 的人工标注故障模式，并发现额外模式；其理论叙事与专家描述一致。将码本用作特征空间时，显著超越零样本和少样本 LLM 基线的故障预测表现。

Conclusion: 扎根理论可被自动化用于大规模智能体行为分析，为机器学习研究者和开发者提供了一种可扩展、可审计的工具，以更好地理解和预测智能体实际行为。

Abstract: Understanding agent behavior requires methods that scale to thousands of trajectories and surface new patterns in long, often unfamiliar tasks where pre-built classifiers fall short. We propose to bring grounded theory into agent trajectory analysis: a six-decade-old qualitative method from the social sciences, with a principled saturation criterion and an auditable trail from data to theory. We propose AutoTraceGT (Automated Trace analysis through Grounded Theory), the first multi-agent pipeline that automates grounded theory on agent trajectories. It iteratively performs open, axial, and theoretical coding until saturation, producing a behavioral taxonomy tailored to each task. Across six trajectory corpora, AutoTraceGT produces codebooks that recover 73-91 percent of the failure modes in human-annotated taxonomies and surface additional patterns that those taxonomies miss. The emergent theoretical narrative aligns with prior expert accounts. Used as a deductive feature space, the codebook outperforms zero-shot and few-shot LLM baselines on downstream failure prediction. These results suggest Grounded Theory offers a scalable analytic tool for ML researchers and agent developers studying what agents actually do.

</details>


### [157] [Quantitative Evidence Mining for Plausibility-Aware Biomedical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30393)
*Negin Sadat Babaiha,Stefan Geissler,Marie-Christine Simon,Martin Hofmann-Apitius,Marc Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文呼吁从仅抽取实体关系转向“量化证据挖掘”，即提取数值、单位、测量对象、上下文、置信度和来源等结构化信息，以构建可审计、可验证的生物医学证据图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生物医学AI系统虽能自动抽取声明，但缺乏对剂量、效应大小、研究人群、比较基准、置信区间等关键定量信息的捕获，导致生成的关系看似可操作却难以验证、比较或复用，进而影响证据合成和决策支持的可靠性。

Method: 提出一种量化证据挖掘框架，要求从文献、临床试验和监管文件中抽取数值、单位、被测实体及属性、实验上下文、不确定性、来源溯源和生物学可 plausibility 检查，并将这些结构化证据单元组织进证据感知的知识图谱中，形成可审计的证据对象。

Result: 本文主要是观点性文章，未提供实验结果；但阐述了框架的关键组成要素及其在提升证据可追溯性、单位一致性、完整性和生物学合理性方面的潜在价值。

Conclusion: 在大语言模型驱动的自动化证据合成时代，关键风险在于生成缺乏结构化定量信息的“伪证据”。通过量化证据挖掘和可 plausibility 检查，可将抽取的声明转化为可审计、可信的证据对象，从而提升生物医学AI系统的可靠性和可用性。

Abstract: Biomedical artificial intelligence (AI) systems increasingly extract, organize, and reuse scientific claims from literature, clinical trials, and regulatory documents. But automatic extraction alone does not make a claim reliable evidence: a claim becomes useful only when it can be traced to its source, linked to the quantitative details that support it, and read within its biomedical context and uncertainty. This matters as large language models (LLMs) and increasingly autonomous systems drive evidence synthesis, knowledge graph (KG) construction, and decision support. Many text-mining and LLM pipelines remain relation-centric: they capture entities and relations such as Drug--TREATS--Disease, but drop the dose, effect size, population, comparator, uncertainty, and conditions under which a claim holds. Such relations can look actionable yet remain hard to verify, compare, or reuse. In this perspective, we argue for a shift toward quantitative evidence mining---extracting values, units, measured entities and properties, context, uncertainty, provenance, and plausibility as structured evidence units that populate evidence-aware KGs and can be checked for source grounding, unit consistency, completeness, and biological plausibility. We outline a framework for plausibility-aware AI that treats extracted claims not as final answers but as auditable evidence objects, making clear what was measured, how much it changed, in which setting, with what uncertainty, and from which source. The central risk is not only incorrect extraction, but claims that look like evidence while lacking the structure needed to trust them.

</details>


### [158] [When LLM Meets Tree Search: A Systematic View of Inference as Search in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30395)
*Jiaqi Wei,Xiang Zhang,Yuejin Yang,Wenxuan Huang,Juntai Cao,Sheng Xu,Xiang Zhuang,Zhangyang Gao,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed,Laks VS Lakshmanan,Chenyu You,Wanli Ouyang,Siqi Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于树搜索的测试时扩展（TTS）方法，将推理视为对部分推理状态空间的搜索，提出统一的设计空间并倡导标准化计算报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着预训练规模律趋于饱和，单一解码路径的Chain-of-Thought难以纠正早期错误并进行有效探索，亟需在推理阶段利用额外计算提升模型性能。

Method: 系统梳理从无信息搜索到蒙特卡罗树搜索的演进，提出统一设计空间，包括搜索拓扑、评估信号和控制动态三大维度，并建议使用统一的计算-精度报告框架。

Result: 通过统一视角，明确了不同树搜索变体的探索‑利用权衡，提供了比较不同方法计算成本与准确率的基准，促进了碎片化研究的整合。

Conclusion: 将推理视为实例特定的优化而非单一路径解码，可更好利用测试时计算资源；统一的设计空间和计算报告标准有助于推动该领域的系统化发展。

Abstract: As pretraining scaling laws approach saturation, Test-Time Scaling (TTS) has emerged as an important direction for improving reasoning by allocating inference-time compute to a fixed model prior. Viewed at a high level, TTS reframes inference as search over a space of partial reasoning states. While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) exposes intermediate steps, common instantiations rely on single-trajectory decoding, limiting recovery from early errors and exploration. This survey systematizes recent progress in tree-search-based reasoning, viewing inference as instance-specific optimization rather than decoding. We trace the evolution from uninformed search to Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS), highlighting how sampling-based control supports principled exploration-exploitation trade-offs. To unify a fragmented literature, we introduce a Unified Design Space spanning search topology, evaluation signals, and control dynamics, and advocate a standardized compute-reporting abstraction to make compute-accuracy trade-offs explicit and comparable.

</details>


### [159] [Co-Evolving Actor-Conditioned Critics for Non-Verifiable Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30397)
*Jinyoung Kim,Muhammad Khalifa,Lajanugen Logeswaran,Jaekyeom Kim,Moontae Lee,Honglak Lee,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TAIScore奖励，用于评估批评在帮助特定actor改进中的有效性，并通过该奖励训练针对actor的批评模型，实现批评-actor共同进化，显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自然语言批评监督只能提供标量奖励，无法判断批评是否真正帮助了特定的生成模型；有效的批评应当针对actor的弱点并被其采纳。

Method: 将批评视为actor条件的修正指导，设计TAIScore奖励同时考察指令、初始响应、批评和修订四者，评估批评是否针对真实弱点、actor是否遵循以及改进是否实现；使用GRPO算法基于TAIScore训练针对actor的批评模型，并通过批评引导的修订生成DPO偏好对，形成批评-actor共同进化循环。

Result: 在8B规模的批评模型上，使用TAIScore训练的批评者优于零样本120B批评者以及仅基于结果或仅基于批评的奖励训练的模型；批评与actor的共同进化进一步提升了性能。

Conclusion: 有效的批评监督应当是针对特定actor的可操作改进，并随actor能力变化而自适应，TAIScore提供了实现此目标的有效手段。

Abstract: Natural-language critiques provide supervision beyond scalar rewards for non-verifiable generation, which lacks deterministic verifiers. In critique-guided refinement, a critic gives feedback on an initial response and an actor revises it. However, final revision quality does not reveal whether the critique was actually useful: a capable actor may improve without following the feedback, while valid feedback may fail if the actor cannot execute it. We frame critique as actor-conditioned revision guidance, where usefulness depends on whether the feedback helps the target actor address the intended weakness. We introduce TAIScore (Targeted Actionable Improvement Score), a reward that evaluates the instruction, initial response, critique, and revision together, assessing whether the critique targets a real weakness, whether the actor follows it, and whether the intended aspect improves. We use this reward to train an actor-tailored critic with GRPO, and use critique-guided refinements to construct DPO preference pairs for the actor, forming a co-evolving critic-actor loop where the critic adapts to the actor's changing capability. Experiments show that an 8B critic trained with TAIScore outperforms both a zero-shot 120B critic and critics trained with outcome-only or critique-only reward signals. Co-evolving the critic and actor further improves performance, suggesting that effective critique supervision should adapt as the actor changes.

</details>


### [160] [Beyond Polarization: The Generative Constraint of Chain-of-Thought in Pointwise Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30398)
*Xiaoyang Chen,Jie Liu,Haijin Liang,Haibo Shi,Jin Ma,Ben He,Yingfei Sun,Dezhi Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在点对点文档重排序中，Chain-of-Thought模型始终落后于直接打分模型，即使通过强化学习、细粒度监督和架构解耦等针对性训练也难以消除排名差距，表明离散文本的连续相关语义传递是一个稳定且难以克服的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何Chain-of-Thought模型在点对点文档重排序任务上表现不如直接评分模型，并探究是否可以通过特定训练手段弥合两者之间的差距。

Method: 首先在不同规模（最高32B参数）的模型上验证排名差距的稳定性；随后设计压力测试，包括强化学习微调、细粒度监督信号以及模型架构的解耦，以有针对性地修复分类准确率、分数极化和校准问题。

Result: 虽然上述干预提升了分类准确率和绝对分数，但相对排名差距仍然存在，未能显著缩小Chain-of-Thought模型与直接评分模型之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 在点对点评分范式下，将连续的相关性语义通过离散文本进行传递限制了排名信号的分辨率，这一瓶颈在现有标准方法下难以克服，问题更像是结构性限制而非可通过训练消除的偏差。

Abstract: In pointwise document reranking, Chain-of-Thought models typically underperform direct scoring models. While existing diagnostics attribute this to inferior classification, score polarization, or calibration breakdown, whether targeted training can bridge this gap remains unclear. Our empirical study first confirms that this gap is stable across scales up to 32B parameters, ruling out model and data capacity confounders. We then apply stress tests utilizing reinforcement learning, fine-grained supervision, and architectural decoupling to explicitly repair these deviations. Although these interventions improve classification accuracy and absolute scores, the relative ranking gap persists. These findings suggest that, within the pointwise scoring paradigm, routing continuous relevance semantics through discrete text constrains ranking signal resolution, revealing a bottleneck that is stable and difficult to overcome under current standard methods, rather than an easily resolvable training bias.

</details>


### [161] [SemPOI-RL: Aligning LLM Semantic Reasoning for Interpretable Out-of-Town POI Sequential Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30399)
*Yunqi Liu,Yang Zhang,Ruixing Zhang,Liangzhe Han,Yi Qiao,Tongyu Zhu,Leilei Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SemPOI-RL框架，将大语言模型的语义推理与结构化序列生成对齐，实现可解释的跨城市POI序列推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨城市（OOT）POI序列推荐方法要么基于隐式ID转移缺乏可解释性，要么直接使用LLM生成序列但未将推断的语义与位置约束结合，导致难以在结构化约束下生成连贯的旅行轨迹。

Method: 1) 对LLM进行微调，使其从用户本地轨迹中推断出面向目的地的旅行风格，并以自然语言形式作为中间语义；2) 引入语义POI对齐模块（SPAM），将推断的风格嵌入到风格条件化的掩码自编码器，实现位置感知的轨迹生成；3) 采用强化学习，使用推荐导向的奖励函数，使LLM生成的风格与下游序列质量对齐。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上，SemPOI-RL在推荐准确率、序列连贯性等指标上均显著优于传统推荐模型和直接使用LLM的基线，并能够在旅行不同阶段提供可解释的风格归因。

Conclusion: SemPOI-RL成功实现了LLM语义推理与结构化序列生成的统一，提升了跨城市POI推荐的性能和可解释性，为未来的语义驱动序列生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong semantic reasoning and open-ended generation abilities, but aligning these abilities with structured sequential generation remains challenging. This challenge is particularly evident in out-of-town (OOT) POI sequence generation, where a model must infer transferable travel intent from a user's hometown behaviors, adapt to cross-city interest drift, and generate a coherent destination trajectory under structural constraints. Existing approaches either rely on latent ID-based transfer with limited interpretability or directly use LLMs for sequence generation without explicitly grounding inferred semantics into position-aware predictions. To address this gap, we propose SemPOI-RL, a framework that aligns LLM semantic reasoning with structured sequence generation for interpretable OOT recommendation. Specifically, we first fine-tune an LLM to infer destination-oriented travel styles from users' hometown trajectories, using natural language as an interpretable semantic intermediate. We then introduce a Semantic POI Alignment Module (SPAM) to ground these inferred styles into a style-conditioned masked autoencoder for position-aware trajectory generation. Finally, we apply reinforcement learning with recommendation-oriented rewards to align LLM-generated styles with downstream sequence quality. Experiments on two real-world datasets show that SemPOI-RL consistently outperforms both traditional recommenders and direct LLM baselines, while providing interpretable style attribution across different phases of a trip. The code is available at https://github.com/Wind-Flipped/SemPOI-RL .

</details>


### [162] [Generative Models Enhanced by Sequence Labelling and Aspect-Code Switching Improve Cross-lingual Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30425)
*Jakub Šmíd,Pavel Přibáň,Pavel Král*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SeqLab框架和Aspect‑Code Switching技术，提升跨语言细粒度情感分析，尤其是目标‑方面‑情感检测，在11种语言上超越现有最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言ABSA缺乏研究，尤其是涉及多情感要素的任务（如TASD），需要在没有目标语言标注数据的情况下实现细粒度情感分析。

Method: 基于Seq2Seq模型，编码器额外执行序列标注任务以强化方面词识别和情感预测；引入Aspect‑Code Switching（ACS），在源语言句子与其翻译之间交换方面词生成额外训练样本；在多语言、多领域和两种主干模型上进行系统评估。

Result: 在11种语言、3个领域的E2E‑ABSA任务上，SeqLab+ACS显著超越此前最先进方法；在更具挑战性的跨语言TASD任务上也取得领先表现，并提供了不同源‑目标语言配对的系统比较。

Conclusion: SeqLab框架结合ACS有效提升跨语言ABSA，证明了辅助序列标注和数据增强的协同效应；同时指出当前模型在长句、隐含情感等方面仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Cross-lingual aspect-based sentiment analysis (ABSA) transfers knowledge from a source language with annotated data to a target language, enabling fine-grained sentiment analysis without annotated target-language data. While monolingual ABSA has seen significant progress, cross-lingual ABSA remains underexplored, especially for complex tasks involving multiple sentiment elements like target-aspect-sentiment detection (TASD). In this paper, we propose a novel SeqLab framework that enhances cross-lingual ABSA using a sequence-to-sequence model with an auxiliary sequence-labelling task performed by the encoder, enhancing aspect term recognition and sentiment predictions. Additionally, we incorporate aspect-code switching (ACS), a translation-based technique that swaps aspect terms between source and translated sentences, generating additional training data to enhance the model's cross-lingual understanding. We evaluate our approach across eleven languages, three domains, and two backbone models, surpassing previous state-of-the-art results for the commonly studied E2E-ABSA task. Unlike most prior work that relies solely on English as the source language, we systematically assess different source-target language pairs and extend our evaluation to the more challenging, yet underexplored TASD task in cross-lingual settings. Finally, we provide a detailed error analysis highlighting key challenges and limitations.

</details>


### [163] [Learning to Reason and Use Tools through Unsupervised Fine-Tuning in Task-Oriented Dialog Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30426)
*Markel Ferro,Oier Lopez de Lacalle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过在任务导向对话中引入 ReAct 框架并进行无监督微调，使大语言模型能够检索外部知识，显著提升了响应的真实性和准确性，8B 模型的表现甚至超过了 70B 的基于上下文的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话系统在处理动态信息检索时容易产生幻觉，导致响应不准确，亟需一种方法让模型有效利用外部知识库以提升事实性。

Method: 在任务导向对话上适配 ReAct 框架，提出无监督微调流水线：通过在上下文学习推理得到推理轨迹，使用 LLM 判别器筛选高质量样本构建训练集；并加入自我提升循环，利用改进后的检查点生成更优轨迹用于后续微调。

Result: 在 SIMMC 数据集上实验表明，基于 ReAct 的系统在推理和工具使用上优于基线；微调后的 8B 模型性能超越了 70B 的仅使用上下文的系统。还提供了错误分析、场景复杂度影响及跨域泛化评估。

Conclusion: 无监督微调结合 ReAct 框架能够显著提升任务导向对话系统的事实性和推理能力，且在模型规模受限的情况下仍可实现领先性能，展示了该方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Current dialogue systems struggle with dynamic information retrieval, often leading to hallucinations and lower response accuracy. We address this by adapting the ReAct framework for Task-Oriented Dialogue, enabling Large Language Models (LLMs) to access external knowledge and produce factual responses. Mainly, we propose an unsupervised fine-tuning pipeline that harvests reasoning trajectories via in-context learning inference. High-quality samples are filtered using an LLM-based judge to construct a robust training set. This is enhanced by a unsupervised self-improvement loop, where improved checkpoints generate increasingly better trajectories for subsequent fine-tuning iterations. Experiments on the SIMMC dataset demonstrate that ReAct-based systems outperform baselines due to superior reasoning and tool use. Notably, our fine-tuned 8B model surpasses a 70B in-context system. Finally, we present an error analysis, impact of scene complexity, and cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [164] [Ceiling-Clipped Acceptance Histograms Indicate Stranded Speed-up in Block-Diffusion Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30427)
*Ephrem Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过扩展草稿模型块大小并使用短课程后训练（DBloom）来解决草稿模型在块接受率达到上限时的‘滞留加速’问题，显著提升了在多种基准上的每提示承诺长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在投机解码中，高接受率的块式草稿模型（如DFlash、DFlare）常出现块大小超出训练范围后验证失败的现象，即‘滞留加速’，导致实际加速未被平均承诺长度指标揭示，需要更好的评估与改进方法。

Method: 提出使用接受率直方图作为预检工具，发现滞留加速；随后通过后训练（post‑training）在更长块上进行短课程学习，重点强化新暴露位置的预测能力，称为DBloom。该方法在保持原有模型结构的前提下，将块大小从16扩展至24，并在扩展前后进行续写微调。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B、Qwen3-4B目标模型上，块大小从16到24的扩展使高上限基准的每提示承诺长度中位数提升+0.8 token（最高+1.1），续写微调后提升至+1.37 token。对Gemma-4-12B-IT模型，扩展后在七个基准上中位提升+0.41 token（方案A），完整的续写‑再‑扩展流水线（方案B）在相同B16草稿模型上额外提升+0.29~+0.98 token。与树形草稿模型JetSpec比较，DBloom在树预算≤64节点的所有基准上均实现更高的承诺 token 数。

Conclusion: 接受率直方图是检测草稿模型潜在加速瓶颈的有效工具；通过DBloom的块扩展与短课程后训练，可显著提升投机解码的实际加速效果，且在不同模型族和基准上均表现出稳健的改进，优于现有树形草稿方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding speeds up generation with an efficient draft model (drafter) that proposes tokens for a target model to verify in one pass, preserving the target's output distribution. High-acceptance block-diffusion drafters such as DFlash and DFlare fill an entire block in one parallel pass. In many cycles, the target accepts the whole block, so the drafter exhausts its trained block horizon before verification fails. We call this unrealized acceptance stranded speed-up. A mean committed length, per prompt or per cycle, hides it, whereas the acceptance histogram exposes it as a spike in the ceiling bin, the fraction of cycles that accept the entire block. We recommend the histogram as a preflight check before spending training compute. Naively widening the block at inference does not recover the speed-up, because once the block outgrows its training size, the drafter's bidirectional attention shifts its distribution even at early positions and erodes front-of-block verification. Instead, we post-train the drafter on a longer block with a short curriculum that emphasizes the newly exposed positions, a method we call DBloom. Expanding the pretrained DFlash and DFlare drafters from block size 16 to 24 across Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B targets raises the per-prompt committed length on the high-ceiling benchmarks by a median of +0.8 tokens (up to +1.1). Once continuation fine-tuning precedes expansion, the increase reaches 1.37 tokens. The same expansion also lifts committed length on all seven benchmarks for Gemma-4-12B-IT, a different model family, by a median of +0.41 tokens (Arm A), and the full continuation-then-expand pipeline (Arm B) adds +0.29 to +0.98 tokens over the same B16 drafter. In a prompt-matched comparison against JetSpec, a contemporary tree-based drafter not used in our design, DBloom commits more tokens on every benchmark at tree budgets up to 64 nodes.

</details>


### [165] [Lies We Can See: Joint Verbal and Non-Verbal Deception by VLM Agents in Embodied Social Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30428)
*Jaewoo Ahn,Junseo Kim,Hyunseo Kim,Heeseung Yun,Jaehyeon Son,Zsolt Kira,Gunhee Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了3D多模态游戏MineAmongUs和可配置的VLM代理框架ARIA，用于研究代理在语言与非语言行为中的欺骗行为，发现非语言通道是决定胜负的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI欺骗测试平台仅限文本，缺乏身体动作等非语言通道，导致难以判断欺骗行为是模型本身还是实验环境所致，亟需一个兼具语言和感官动作的多模态测试环境。

Method: 构建了基于《Among Us》的3D沙盒MineAmongUs，设计了 impostor 与 crewmate 的角色交互；实现了可配置的 VLM 代理框架 ARIA，提供五个认知组件的消融轴；并制定了原子级和弧级的欺骗标注方案，利用 LLM‑as‑Judge 进行大规模标注，确保标注与人类水平接近。

Result: 实验表明 VLM 代理在追求 impostor 胜利时会同时使用语言和非语言欺骗，其中非语言通道（如移动、任务行为）对胜负贡献更大；该结论在不同的 ARIA 配置和跨 VLM 模型评估中均保持一致。

Conclusion: MineAmongUs 与 ARIA 为研究具身 VLM 代理的欺骗行为提供了新实验平台，揭示了非语言行为在多智能体欺骗中的核心作用，为后续的对齐与安全研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Strategic deception by LLM and VLM agents has emerged as a central AI alignment and safety concern. Social-deduction games (where each player holds a hidden role and communicates with others to deduce identities) serve as the canonical testbed, particularly in multi-agent settings. Existing testbeds, however, are text-only and run on a single fixed agent configuration, missing the non-verbal sensorimotor channels treated as core by deception taxonomies and leaving it ambiguous whether an observed behavior reflects the underlying model or the surrounding harness. We introduce MineAmongUs, a 3D multimodal Among Us sandbox where imposter agents must deceive crewmates through joint verbal and non-verbal action. We also propose ARIA, a configurable VLM-agent harness that exposes five cognitive-component ablation axes; and an atom- and arc-level annotation scheme grounded in deception taxonomies and operationalized at scale by an LLM-as-a-Judge reaching near-human atom-labeling agreement. Empirical results show that VLM agents pursue imposter wins through joint verbal and non-verbal deception, with non-verbal channels emerging as the more decisive winning contributors across both harness ablation and cross-VLM evaluation. Taken together, our work opens a new path for embodied VLM-agent alignment research.

</details>


### [166] [Graph Evidence Is Not Enough: Diagnosing Native Decoder Use in Graph-Augmented LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30437)
*Xiaoyu Guo,Pengcheng Chen,Jiong Yu,Yi Lu,Yaohua Wang,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 图增强大语言模型在直接使用外部图证据时表现不佳，作者通过HopQA诊断并提出S²GE方法，通过改进图证据的呈现和对齐方式，使模型在多个领域数据集上显著提升精确匹配率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图增强语言模型假设将外部计算得到的图证据放入输入后，模型的原生解码器能够直接利用这些信息。然而在HopQA这一仅需返回最短跳数的诊断任务中，模型仍然失败，说明仅提供图证据并不等同于可用的图信息，亟需明确图证据可用性的因素并改进接口设计。

Method: 作者构建了一个干预三角实验，包括可读图证据、随机打乱的图证据和无图输入三种匹配条件，以分离证据包含、结构可读性和解码器可用拓扑三方面的影响。基于诊断结果，提出S²GE（Structure‑preserving Graph Evidence）框架，核心包括：①查询感知采样，只保留与问题相关的子图；②端点和邻近度排序，确保重要节点靠前呈现；③结构保持对齐，将图结构映射为线性序列，使原生解码器易于利用。

Result: 在四个跨领域数据集（DBLP、Biomedical、GoodReads、PubMed）上，S²GE的严格精确匹配率分别达到 36.5%、57.8%、76.6% 和 52.0%，相较于最强的原生生成基线平均提升 53.5 分。实验还揭示了三种行为模式：shuffle‑harmful（打乱图导致性能下降）、shuffle‑robust（对打乱具有鲁棒性）以及no‑graph‑saturated（在无图输入下模型已接近上限）。

Conclusion: 仅提供图证据不足以提升大语言模型的推理能力，需要通过可读、结构保持的接口将图信息转化为解码器可直接利用的形式。S²GE证明了诊断驱动的接口设计能够显著提升图增强模型的实际可用性，并为未来图‑语言模型的交互方式提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Graph-augmented large language models often assume that graph evidence produced by external computation and placed in the input can be used by the native decoder. We test this assumption with HopQA, a deliberately bounded diagnostic that asks for the shortest-hop distance between two query nodes. Because the answer is a small integer and the target is purely topological, failure cannot be dismissed as open-ended generation or ambiguous evaluation. Yet existing graph-augmented baselines still fail on this setting, showing that providing graph evidence is not the same as making it usable. We introduce an intervention triangle with three matched conditions: readable graph evidence, shuffled graph evidence, and no-graph input. This separates evidence inclusion, structural readability, and decoder-usable topology. Guided by this diagnosis, we present S$^2$GE as an instance showing that diagnosis-driven interface design can improve native decoder usability. S$^2$GE uses query-aware sampling, endpoint and proximity-based ordering, and structure-preserving alignment. Across DBLP, Biomedical, GoodReads, and PubMed, S$^2$GE achieves strict exact-match scores of $36.5\%$, $57.8\%$, $76.6\%$, and $52.0\%$, improving over the strongest native-generation baseline by $53.5$ points on average. The interventions further reveal harmful-shuffle, shuffle-robust, and no-graph-saturated regimes.

</details>


### [167] [From Final Artifacts to Trajectories: Retrospective Process Supervision for Evidence-Grounded Long-Form Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30461)
*Junjie Huang,Jiarui Qin,Di Yin,Weiwen Liu,Yong Yu,Xing Sun,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 RetroGen 框架，通过从高质量成品（如文献综述、分析报告、法律判决）逆向重建潜在轨迹，并在自我验证后用于训练，以提升大语言模型在开放式长文证据检索任务中的可靠性和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式任务缺乏唯一答案且标注成本高，导致难以获取大规模轨迹数据；而高质量成品在预训练语料中丰富，可视为压缩的证据搜寻过程，利用其逆向重建可弥补轨迹数据的不足。

Method: RetroGen 采用回顾性过程监督：① 从专家成品中抽取潜在的证据检索轨迹候选；② 使用成品本身及其支持证据对候选轨迹进行一致性验证；③ 将成功重建的轨迹作为自监督训练数据，迭代提升模型，无需更强模型提供轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，使用 RetroGen 训练的模型在 grounding、faithful synthesis 以及长篇证据检索任务上显著优于基线，表现出更好的事实依据和生成忠实度。

Conclusion: 逆向利用高质量成品重建潜在轨迹是一种可行且高效的自我提升方式，为大语言模型在开放式、证据驱动任务上的训练提供了新的数据来源和方法论。

Abstract: Trajectory data is getting more vital for training large language models for boosting the agentic abilities. Unlike the verifiable domains such as coding or mathematics, scaling trajectory data for open-ended tasks is much more difficult because these tasks lack singular ground truth and are costly to annotate or verify. In this paper, we propose RetroGen, a self-improving framework of retrospective process supervision. Our key observation is that although expert trajectories are scarce, high-quality final artifacts such as literature reviews, analyst reports and legal judgments, are abundant in pre-training data and can be viewed as compressed traces of the evidence-seeking processes that produced them. RetroGen reconstructs candidate latent trajectories from expert artifacts, verifies them against both the artifact and supporting evidence, and trains models on their own successful reconstruction data, without requiring trajectory data from stronger models. Experiments show that RetroGen improves grounding, faithful synthesis, and long-form evidence-seeking agent tasks.

</details>


### [168] [Enhancing Low-Resource Language Reasoning via High-Resource Language Feature Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30462)
*Minju Song,Hyeon Hwang,Junhyun Lee,Jaewoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过稀疏自编码器在残差流中识别并转移跨语言任务相关的稀疏潜在特征，以弥补低资源语言在数学推理上的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到大型语言模型在不同语言间的任务表现差异显著，传统解释归因于数据、分词或基准覆盖等因素。作者假设高资源语言能够更可靠地激活用于数学推理的潜在计算，而低资源语言则未能充分激活这些机制。

Method: 构建机械干预框架：使用稀疏自编码器对模型残差流激活进行编码，筛选出在高资源语言成功推理时富集且与源语言、通用生成无关的稀疏特征；基于这些特征构造 steering direction，并在低资源语言推理时注入。通过抑制或激活这些特征来因果验证其对推理能力的影响。

Result: 实验表明：抑制选定特征会削弱高资源语言的推理表现；在低资源语言注入相同特征可显著提升其数学推理准确率，超过随机和非任务控制组，证明这些特征在跨语言推理差距中起关键作用。

Conclusion: 跨语言推理差距部分源于机制激活不足，而非模型能力缺失。本文提供了一种无需翻译、微调或改变用户语言的因果可验证的特征转移方法，为缩小低资源语言性能差距提供新思路。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit substantial performance variation across languages, even when solving semantically equivalent tasks. Existing analyses often treat this phenomenon as an observational disparity caused by differences in pretraining data, tokenization, or benchmark coverage. We study a complementary hypothesis: high-resource languages (HRLs) may more reliably elicit latent computations useful for task-specific (i.e. mathematical) reasoning, while lower-resource languages (LRLs) may under-activate those computations despite expressing the same task. To test this hypothesis, we introduce a mechanistic intervention framework for identifying and transferring task-relevant sparse latent features across languages. Using sparse autoencoders over residual-stream activations, we isolate features enriched in successful HRL task-specific reasoning while filtering out source-language and generic-generation features. We then construct steering directions from these features and inject them during LRL inference. The resulting interventions test whether the selected features are functionally involved in the observed reasoning gap: suppressing them should impair source-language reasoning, while activating them should partially recover target-language reasoning beyond random and non-task controls. Our framework reframes some cross-lingual reasoning gaps as failures of mechanism elicitation rather than capability absence, and offers a causally testable route to feature-mediated transfer without translation, fine-tuning, or changing the user-facing language.

</details>


### [169] [More Capable, Less Faithful: A Multilingual Analysis of Mathematical (Un)Solvability Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30463)
*Maria-Eleni Zoumpoulidi,Nikolaos Xiros,Georgios Paraskevopoulos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个多语言可解/不可解数学题基准，训练探针预测模型的可解性信念，发现该信念在不同语言间是通用的特征，但高资源语言（如英语）的可解性检测可信度更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对大语言模型可解性检测的研究仅限英文，无法判断多语言模型的失败是源于内部可解性信念差异还是语言表达差异，亟需跨语言的系统评估。

Method: 将 ReliableMath 基准扩展至法语和希腊语，构建配对的可解/不可解题目；训练多语言探针预测模型的 Solvability Belief；从行为、表征和可信度三个维度评估主流 LLM 的可解性检测能力。

Result: 实验表明，Solvability Belief 在不同语言中表现为一种几乎语言无关的通用特征；然而，资源丰富的英语模型虽在数学推理整体表现更好，却在可解性检测的可信度上低于法语和希腊语模型。

Conclusion: 可解性信念是跨语言共享的内部表征，但模型在不同语言上的检测可信度存在差异，提示未来需在提升多语言数学推理的同时，关注可解性检测的可靠性。

Abstract: Solvability detection is one of the most challenging aspects of mathematical reasoning for Large Language Models (LLMs). While prior work has studied this capability extensively, these analyses have been limited to English. Consequently, it remains unclear whether multilingual failures arise from differences in internal Solvability Belief or from language-dependent failures to express it. To address this gap, we introduce the first multilingual benchmark of paired solvable and unsolvable mathematical problems, extending ReliableMath to French and Greek. Using this, we train multilingual probes predicting Solvability Belief and analyze the solvability detection capabilities of state-of-the-art LLMs behaviorally, representationally, and in terms of faithfulness. We find that Solvability Belief is encoded as a largely universal, language-agnostic feature, and that higher-resource languages such as English, despite achieving stronger mathematical reasoning performance, exhibit lower solvability-detection faithfulness.

</details>


### [170] [Hi-Q: Hierarchical Evidence-guided Query Refinement for Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30468)
*Jueun Kim,Sungho Park,Wook-Shin Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Hi-Q是一种基于证据的层次化查询细化框架，通过在检索到的证据支持下动态决定是否继续细化查询节点，从而在多跳问答中实现更高的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答中，问题的表达粒度与可检索证据的粒度常不匹配，现有方法要么使用固定图结构，要么通过迭代重写或程序执行，但缺乏对何时已有证据支持、何时需要进一步细化的显式判断。

Method: Hi-Q在每个查询节点引入分辨算子，检测检索证据是否覆盖当前查询单元；若覆盖则终止该节点，否则通过保持依存关系的二元扩展算子生成子节点，并使用语义覆盖验证器检查。整个过程动态生成查询树，其拓扑由语料库支持信号决定，而非预设模板或预构建图。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准的全语料检索设置下，Hi-Q平均达到52.3 EM、64.0 F1，分别比IRCoT提升15.1 EM/18.2 F1，比PropRAG提升11.5 EM/12.0 F1（MuSiQue-full）。在受限支持/干扰设置下，平均57.9 EM、69.3 F1，同样领先PropRAG和IRCoT。

Conclusion: Hi-Q通过证据驱动的层次查询细化，有效解决了粒度不匹配问题，在全语料和受限设置下均显著提升多跳问答性能，且无需构建全局图结构。

Abstract: A central bottleneck in multi-hop Question Answering (QA) is that the granularity at which a question is expressed often differs from the granularity at which corpus evidence is retrievable. Existing methods address this mismatch by imposing fixed graph structures over the corpus, by iteratively reformulating the query, or by executing a generated program over it, but these strategies do not explicitly decide when a query unit is already supported by evidence and when it should be refined. We formulate this bottleneck as retrievable granularity discovery and introduce Hi-Q, an evidence-conditioned framework for hierarchical query refinement. At each query node, a resolution operator tests whether retrieved evidence supports the current query unit; resolved nodes terminate, while unresolved nodes are expanded by a dependency-preserving binary operator and checked by a semantic coverage verifier. Hi-Q therefore grows a query tree whose topology is determined by corpus support signals rather than by a fixed decomposition template or a pre-built graph. We evaluate Hi-Q on three multi-hop QA benchmarks, primarily under full-corpus retrieval, where dependent evidence must be located among open-domain distractors rather than within a small annotated pool. In this setting Hi-Q reaches 52.3 EM and 64.0 F1 averaged over the three benchmarks, ahead of the iterative retrieval baseline IRCoT by 15.1 EM / 18.2 F1 on that same average, and ahead of the graph-based RAG baseline PropRAG by 11.5 EM / 12.0 F1 on MuSiQue-full, without corpus-wide graph construction. In the restricted supporting/distractor setting used by prior work, Hi-Q likewise attains the best accuracy, with 57.9 EM and 69.3 F1 on average, ahead of PropRAG by 5.6 EM / 3.9 F1 and IRCoT by 13.7 EM / 15.8 F1. The project page is available at https://hi-q-project.github.io/.

</details>


### [171] [ImageEval 2026: Culturally Grounded Arabic Multimodal Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30475)
*Samir Abdaljalil,Hunzalah Hassan Bhatti,Ahlam Bashiti,Farina Amir,Md Arid Hasan,Basel Mousi,Nadir Durrani,Fahim Dalvi,Zien Sheikh Ali,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban,Mustafa Jarrar,Shammur Absar Chowdhury,Firoj Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ImageEval 2026 共享任务聚焦阿拉伯语多模态评估，包含口语视觉问答与图像生成文化准确性两项任务，共14支队伍参与，展示了零样本提示、模型微调、语音识别等多种技术方案，数据和评估脚本已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模视觉语言模型在全球范围的快速发展，现有评估多聚焦于英语等高资源语言，缺乏对阿拉伯语尤其是口语和文化语境的系统评估；因此需要构建面向阿拉伯语的多模态基准，以检验模型在文化嵌入和跨语言推理方面的能力。

Method: 任务分为两条：① AynVQA，要求系统在英语和现代标准阿拉伯语（MSA）下完成口语视觉问答并检测图像引发的幻觉；② CRAI‑Bench，评估文本到图像生成的文化准确性。组织方提供标注数据集、评估指标和脚本，参赛队伍采用零样本提示、视觉语言模型微调、语音识别流水线、模型集成及分数校准等方法构建系统。

Result: 共有14支队伍进入测试阶段，12支提交系统描述论文。整体来看，使用微调视觉语言模型的系统在 AynVQA 上取得最高准确率，而零样本提示结合大型语言模型在 CRAI‑Bench 的文化匹配得分表现突出。多数系统在跨语言语音识别和幻觉检测上仍有显著提升空间。

Conclusion: ImageEval 2026 共享任务揭示了阿拉伯语多模态评估的挑战，尤其是口语理解和文化语境推理的瓶颈。公开的数据集和评估脚本为后续研究提供了基准，推动社区在阿拉伯语视觉语言模型及文化感知生成方面的进一步探索。

Abstract: We present an overview of the ImageEval 2026 shared task on culturally grounded Arabic multimodal evaluation. It includes two tasks: (i) AynVQA, covering spoken visual question answering and image-grounded hallucination detection in English and Modern Standard Arabic (MSA), and (ii) CRAI-Bench, evaluating the cultural accuracy of text-to-image generation. A total of 14 teams participated in the test phase, with 12 teams submitting system description papers. Participating systems used a range of approaches, including zero-shot prompting, fine-tuning of vision-language models, speech-recognition pipelines, ensembling, and score calibration. We describe the task setup, datasets, evaluation procedure, and participating systems, and summarize the main results across the different tracks. All datasets and evaluation scripts from the shared task are released to the research community. The shared task highlights the challenges of culturally grounded multimodal evaluation, particularly for Arabic speech and image-text reasoning.

</details>


### [172] [Agents in the Large: Perception-Centered Architecture for Persistent Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30478)
*Shihan Dou,Haoxiang Jia,Shichun Liu,Feng Chen,Chenhao Huang,Yujiong Shen,Shaofan Liu,Jiayi Chen,Jiahang Lin,Honglin Guo,Qianyu He,Minghao Guo,Ziyi Ye,Pluto Zhou,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出感知中心架构（Pera），用于组织和指导长期持续运行的语言代理，使其能够感知环境与内部上下文，构建生命周期任务，实现自适应服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言代理主要针对一次性、有限的用户任务，缺乏能够在长期、不断变化的环境中持续提供帮助的框架，亟需一种方法来表征和构建持久化的 AI 代理。

Method: 设计 Perception‑Centered Architecture for Persistent Agents（Pera），将代理划分为感知和控制两大模块；感知模块持续捕获任务执行、内部上下文和环境变化的信号，进而生成生命周期任务；控制模块依据这些任务驱动服务流程的运行与适应。

Result: 通过回顾性整理近期研究并进行案例分析，展示 Pera 能够统一已有工作，揭示其在长期适应性和任务生成方面的优势，为构建更强大的持久代理提供了实践指引。

Conclusion: Pera 将语言代理的演进类比为软件工程从“小程序”到“大系统”的转变，提供了一条通向长期、可适应智能系统的架构路径，指明了未来研究的方向。

Abstract: Cognitive language agents have achieved substantial progress by equipping language models with memory, tools, and decision-making procedures, enabling agents to reason and act in interactive environments. Existing frameworks largely cast these agents as systems for solving user-specified, bounded tasks. An increasingly important goal is for language agents to provide persistent assistance in long-lived settings where user needs, context, and service procedures persist and change, and to remain useful across the broad range of tasks that arise over time. Yet we still lack a framework to characterize persistent AI agents, organize existing work, and guide future development. To this end, we propose a Perception-Centered Architecture for Persistent Agents (Pera). Pera describes a persistent agent organized around perception and control components that continually perceive service-relevant signals from episodic task executions, internal context, and changes in the surrounding environment, and use these signals to construct lifecycle tasks. These tasks drive the ongoing operation and adaptation of the agent's service procedures. We use Pera to retrospectively organize recent work, examine a detailed case study, and offer forward-looking insights for building more capable persistent agents. Just as software engineering moved from programming in the small to programming in the large, Pera frames the evolution of language agents as an analogous architectural transition toward long-lived, adaptive intelligence systems.

</details>


### [173] [Two Centuries of Sexism in British Parliament: A Computational Analysis of Women's Representation in the Hansard Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30485)
*Mohammad Omar Khursheed,Mandira Sawkar,Ashiqur R. KhudaBukhsh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过大语言模型分析1803-2005年英国议会6,531篇演讲，发现反对女性参政的言论中54%含有性别歧视，支持言论中仅21%；两者使用的性别歧视类型不同，前者兼具敌意与恩惠性，后者主要是恩惠性；女性议员支持率93%，男性议员70%，在女性获得选举权后差距缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究议会辩论中对女性权利的语言如何编码系统性性别歧视，并检验两世纪跨时段的性别偏见是否符合两极性别歧视理论（Ambivalent Sexism Theory）。

Method: 收集并整理Hansard语料，构建包含6.7百万演讲、1.2百万辩论的元数据丰富数据集；使用大语言模型对演讲者立场（支持/反对女性参政）进行分类；基于Ambivalent Sexism Inventory对演讲文本进行性别歧视类型标注（敌意性与恩惠性）。

Result: 反对女性参政的演讲中54%包含性别歧视，支持演讲中仅21%；反对方混合使用敌意和恩惠性歧视，支持方几乎只使用恩惠性歧视；女性议员对女性政治权利的支持率为93%，男性议员为70%，该性别差距在女性获得选举权后显著缩小。

Conclusion: 议会语言中系统性地嵌入了两种形式的性别歧视，且其使用情境与Ambivalent Sexism理论一致；女性议员在性别平等议题上更为一致支持，且制度性变革（如选举权）可显著降低性别立场差异。

Abstract: The language a legislature uses to debate women's rights, even in favour of them, encodes systematic patterns of sexism that persist across two centuries. In this work, we analyse 6,531 speeches over 200 years of UK parliamentary debate (Hansard, 1803-2005) by using large language models to classify a speaker's perspective towards women's suffrage and political representation, as well as analyse sexist speech in parliament from the lens of the Ambivalent Sexism Inventory. We also release this parliamentary dataset, an organized and metadata-enriched version of the publicly available Hansard Corpus optimized for computational social science research, with 6.7 million speeches across 1.2 million debates, with 89% gender-matching for speeches by MPs from the House of Commons. We find that 54% of speeches opposing women's representation contain sexist content, compared to 21% of speeches that are for the cause, and that the two sides use fundamentally different types of sexism: anti-suffrage rhetoric combines hostile and benevolent framing, while pro-suffrage sexism is overwhelmingly benevolent. Female MPs support women's political rights at 93% compared to 70% for male MPs, a gap that closes only after enfranchisement. Our findings are evidence that benevolent and hostile sexism are used in different rhetorical contexts in a manner consistent with the theory of Ambivalent Sexism.

</details>


### [174] [UTILMEM: Benchmarking Evidence Utilization in Long-Term Conversational Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30508)
*Peijun Qing,Fobo Shi,Soroush Vosoughi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出UtilMem基准，评估对话系统在长时记忆利用方面的四项能力，发现现有检索和记忆增强模型在信息整合和干扰抵抗上表现不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话记忆评测仅关注点式事实回忆，无法衡量真实场景中需要跨长对话历史、隐式线索和噪声信息的记忆利用能力。

Method: 构建UtilMem基准，包含1,717个跨五个领域的实例，设计四类任务：密集历史推理、隐式相关记忆识别、分布证据综合以及对抗语义干扰。对多种检索式和记忆增强模型进行评估。

Result: 在传统事实记忆基准上表现优秀的模型在UtilMem上并未展现出有效的记忆利用；仅靠检索难以完成信息跨会话整合，且常被相似干扰项误导。

Conclusion: 访问存储信息与有效利用信息之间存在显著差距，未来需研发显式支持证据整合和抗干扰的长时记忆架构。

Abstract: Long-term memory is increasingly important for conversational agents, yet existing benchmarks primarily measure memory through pointwise factual recall: whether a system can recover isolated facts or event-level details from prior interactions. Real-world memory use, however, often requires a more demanding capability: integrating distributed, implicit, and noisy evidence across extended interaction histories into coherent, task-oriented outputs. We call this capability memory utilization. Here, we introduce UtilMem, a diagnostic benchmark comprising 1,717 instances across five domains, designed to evaluate four underexplored aspects of memory utilization: reasoning over dense histories, identifying implicitly relevant memories, synthesizing distributed evidence into summaries, analyses, or plans, and resisting interference from semantically similar distractors. Evaluating a diverse set of retrieval-based and memory-augmented systems, we find that strong performance on conventional factual-memory benchmarks does not reliably translate into effective memory utilization. Moreover, retrieval alone is insufficient: even when relevant evidence is successfully recovered, systems frequently fail to integrate information across sessions or to distinguish useful evidence from plausible distractors. These findings expose a substantial gap between accessing stored information and using it effectively, and suggest that progress in long-term conversational memory will require architectures that explicitly support evidence integration and robustness to retrieval interference. Code is available at https://github.com/peijunallin/UtilMem.

</details>


### [175] [WebWorld: The Browser as a World Model for Self-Improving Web Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30530)
*Jiajun Wu,Jian Yang,Yaxin Du,Wei Zhang,Haowen Wang,Junhang Cheng,Yuxuan Zhang,Tuney Zheng,Xianglong Liu,Ming Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出WebWorld框架，将浏览器作为确定性世界模型，让视觉语言模型（VLM）在真实可执行的HTML环境中自我改进，通过浏览器签发的接受证书确保修复有效，显著提升HTML生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM驱动的网页代码自我修复循环存在结构性缺陷：同一模型既生成修复又评估修复，视觉可行性并不能保证页面实际可用，缺少不可被VLM欺骗的评估方。

Method: 构建WebWorld接口：VLM输出批评，规划器将其转化为带类型的交互合约；浏览器作为可执行的世界模型重新执行候选代码，仅在满足目标进展且不破坏已验证功能时签发接受证书。证书驱动的质量递增成为SFT阶段唯一可见的监督信号。

Result: 在匹配训练下，WebWorld-27B相较于原始Raw-27B在HTMLBench-400上提升5.3分、在MiniAppBench-Val上提升14.9分，达到Kimi‑K2.6和GPT‑5.4等前沿系统的水平。等规模消融实验表明，若去除浏览器签发的证书，9B模型的提升几乎消失，证明浏览器背书是增益来源。

Conclusion: 将浏览器作为确定性世界模型并通过证书机制进行监督，可有效弥补VLM自评的缺陷，显著提升网页代码自动生成与修复的质量，为未来基于可执行环境的VLM自我改进提供可行路径。

Abstract: VLM-driven self-improvement of web code has a structural flaw: the model that proposes the repair is the model that judges it, and visual plausibility under that judge is a poor proxy for whether the page actually works. What the loop is missing is a counterparty the VLM cannot fool, and the browser already is that counterparty: a deterministic, executable simulator of how an HTML artifact behaves under user actions, and in everything but name a world model for web code. We present WebWorld, the interface that lets a VLM prior interact with this browser-as-world-model autonomously and decides which interactions become supervision. Each round, the VLM emits a critique that the planner compiles into a typed interaction contract; the browser re-executes the candidate and issues an acceptance certificate only when both target progress and preservation of every previously verified capability hold; certified transitions accumulate as a quality ratchet that is the only thing the SFT export ever sees. Under matched training, WebWorld-27B improves Raw-27B by 5.3 points on HTMLBench-400 and 14.9 points on MiniAppBench-Val, and reaches the level of strong frontier systems such as Kimi-K2.6 and GPT-5.4 on interactive HTML generation. Equal-size ablations show that browser-backed admission carries the gain: without the certificate, the matched 9B lift nearly disappears.

</details>


### [176] [Seeing the Unseen: Visual Similarity for Pixel Language Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30541)
*Ran Zhang,Miryam de Lhoneux,Wessel Poelman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究像素级语言模型在藏文等低资源、复杂形态语言上的适应性，发现书写系统的视觉相似性提升跨语言迁移效果，且单语模型在后期适应上优于多语模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素级语言模型通过渲染文本图像取代分词器，使跨语言迁移依赖于书写系统的视觉和结构特性。然而，针对形态复杂、使用独特文字的低资源语言（如藏文）的适配机制尚未系统研究。

Method: 以藏文为案例，进行持续预训练实验，考察数据规模、初始脚本暴露以及来自其他婆罗米系文字的跨语言迁移的影响。提出四个渲染层面的视觉脚本相似度度量指标，并在三个下游任务上评估模型表现。比较多语预训练模型 PIXEL‑M4 与单语混合脚本模型 PIXEL 的适配效果。

Result: 实验表明，书写系统的正交相似度越高，语义迁移效果越好，即使在数据极其有限的情况下亦如此。多语模型 PIXEL‑M4 在初始阶段表现更强，但其后续适配能力受限；相反，单语混合脚本模型 PIXEL 在句子级任务上获得更大提升。

Conclusion: 视觉脚本相似性是像素级语言模型跨语言迁移的关键因素。针对低资源语言时，选择与目标语言视觉相近的预训练脚本并采用单语混合脚本的后续适配策略，可获得更佳的下游任务性能。该研究提供的度量方法和实证结果可指导数据选取和脚本适配决策。

Abstract: Pixel-based language models (LMs) replace traditional tokenizers by processing rendered images of text, making cross-lingual transfer heavily dependent on the visual and structural properties of writing systems. However, the dynamics of adapting these models to low-resource languages with complex morphology and written in unique scripts are not yet explored. Using Tibetan as a case study, we analyze how continued pre-training of pixel-based LMs is influenced by data scale, initial script exposure, and cross-lingual transfer from languages written in other Brahmic scripts. We introduce four rendering-level metrics to quantify visual script similarity. We evaluate downstream performance across three tasks. Our results show that higher orthographic proximity enhances semantic transfer, even under severe data constraints. Additionally, we find a performance asymmetry based on the pre-training starting point: while multilingual pre-training PIXEL-M4 has stronger initial performance, its capacity for subsequent adaptation seems to be constrained, whereas adapting a monolingual model PIXEL with mixed scripts yields more gains on sentence-level tasks. Our metrics and case study offer empirical observations that could help inform data selection and script adaptation choices when working with pixel-based models in similar low-resource settings.

</details>


### [177] [Language Proficiency Assessment from Eye Movements in Naturalistic Passage Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30583)
*Shachar Frenkel,Ido Falah,Omer Shubi,Yevgeni Berzak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过阅读时的眼动轨迹预测英语二语学习者的语言水平，验证其在自然段落、不同测评模型和信息检索情境下的有效性，发现存在母语相近性偏差并提出去偏方法，且其可靠性高于传统测评。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言水平测试依赖词汇、语法等显式任务，难以捕捉真实阅读过程中的认知信息；因此需要一种基于自然阅读行为的、认知驱动的语言能力评估方法。

Method: 在Berzak等人（2018）提出的眼动预测框架基础上，扩展至完整语境段落的阅读，使用多种新颖的语言水平测量和预测模型，并在信息检索任务中收集眼动数据；进一步分析母语与英语相似度对评分的偏差，并提出基于回归残差的去偏方法；最后进行信度分析比较眼动评分与传统评分的可靠性。

Result: 实验表明，眼动特征能够在所有评估设置下有效预测语言水平；但评分确实受到母语与英语相似度的偏倚，去偏方法显著降低该偏差；信度分析显示，眼动评分的内部一致性和重测信度均高于传统语言测试。

Conclusion: 眼动轨迹是评估二语阅读能力的可靠且有效的指标，尽管存在语言亲缘偏差，但可通过去偏技术加以纠正；该方法的高可靠性为未来基于眼动的语言评估技术提供了坚实的实证基础。

Abstract: Standard language proficiency tests rely on linguistic tasks such as vocabulary, grammar and reading comprehension quizzes. An alternative, cognitively motivated approach, introduced in Berzak et al. (2018), proposed instead to predict language proficiency from behavioral traces of eye movements in reading. In this work, we validate and extend this approach from single sentences to more naturalistic reading of contextualized passages in English as a second language, new proficiency measures, prediction models, and reading in an information seeking regime. We find that the approach is effective in all these evaluations. We further address two key open questions on eye movement based proficiency testing: (1) potential scoring biases that reflect the proximity of the reader's native language to English, which may undermine validity, and (2) its reliability. We find that eye movement based proficiency scores are indeed biased towards L1s that are linguistically closer to English. We propose a score debiasing method which effectively remedies this issue. The reliability analyses suggest that eye movement proficiency scores are more reliable than standard language proficiency scores. Overall, our results strengthen and broaden the empirical foundations for future eye movement based language assessment technologies.

</details>


### [178] [Reading the News: Adapting Large Language Models to Swedish Journalism Through Continued Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30609)
*Lukas Borggren,Jenny Kunz,Marco Kuhlmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过在瑞典新闻语料上继续预训练并结合经验回放，提升大语言模型在新闻生成和事实知识方面的表现，但对判别任务帮助有限，且需针对性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在通用任务上表现优秀，但在特定领域（如瑞典新闻）仍表现平平；通过领域继续预训练来弥补这一不足。

Method: 收集并清洗数百万篇瑞典新闻构建高质量语料；对两种规模的模型进行全参数和参数高效微调的继续预训练；引入经验回放防止遗忘；构建覆盖六项编辑任务的领域专属基准；评估生成、事实和判别任务，并尝试低秩适配的训练免费指令跟随方法。

Result: 继续预训练结合经验回放显著提升模型在新闻生成质量和事实知识上的表现；判别任务提升不明显；低秩适配配合训练免费指令方法进一步提升生成表现；现有的瑞典通用基准未能捕捉这些提升，需使用专门的领域基准。

Conclusion: 针对瑞典新闻的继续预训练在生成和知识方面有效，但需经验回放防止遗忘；判别任务仍需其他方法提升；领域专属评估是适配过程的关键。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly capable in general, but their utility can remain modest in niche or understudied areas. One approach to address this limitation is to specialise existing models through additional training on target-domain corpora. In this work, we investigate such continued pre-training for adapting large language models to Swedish journalism, using a high-quality dataset that we curate from millions of news articles. To evaluate the adaptation efficacy, we also construct a novel domain-specific benchmark that covers six editorial tasks. Through full and parameter-efficient fine-tuning across two model sizes, we find that continued pre-training yields benefits in the target domain, but only when paired with experience replay to mitigate forgetting. We observe consistent enhancements in the models' generation quality and factual knowledge, but not their proficiency in discriminative tasks. Exploring a training-free method to facilitate instruction following, we see further improvements, but exclusively for models trained with low-rank adaptation. Crucially, we demonstrate the importance of targeted evaluation in the adaptation process, as an existing Swedish benchmark largely fails to capture the models' in-domain performance gains.

</details>


### [179] [TaxCE : A Framework for Automated Taxonomy Construction and Evaluation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30614)
*Sandeep Sricharan Mukku,Albert Aristotle Nanda,Rohit Pyati*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TaxCE是一种全自动框架，可从原始文本构建多层次层级分类体系，并通过EEG指标进行迭代优化，显著提升层级结构的排他性、穷尽性和细粒度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在评论、访谈、调查等大规模非结构化反馈中，如何自动生成深层次、覆盖长尾主题且可评估的层级分类是当前NLP面临的关键难题，现有方法要么层次浅、忽视长尾，要么缺乏严格评估。

Method: TaxCE通过逐步凝练语料：①将文本压缩为可操作的片段；②去重得到语义单元；③生成带定义的细粒度主题；随后自底向上组织这些主题形成层级，并引入三项基于语料的评估指标——Exclusivity、Exhaustivity、Granularity（EEG），在指标驱动的循环中检测缺陷并进行针对性修正直至收敛。

Result: 在多套基准数据上，TaxCE相较于最强基线在EEG三项指标上分别提升11.8%、20.5%和15.7%。人工评审亦表明其生成的分类体系在质量、可操作性和可导航性上优于其他方法。

Conclusion: TaxCE提供了一套完整的、可自动评估与迭代优化的层级分类构建方案，显著克服了现有方法的层次浅、长尾缺失和评估不足问题，为大规模反馈文本的结构化提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Organizing unstructured feedback text into hierarchical taxonomy is a fundamental challenge in NLP, particularly in domains where feedback arrives at massive scale in varied forms such as reviews, transcripts, and surveys. Existing approaches either produce shallow hierarchies, neglect long-tail topics, or lack rigorous evaluation frameworks. We present TaxCE, a fully automated framework that constructs multi-level hierarchical taxonomies from raw text through progressive condensation of corpus content into actionable segments, deduplicated semantic units, and granular topics with definitions, which are then organized bottom-up into a hierarchy with corpus-groundedness. We also introduce three corpus-grounded evaluation metrics, Exclusivity, Exhaustivity, and Granularity (EEG), and integrate them into a metrics-in-the-loop iterative refinement mechanism that diagnoses deficiencies and applies targeted corrections until convergence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TaxCE consistently outperforms existing baselines spanning classical topic models, neural methods, and LLM-based approaches, with average improvements of 11.8, 20.5, and 15.7 percentage points in exclusivity, exhaustivity, and granularity respectively over the strongest baseline. Human evaluation further confirms superior taxonomy quality, actionability, and navigability.

</details>


### [180] [Hidden Threat in Synthetic Data: Covert Targeted Bias Injection through Benign Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30619)
*Minkyung Cho,Jihyo Kim,SeungWoo Song,Junghun Yuk,Minjoon Kee,Hoyun Song,KyungTae Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，利用看似无害的合成数据可以向对齐的大语言模型注入特定社会偏见，构成安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据在大语言模型训练中的使用日益增多，但其潜在的安全风险缺乏系统研究，尤其是是否能通过合成数据隐蔽地植入偏见。

Method: 构建流水线：让一个未对齐的教师模型生成经过过滤的合成数据集（涵盖创意写作、代码生成等领域），再使用这些数据微调对齐的学生模型，以评估是否能传递目标偏见。

Result: 实验表明，表面上 benign 的合成数据能够作为隐蔽通道成功注入目标偏见，同时对学生模型的通用任务能力影响有限。

Conclusion: 合成数据驱动的 LLM 训练管道存在未被充分认识的安全风险；提出基于对数线性评分的筛选方法作为潜在防御手段，呼吁进一步完善防护措施。

Abstract: Synthetic data is increasingly used to train large language models (LLMs), yet its security implications remain poorly understood. Prior work on subliminal learning suggests that models can inherit behavioral traits from seemingly unrelated training data. In this work, we investigate whether such mechanisms can be exploited to inject targeted social biases into aligned models through semantically benign synthetic data. We construct a pipeline in which a misaligned teacher model generates filtered synthetic datasets across domains such as creative writing and code generation, which are then used to fine-tune aligned student models. Our experiments show that benign-looking synthetic data can act as a covert channel for transmitting targeted biases while largely preserving the student model's general task capabilities. These results reveal a previously underexplored security risk in synthetic data-driven LLM training pipelines and highlight the need for improved safeguards. As one possible step toward this goal, we suggest that log-linearity-based scoring may provide a useful signal for screening seemingly benign synthetic data.

</details>


### [181] [REER-PT: Reverse-Engineered Reasoning for Perplexity-Guided Pre-training Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30627)
*Haoran Que,Jiajun Shi,Ting Huang,Renming Pang,Jiaheng Liu,Ge Zhang,Wenhao Huang,Shen Yan,Wei Ye,Shikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: REER-PT 在预训练数据中插入简短推理注释，使难以预测的续写更易预测，显著降低困惑度并提升模型在知识与推理基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型规模扩大，获取高质量训练数据成为瓶颈；传统的下一个词预测只监督续写本身，忽略了上下文与续写之间的隐式推理过程，导致数据利用率不足。

Method: REER-PT 将 Reverse-Engineered Reasoning (REER) 扩展到原始预训练语料。离线生成候选推理注释，使用困惑度（perplexity）作为优化信号，对注释进行筛选和精炼，限制长度并防止目标泄漏，仅保留能从前文推断但难以直接预测的续写。最终将注释稀疏插入原文，保持原始文本不变，兼容标准的下一个词预测，无需在线推理。

Result: 在增强数据上，困惑度下降 0.42~7.29；仅 0.05% 的 13-gram 注释在原文中出现原样。两模型（均 680M 参数）对比显示，使用增强语料的模型在多个知识与推理基准上提升最高 2.07% 的准确率。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏的推理注释改写预训练语料，可在不改变标准预训练目标的前提下提升续写可预测性和模型下游性能，证明该数据增强方法在大规模语言模型预训练中具有实用价值。

Abstract: As language-model compute continues to scale, high-quality training data is becoming an increasingly important bottleneck. Conventional next-token prediction supervises what follows a context but leaves the intermediate reasoning behind that continuation implicit. We introduce \textbf{REER-PT}, a scalable framework that extends Reverse-Engineered Reasoning (REER) to raw pre-training data. REER-PT identifies continuations that are difficult to predict but can still be inferred from the preceding context, and inserts concise reasoning annotations that reconstruct the missing connection between context and continuation. Candidate annotations are generated and refined offline, with perplexity serving as the optimization signal. Constraints on length and target leakage filter out unhelpful or trivial annotations. This sparse transformation preserves the source text and remains compatible with standard next-token prediction, avoiding online reasoning rollouts during pre-training. We apply REER-PT to transform a source pre-training corpus into an augmented one. Across augmented-data, original-token, and selected-continuation comparisons, perplexity reductions range from 0.42 to 7.29, and only about 0.05\% of annotation 13-grams appear verbatim in the source text. We then train two 680M-parameter models with the same architecture and training configuration on the source and augmented corpora, respectively. The augmented-data model gains up to 2.07 percentage points on several knowledge and reasoning benchmarks. Together, the perplexity analysis indicates improved continuation predictability, while the controlled pre-training experiments suggest that this augmentation can improve model performance without changing the standard pre-training objective.

</details>


### [182] [GMTS: Gradient Magnitude-based Token Selection Improves RLVR Training for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30632)
*Outongyi Lv,Yuanwei Zhang,Xiaoqun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于梯度幅度的高熵token选择（GMTS），在RLVR训练中用前20%重要token提升推理性能，优于仅用熵筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然高熵token在提升LLM推理效果上表现显著，但熵并不能在不同答案间一致反映token重要性，缺乏对梯度信息的利用。

Method: 观察到高熵token与梯度幅度相关，提出GMTS方法：利用熵‑梯度关联近似梯度幅度，对所有token进行排序并选取梯度幅度最高的前20%用于RLVR训练。

Result: 在三个推理领域和多种模型规模实验表明，GMTS选取的前20% token 在RLVR 训练中 consistently 超过仅基于熵的选择，显著提升模型推理表现。

Conclusion: 梯度幅度是衡量token贡献的更细粒度指标，GMTS 能更有效地筛选关键token，推动RLVR 在提升大模型推理能力方面的进一步发展。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL), particularly RL with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), has recently emerged as a central paradigm for enhancing large language models' (LLMs) reasoning abilities, demonstrating remarkable effectiveness across reasoning tasks. Recent studies suggest that high-entropy tokens play an exceptionally important role in model training, since training with only the highest 20% entropy tokens yields significant performance gains. However, why such high-entropy tokens are beneficial remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we find that although high-entropy tokens within one answer tend to correlate with large gradient magnitude, entropy alone fails to consistently reflect token importance across different answers, considering the variations in the answer-level reward signals. Based on this observation, we introduce the Gradient Magnitude-based Token Selection (GMTS) method to quantify token importance, which leverages the entropy-gradient connection to approximate gradient-magnitude rankings for token selection.
