We find that training on the top 20% tokens ranked by GMTS consistently outperforms entropy-based token selection across three reasoning domains and various model sizes, suggesting that GMTS provides a more fine-grained estimate of token contribution for RLVR training.

</details>


### [183] [BiG-SURE - Bipartite Graph for Semantic Uncertainty and Reliability Estimation of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30646)
*Debarpan Bhattacharya,Malay Phadke,Sriram Ganapathy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 BiG-SURE，一种基于跨温度语义一致性的黑盒不确定性估计方法，通过低温锚点和高温探针构建二分图并利用谱能量计算置信度，在多语言和多模态问答任务上提升了平均弃答 AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键场景中，需要对大语言模型和视觉语言模型的预测不确定性进行可靠估计，尤其在模型参数不可得（黑盒）时，现有方法不足以提供有效的置信度评估。

Method: BiG-SURE 先在低温度下采样得到语义稳定的锚点响应，再在高温度下对保持意义不变的输入变换进行采样得到探针响应；利用自然语言推理（NLI）得分构建锚点‑探针二分图（BiG），并通过该矩阵的归一化平方谱能量定义置信度，置信度的补数即为不确定性。

Result: 在文本 QA、多语言 QA 与多模态 QA 等多种任务和模型族上实验表明，BiG‑SURE 相较于已有的黑盒不确定性估计器提升了平均弃答 AUROC，且保持了方法的简洁、无监督和黑盒适用性。

Conclusion: BiG‑SURE 通过跨温度语义一致性和谱能量度量提供了一种有效、通用的黑盒不确定性估计方案，为大模型在安全关键应用中的可靠部署提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimation is a crucial requirement for deploying large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) in safety-critical settings, especially when the model parameters are not accessible (black-box). We propose BiG-SURE, an uncertainty estimator based on cross-temperature semantic agreement. The method samples low-temperature responses as stable semantic anchors and high-temperature responses as probes under meaning-preserving input transformations. It then constructs an anchor-probe Bipartite Graph (BiG) using NLI-based entailment scores and defines confidence through the normalized squared spectral energy of this matrix, with uncertainty given by its complement. This bipartite graph-based Semantic Uncertainty and Reliability Estimation (SURE) score measures whether high-temperature probes remain semantically aligned with the model's stable low-temperature belief or not. We evaluate BiG-SURE on text QA, multilingual QA, and multimodal QA tasks across multiple model families. In these experiments, BiG-SURE improves average abstention AUROC over prior black-box uncertainty estimators, while remaining simple, unsupervised, and applicable to black-box model settings.

</details>


### [184] [What It Costs to Compose, Rebuild, and Correct Precomputed Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30647)
*Asa Shepard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 预计算记忆（缓存或压缩的键值对）在重复查询时可减少上下文输入，但其正确性仅在能及时重建或实时更新时保持；单独组装的记忆会导致错误，重建成本高，且对 phrasing 敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在每次请求时重新读取大量上下文成本高，研究希望通过预先计算并存储记忆来降低重复查询的计算开销，并探讨其可靠性和局限。

Method: 在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上进行实验，比较直接使用键值缓存、训练得到的缓存压缩两种预计算记忆；评估不同组装方式、重建频率、以及通过特定短语注入的实时更新对正确性的影响，并测量相应的计算成本。

Result: 1) 将预计算记忆拆分后再组合会显著降低正确率；2) 记忆只有在频繁重建（成本约为完整准备的较大比例）时才能保持最新；3) 对 phrasing 敏感的实时更新可以在重建之间临时保持正确性；4) 训练压缩的缓存在热重建时表现出成本效益。

Conclusion: 预计算记忆适用于查询频繁且信息更新周期可预测的场景；应在信息变动的节奏上定期重建记忆，或通过特定短语的实时注入在重建间隙保持准确性。未来工作需解决重建成本、组合记忆的鲁棒性以及更通用的实时更新机制。

Abstract: Language models can answer from precomputed memory, a model's saved reading of a body of material, reused across requests instead of read again at each. This paper maps where that practice preserves correctness and the conditions under which it fails. Across experiments on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct using both saved key-value caches and trained compressions of them, precomputed memory degrades when assembled from separately prepared parts, stays current only through rebuilds costing a large fraction of full preparation in our measurements, and ignores corrections served beside it conditional on phrasing. If precomputed memories can be served alongside one another, be cost-efficiently rebuilt, and be superseded by new information arriving in real-time, they can serve as a way to avoid re-feeding context to a model over repeated queries. The implication of our results for a deployed system that deals with a variety of queries is that precomputed memories are best rebuilt on the cadence at which new information changes what the memory was originally computed from. Both warm-rebuilding trained compressions of key-value caches and serving specifically-phrased updates beside a memory, as pasted text or injected cache state, show particular promise for keeping precomputed memories current, the latter as an interim measure between rebuilds, and we measure the cost and name the remaining questions associated with each.

</details>


### [185] [Where Identity Lives: Localized, Retain-Free Identity Unlearning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30649)
*Kangwook Ko,Jaehyuk Jang,Wonjun Lee,Hee-Seon Kim,Changick Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了无需保留集的多模态大语言模型忘记方法 PAVA，只在解码器 MLP 层局部更新，并通过视觉属性锚定保持感知能力，实现了在仅使用忘记集的情况下最佳的忘记-保留权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型部署后需要删除特定个人信息，但现有方法依赖难以获取的保留集，且重新构建保留集会再次暴露隐私；仅使用忘记集会破坏视觉-语言计算，导致感知性能下降。

Method: 将无保留集的忘记问题视为定位问题，发现身份信息主要存储在解码器早中期的 MLP 层。提出 Pathway‑Aware Visual‑attribute Anchoring (PAVA)：仅在这些层进行权重更新，并结合忘记损失与视觉属性锚定损失，后者通过对忘记图像的自身未忘记答案进行蒸馏，保持图像感知行为。

Result: 在 MLLMU‑Bench 与 ReMem 两个基准上，PAVA 在仅使用忘记集的情况下实现了最强的忘记‑保留权衡，且与基于保留集的基线方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: PAVA 证明了在多模态大语言模型中，无需保留集即可实现高效、低干扰的身份信息忘记，为实际部署后的隐私保护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Removing a specific individual's information from multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is often needed after deployment, but existing methods rely on a retain set, which is hardest to obtain at that point, and rebuilding it recreates the privacy exposure that unlearning aims to remove. Forgetting from the forget set alone instead damages the shared visual-language computation, harming perception. We cast retain-free unlearning as a localization problem: causal tracing, weight transplant, and Fisher overlap all point to early-to-mid decoder MLPs as the layers where identity information is stored and, unlike other module families, can be modified without substantially disrupting vision. We turn this into Pathway-Aware Visual-attribute Anchoring (PAVA), which confines updates to these layers and pairs a forget loss with a visual-attribute anchor that preserves image-grounded behavior by distilling the model's own pre-unlearning answers from the forget images alone. On MLLMU-Bench and ReMem, PAVA gives the strongest forget-retain trade-off among forget-set-only methods and remains competitive with retain-based baselines.

</details>


### [186] [SwarmBench: Can Large Language Models Act as Agent Swarm Orchestrators?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30661)
*Jinshan Gao,Zhuoran Jin,Tianyi Men,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SwarmBench 基准用于多视角评估大语言模型在 Agent Swarm 编排中的表现，并基于此提出 SwarmExp 经验提取与回放方法提升编排能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测多代理系统的基准主要针对单代理或通用任务，缺乏对动态编排能力的系统化评估，导致难以衡量模型在协同、效率、成本等方面的真实水平。

Method: 构建 SwarmBench 基准，分别从准确性、效率、成本和编排过程质量四个维度对模型进行评测；随后提出 SwarmExp，通过从历史交互中提取经验并在训练/推理阶段进行经验回放，以强化模型的编排策略。

Result: 实验表明不同大语言模型在编排能力上差异显著，体现在最终准确率、执行效率、资源消耗以及编排过程的整体质量上；使用 SwarmExp 可在多种模型上实现一致的编排性能提升。

Conclusion: SwarmBench 为评估多代理编排提供了全面基准，验证了模型编排能力的多样性；SwarmExp 通过经验提取与回放的简单机制，有效提升了大语言模型在 Agent Swarm 场景下的编排表现。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems are evolving from fixed interaction topologies toward dynamically orchestrated Agent Swarms. However, existing benchmarks are still largely based on single-agent or general-purpose agent tasks, making it difficult to systematically evaluate key orchestration capabilities. We propose SwarmBench, a benchmark that evaluates model performance from multiple perspectives, including accuracy, efficiency, cost, and process quality. Experimental results show that current models exhibit substantial differences in orchestration capability. These differences are reflected not only in final accuracy, efficiency, and cost, but also in the overall quality of the orchestration process itself. Based on these findings, we further propose SwarmExp, a simple yet effective method based on experience extraction and experience replay, which consistently improves the orchestration performance of large language models.

</details>


### [187] [MURANO: Design, Run, and Reproduce Mechanistic Interpretability Experiments as Composable Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30662)
*Alireza Bayat Makou,Emirhan Böge,Phu Gia Hoang,Federico Tiblias,Jingcheng Niu,Subhabrata Dutta,Richard Eckart de Castilho,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文推出 Murano，一个开源框架，用于统一、可组合地组织大语言模型机制解释性研究的加载、记录、归因、干预和评估等步骤，实现研究流程的可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释性工具多聚焦于工作流的单一环节，研究者需在多个库之间转换数据，导致适配成本高、复现困难。为降低跨库集成壁垒、提升研究可复现性，亟需一个统一的、可组合的框架。

Method: Murano 将解释性研究的五大环节抽象为可组合的 Step，Step 通过命名的输入/输出 Artifact 进行数据交换，并使用统一的地址体系标识组件。用户按顺序组装 Step 成 Pipeline，框架负责调度执行并兼容已有解释性和机器学习库。

Result: 通过两项已有解释性研究的复现以及一个稀疏自编码器案例，验证了 Murano 在不同任务和库之间的兼容性、步骤组合的灵活性以及结果的可复现性。

Conclusion: Murano 提供了一个跨学科、可扩展的解释性研究平台，简化了工作流集成，促进了大语言模型机制解释研究的共享与复现。

Abstract: This paper presents Murano, an open source framework for designing, running, and reproducing mechanistic interpretability studies of large language models, intended for researchers across disciplines. These studies often combine loading, recording, attribution, intervention, and evaluation, while existing libraries tend to focus on different parts of this workflow. As a result, researchers using several libraries may need to adapt outputs from one for use by another. To bridge this gap, Murano represents operations from these five areas as composable steps. Steps exchange named result artifacts and declare the inputs they require and the outputs they produce. A pipeline executes its steps in the order supplied, and Murano uses canonical addresses when component identities pass between operations. Murano builds on existing interpretability and machine learning libraries. We demonstrate Murano through two reproductions of established interpretability studies and one illustrative sparse autoencoder case study.

</details>


### [188] [OCR-MetaReasoning Benchmark: Evaluating the Meta-Reasoning Ability of MLLMs in Text-Rich Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30678)
*Gengxu Li,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 OCR-MetaReasoning 基准，系统评估多模态大语言模型在文本图像中的演绎、归纳、溯因三种推理方向及其过程合规性，发现现有模型在可见规则应用和布局推理上仍有显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本丰富的图像理解需要模型在 OCR 基础上组织词汇、布局、字段、图表等多模态证据。现有评测混淆了信息抽取与推理，且未区分推理方向（可见规则、抽象规律、缺失前提），导致对模型真实推理能力的评估不准确。

Method: 构建 OCR-MetaReasoning 基准：1500 条经人工验证的单图样本，形成 3（推理类型）×5（OCR 对象类别）的平衡分类。为每题提供参考推理步骤，设计自动评分机制，包括最终答案正确性（MRMS）和推理过程合规性（RPCS），实现对答案与过程的独立评估。

Result: 在多种闭源和开源 MLLM 上实验表明：①模型在可见规则应用和布局敏感推理上表现差强人意；②即使最终答案错误，模型仍能生成过程合规的推理文本；③整体 MRMS 与 RPCS 均远低于饱和水平，说明该任务仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: OCR‑MetaReasoning 为文本图像的元推理提供了细粒度、方向区分的评测框架，揭示了当前 MLLM 在规则应用、布局推理及过程一致性方面的显著短板，为后续模型改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Text-rich image understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to organize OCR (Optical Character Recognition)-grounded evidence across words, layout, fields, charts, and visual correspondences. Existing evaluations often conflate extraction with reasoning and rarely test whether models follow the required reasoning direction: applying visible rules, abstracting hidden regularities, or recovering missing premises. We introduce OCR-MetaReasoning, a controlled single-image benchmark that treats deduction, induction, and abduction as distinct directions and separates final-answer correctness from reasoning-process compliance. The benchmark contains 1,500 verified samples in a balanced \(3\times5\) taxonomy crossing three reasoning types with five OCR-object categories, along with reference reasoning steps, automatic answer scoring, the Meta-Reasoning Macro Score (MRMS), and the Reasoning Process Compliance Score (RPCS). Experiments with representative closed-source and open-source MLLMs show that OCR-grounded meta-reasoning remains far from saturated: models struggle with visible-rule application and layout-sensitive inference, while process-compliant rationales can accompany incorrect final answers under exact-match evaluation. The code is available at https://github.com/gengxuli/OCR-MetaReasoning.

</details>


### [189] [LCoT-GV: Graph Attention Networks for Verifying Long Reasoning Chains in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30679)
*Bérénice Jaulmes,Mehwish Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于图的长链式思考验证框架 LCoT‑GV，用图注意力网络对推理图进行正确性预测，提升对 LCoT 步骤质量的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型生成的长链式思考（LCoT）虽能得到正确答案，但中间步骤常出现矛盾、无依据或无关信息，缺乏有效手段评估这些步骤的可靠性。

Method: 将 LCoT 转化为推理图：节点为单步推理，边表示语义/逻辑关系；使用图注意力网络（GAT）对图进行特征聚合并预测整体链式思考的正确性。构建了面向多领域问答的图式验证数据集用于训练与评估。

Result: 在多个推理基准上实验表明，LCoT‑GV 在链式思考正确性预测上与最先进的对比方法持平或略有优势，验证了图结构建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 图化表示和 GAT 预测为评估 LCoT 步骤质量提供了可行路径，未来可进一步结合更细粒度的逻辑约束和跨模态信息，以提升大模型推理的可解释性和可靠性。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models produce Long Chains-of-Thought (LCoTs) which involve breaking down the problem into smaller reasoning steps before reaching the conclusion. However, these steps often contain contradictions, unsupported inferences, or irrelevant steps, even when the final answer is correct. We propose Long Chain-of-Thought Graph Verifier (LCoT-GV), a graph-based framework that represents LCoTs as reasoning graphs. Each node in the graph represents a reasoning step and the edges encode semantic and logical relations. A Graph Attention Network is then trained to predict chain-of-thought correctness from the reasoning graph. We construct a new graph-oriented verification dataset from multiple reasoning benchmarks for question answering in various domains. The results show that our method is competitive with the most similar approaches.

</details>


### [190] [WildSEEK: Evaluating Language Models for Information-Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30683)
*Tanise Ceron,Joachim Baumann,Elisa Bassignana,Berat Cabuk,Dirk Hovy,Debora Nozza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出WildSEEK数据集和评估框架，系统评估LLM在真实信息检索查询中的可靠性、安全性和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型成为用户获取信息的主要渠道，现有评估多聚焦特定主题或合成数据，难以反映真实查询的复杂性和潜在风险，亟需面向真实用户交互的系统化评估方法。

Method: 构建WildSEEK数据集，收集并人工标注3000条真实信息检索查询，标注风险敏感领域、事实型与分析型查询等；基于此训练分类器，对180万条真实查询进行分析；设计四项评估指标（迎合行为、过度依赖、美国中心视角、对弱势群体的处理），评估LLM生成的回答。

Result: 约三分之一的查询属于高风险且更倾向于分析型；LLM在四项指标上的失效率显著高于事实型查询，尤其在迎合、过度依赖、美国中心视角和弱势群体处理方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: WildSEEK提供了监测LLM可靠性、安全性和公平性的实证基础，表明在信息获取场景中，LLM需在分析型高风险查询上提升表现，以实现更公平可靠的信息生态。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly mediating information access to end users, urging a systematic evaluation of their responses for a fair and reliable information ecosystem. Existing evaluations, however, are often topic-specific or synthetic, limiting their ability to capture the complexity of "in the wild" information-seeking queries and the risks present in model responses. To address this gap, we introduce WildSEEK, a manually annotated dataset of 3k information-seeking queries from real user interactions, and an evaluation framework for LLM-generated responses. WildSEEK includes annotations for risk-sensitive domains (e.g. health and financial information), and distinguishes factoid queries from analytical queries which seek responses beyond facts. We train classifiers on WildSEEK to analyze more than 1.8M realistic user queries. We find that over a third of information-seeking queries are high-risk and more often analytical. Our findings show that LLM responses fail more often in four criteria: sycophantic behavior, overreliance, a default US-centric perspective, and poor handling of vulnerable populations -- with failure rates being mostly higher for analytical queries. By providing methods to monitor the reliability, safety, and fairness of LLM behavior, our dataset and evaluation framework offer an empirical foundation for the broader question of how these systems should behave as they take on a growing role in information access.

</details>


### [191] [An Agentic Retrobiosynthesis Framework with Learned Frontier Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30702)
*Philippe Meyer,Guillaume Gricourt,Thomas Duigou,Joan Hérisson,Jean-Loup Faulon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在生物逆合成任务中，针对前沿分子选择的策略（使用 Qwen2.5-7B 通过 Prompt 与 LoRA 微调）显著提升了在固定搜索预算下的解题率，且优于传统的 MCTS 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被用作多步逆合成的智能体，需明确其搜索策略本身对结果的贡献，而非仅依赖底层反应模型。因此，需要在保持反应生成一致的前提下，评估策略对搜索效率的影响。

Method: 采用基于规则的生物逆合成引擎生成统一的反应转移，策略仅决定下一个扩展的前沿分子。使用 Qwen2.5-7B 模型通过 Prompt 与 LoRA 微调实现前沿选择，比较其在 LASER、RetroPath RL Golden 与 BioNavi-NP 基准上的表现，并与 Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) 进行对照。

Result: 微调后的策略在 10 次扩展时在 LASER 上的解题率为 65%±1%，高于 MCTS 的 59%；在 200 次扩展时分别达到 LASER 78%±1%、RetroPath RL Golden 88%±3%（对比 MCTS 的 80%）以及 BioNavi-NP 63%±2%（对比 45%）。微调始终优于直接 Prompt。

Conclusion: 在保持生化生成不变的情况下，针对前沿分子进行路由监督的选择策略能够在预算受限的搜索中提升逆合成成功率，但整体性能仍受前沿构建和反应排序质量的限制。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as agents for multistep retrosynthesis, raising the question of how much their search policy contributes independently of the underlying reaction model. We investigate this question in a biological setting through rule-based retrobiosynthesis: a deterministic biochemical engine generates the same validated transitions for every method, searching for routes that terminate in metabolites available to an \emph{Escherichia coli} chassis, while the policy only selects which frontier molecule to expand next. Prompted and LoRA-tuned Qwen2.5-7B policies use a strict choice-only interface. The fine-tuned policy reaches $65\pm1$\% solve rate at 10 expansions on LASER versus 59\% for MCTS, and at 200 expansions reaches $78\pm1$\% versus 75\% on LASER, $88\pm3$\% versus 80\% on the RetroPath RL Golden benchmark, and $63\pm2$\% versus 45\% on the BioNavi-NP benchmark. Fine-tuning also consistently outperforms direct prompting. These results show that route-supervised frontier selection can improve budgeted search without altering biochemical generation, although performance remains dependent on frontier construction and reaction ranking.

</details>


### [192] [GUIDE: Guiding Internal Evidence with Language Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30712)
*Soyeon Caren Han,Hyunsuk Chung,Jinwoo Kim,Seungyeon Ji,Kyungreem Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GUIDE框架，通过指令调节多模态模型内部证据路径，实现对证据使用的可控性并提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型虽能遵循生成指令，但仍会依赖快捷线索而非指令要求的证据，缺乏对证据来源的可控性。

Method: GUIDE结合分组参数高效适配和指令条件门控，在推理和生成过程中调制多模态证据通路；并提出路径级评估框架，衡量指令条件下的证据依赖敏感性、受控扰动、通路调制及自回归解码动态。

Result: 在GQA、TextVQA、MM-IMDb、CREMA-D、RAVDESS、Flickr30K等任务上，GUIDE实现了结构化的证据依赖重分配，保持任务性能的同时提升了针对证据扰动的鲁棒性，并支持跨多模态场景的可控调节。

Conclusion: 多模态指令跟随可超越输出控制，向调节不同证据来源对预测的贡献扩展，GUIDE展示了实现此目标的有效路径。

Abstract: Large multimodal models follow instructions about what to generate, but not necessarily about what evidence to rely on. Hence, models may continue to depend on shortcut-associated cues even when instructions suggest otherwise. We introduce GUIDE, a framework for controlling internal evidence usage through language instructions. GUIDE combines grouped parameter-efficient adaptation with instruction-conditioned gating to modulate multimodal evidence pathways during reasoning and generation. We further introduce a pathway-level evaluation framework that characterizes instruction-conditioned evidence modulation through reliance sensitivity, controlled perturbation analysis, pathway modulation, and autoregressive decoding dynamics. Across multimodal reasoning, classification, and generation, GUIDE induces structured and instruction-aligned redistribution of evidence reliance while largely preserving task behavior. Experiments on GQA, TextVQA, MM-IMDb, CREMA-D, RAVDESS, and Flickr30K show that GUIDE improves robustness under targeted evidence perturbations and enables controllable modulation across diverse multimodal settings. This suggests that multimodal instruction following can extend beyond output control toward regulating how different evidence sources contribute to model predictions.

</details>


### [193] [SocialReasonBench: A Video-QA Benchmark for Social Reasoning with Counterfactual Narrative Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30716)
*Zheyu Huang,Zijing Shi,Haozhe Luo,Huadong Tang,Mingyu Liu,Meng Fang,Ling Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SocialReasonBench——基于《底特律：变人》分支剧情的多选视频问答基准，用于评估大规模多模态模型在社会情境推理上的能力，实验表明现有模型在基本社交理解尚可，但在反事实与因果推理上表现不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型多模态模型在视频理解取得进展，但现有评测多聚焦单一路径视频，难以判断模型是否真正理解社会动态，容易被重复叙事模式利用；因此需要一个能够检验模型对人类社会情境深层推理的基准。

Method: 构建 SocialReasonBench：从《底特律：变人》游戏视频中利用分支剧情生成多选题；采用多代理策划流水线定位社会意义片段、基于游戏状态信号标注答案、并生成理论指导的带诊断干扰项的问题；覆盖意图识别、情感共情、道德困境、反事实推理、因果前因等七个推理维度。

Result: 在多种最新 LMM 上进行实验，模型在基本社交理解任务上取得合理分数，但在反事实推理和因果推理上显著下降；消融实验和错误诊断显示模型依赖不完整的模态线索，易受视觉捷径等推理陷阱影响。

Conclusion: SocialReasonBench 揭示了当前 LMM 在可观测事件识别与潜在社会状态深层推理之间的差距，表明未来需要加强模型对多模态线索的综合利用和对潜在社会因素的推理能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have greatly improved video understanding, yet their ability to reason about human-centered social situations remains limited. Existing benchmarks typically rely on videos with a single observed trajectory, making it difficult to determine whether models truly understand social dynamics or merely exploit recurring narrative patterns. We introduce SocialReasonBench, a video multiple-choice QA benchmark for evaluating socially grounded reasoning in scenarios derived from interactive narratives. Built from gameplay videos of Detroit: Become Human, the benchmark leverages branching storylines where player decisions lead to alternative social outcomes that can be checked against the game's own script, flowchart, and recorded branches. We develop a multi-agent curation pipeline that localizes socially meaningful clips, grounds answer labels in game-state signals, and generates theory-guided questions with diagnostic distractors. SocialReasonBench covers seven reasoning dimensions, including intent recognition, emotional empathy, moral dilemma, counterfactual reasoning, and causal antecedent. Experiments on contemporary LMMs show that models perform reasonably well on basic social understanding but struggle with counterfactual and causal reasoning. Further ablation and diagnostic error analyses reveal that models often depend on incomplete modality cues and fall into reasoning traps such as visual shortcuts, highlighting a gap between observable event recognition and deeper reasoning over latent social states.

</details>


### [194] [Mind the Gap: Theory-of-Mind-Grounded Friction for Epistemic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30719)
*Yifan Zhu,Kyeongmin Rim,James Pustejovsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将理论心理模型（ToM）推理转化为可控信号，用于在对话中检测并校正表层协作与认知对齐的分歧，通过四元信念结构量化摩擦，实现对指称歧义的干预，实验表明该信号在表示层和策略层均显著提升误解召回率、干预效果和校准性能，且训练更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产性对话中，需要区分表层协作（如确认、任务推进）和认知对齐（信念收敛）。现有基于偏好的方法只优化响应层面，缺乏对认知对齐的显式建模，导致在“沉默分歧”（双方自信但指称不同）情形下误解频发。

Method: 在 Frictive Policy Optimization 框架中，将 ToM 推理作为控制信号。对每个指称表达提取四部分信念：说话者意图指称、听者解释、说话者对听者信念的模型、听者对说话者信念的模型。通过比较这些认知状态计算“摩擦”，量化 epistemic 差异。提出两种基于该信号的策略改进：奖励塑形（FAR）和信任域约束（FTR），并在 DPO 基线上进行对比。

Result: 在表示层实验中，去除二阶信念通道将误解召回率从 65% 降至 26%。在策略层，FAR 与 FTR 相比 DPO 显著提升干预 F1、上下文校准（Brier 分数下降），并保持更高的训练稳定性；DPO 在不同运行间波动大，甚至削弱原有干预能力。

Conclusion: 基于 ToM 的摩擦信号能够有效捕捉指称信念分歧，为上下文敏感的干预提供可训练的控制信号，提升对话系统在认知对齐方面的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Abstract: Productive dialogue alignment requires distinguishing \emph{surface coordination} (acknowledgments and smooth task progression) from \emph{epistemic alignment} (convergence of belief states); standard preference-based methods typically optimize response-level preferences without explicitly modeling the latter. We operationalize Theory-of-Mind (ToM) inference as a control signal within Frictive Policy Optimization by extracting, at each referring expression, a four-part belief structure: the speaker's intended referent, the addressee's interpretation, and each participant's model of the other's belief. This makes friction mechanically computable from epistemic-state comparisons, capturing \emph{silent divergence}, where both participants proceed confidently while grounding to different referents. We evaluate the signal at two levels. At the representation level, ablating the second-order channel reduces misunderstanding recall from $65\%$ to $26\%$. At the policy level, reward-shaping (FAR) and trust-region (FTR) variants improve intervention F1 and warranted-context calibration over DPO, with Brier scores independently supporting the calibration gains. Across three training runs, FAR and FTR remain substantially more stable, whereas DPO varies widely and can degrade intervention competence already present in the base policy. Thus, ToM-grounded friction provides a trainable signal for context-sensitive intervention under referential belief divergence.

</details>


### [195] [Where Do Multilingual Vision-Language Encoders Fail on Low-Resource Languages?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30725)
*Donghoon Han,SungHyun Moon,Aidyn Zhakatayev,Junghun Cha,SeungJae Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，多语言视觉语言编码器在低资源语言检索性能差的根本原因不是线性语言偏差，而是编码器深层的EOS隐藏状态随层次的语言轨迹分歧。通过在投影层前替换EOS隐藏状态为对应的英文值，可显著提升低资源语言检索效果，同时保持高资源语言性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多语言视觉语言编码器已覆盖数百种语言，但在低资源语言（如斯瓦希里语）的检索任务上仍比高资源语言（如英语）低30个百分点以上。作者希望定位导致这一差距的内部机制，以便改进模型。

Method: 1) 使用线性语言分类器（LEACE）和迭代消除线性投影（INLP）削弱模型的线性语言方向，验证线性偏差是否为根因；2) 分析编码器内部，发现EOS隐藏状态的语言轨迹随层数分歧；3) 在投影层前的三层位置，用对应的英文EOS隐藏状态替换各语言的EOS隐藏状态；4) 设计对照实验排除位置聚合和英文特异性因素；5) 在训练阶段加入前层trunk，使每种语言的投影向内容中心对齐。

Result: - 削弱线性语言方向后，低资源语言检索性能几乎不变（±1.5个百分点），说明线性偏差是症状而非原因。- 替换EOS隐藏状态后，Swahili检索准确率从22.1%提升至69.1%。- 前层trunk在训练时提升低资源语言XM3600检索9.6/17.1个百分点，并在其他三项基准上保持一致提升，同时不损失高资源语言表现。

Conclusion: 低资源语言检索性能的瓶颈源于编码器深层的EOS隐藏状态随语言的分歧，而非表层的线性语言偏差。通过在投影前对EOS隐藏状态进行跨语言对齐或在训练时加入语言对齐trunk，可显著改善低资源语言的检索效果，且不会牺牲高资源语言的性能。

Abstract: Recent multilingual vision--language encoders cover hundreds of languages in a single model, yet on two state-of-the-art instances retrieval on low-resource languages (LRL; e.g. Swahili) trails high-resource ones (HRL; e.g. English) by $30^+$\,pp. We ask where in the trained encoder this gap is located. Prior modality-gap and cross-lingual subspace work suggests a linear language direction at the output crowds out alignment-relevant geometry. We falsify this: LEACE drives the linear language classifier from $>99\%$ to near chance and iterated INLP to $37$--$50\%$ while LRL retrieval moves within $\pm 1.5$\,pp and all tier means within $2.2$\,pp, tracking random controls. The linear bias is a \emph{symptom}, not the cause. Instead, the alignment-causal factor lies along the encoder's forward path: the EOS (end-of-sequence) hidden state's per-language trajectory diverges with depth. Substituting the EOS with its parallel English value three blocks before the projector lifts Swahili from $22.1\%$ to $69.1\%$ on one encoder (and reproduces on the other); three controls rule out pooled-position tautology and English specificity. A front-layer trunk that pulls each language's projection toward the parallel-content centroid corroborates the diagnosis at training time, recovering $+9.6$ / $+17.1$\,pp on LRL XM3600 retrieval (1{,}000-image subset), with consistent gains across three further benchmarks while preserving HRL performance.

</details>


### [196] [Calibrating Small Language Models for Claim Check-Worthiness Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30731)
*Pratuat Amatya,Venktesh Viswanathan,Vinay Setty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 NN-PPI 方法，通过在推理时对小模型进行轻量级后置校准，使其在检查价值检测任务上达到与大语言模型相当的准确率，显著提升 F1 并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在初创公司实际部署中，使用大语言模型对每条声明进行检查价值评估成本高、延迟大，而小模型虽省资源但准确率不足，需要一种既经济又能提升小模型性能的方案。

Method: 在预测阶段引入 NN-PPI（Prediction‑Powered Inference 的点式扩展），作为后置层对模型输出进行残差校准，无需重新训练原始模型，兼容少样本小模型和已微调的生产模型。

Result: 在不同基线模型上，NN‑PPI 将加权 F1 提升 12%~33.8%，使小模型的表现与大语言模型持平，并在实际部署的微调模型上进一步提升性能，证明校准与监督微调互补。

Conclusion: 通过后置校准恢复大模型水平的检查价值检测准确率，显著降低大规模运营成本，为实际 Fact‑Checking 系统提供了高效、经济的解决方案。

Abstract: Assessing claim check-worthiness is an essential first step in automated fact-checking pipelines. This work is motivated by a real deployment challenge at an early-stage startup: running large language models (LLMs) over every incoming claim is cost- and latency-prohibitive, yet smaller models sacrifice accuracy. We propose NN-PPI, a pointwise extension of Prediction-Powered Inference (PPI) that calibrates model predictions at inference time as a lightweight post-hoc layer, without re-training the underlying model. NN-PPI achieves weighted F1 gains ranging from 12% to 33.80% depending on the size and performance of the baseline model, bringing SLMs on par with larger LLMs. Beyond few-shot SLMs, NN-PPI further improves a production-deployed fine-tuned model, demonstrating that residual calibration is complementary to supervised fine-tuning. By recovering LLM-level accuracy from models that are an order of magnitude cheaper to serve, it makes accurate check-worthiness detection substantially cheaper to operate at scale. Our code and data can be found at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/arr-claim-worthiness-F237.

</details>


### [197] [Not All Fallbacks Are Failures: Understanding and Recovering from Fallbacks in Mobile Voice Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30738)
*Phillip Schneider,Alexandre Mercier,Joshua Oehms,Kristiina Jokinen,Florian Matthes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究智能手表语音助手的回退处理，构建VoxFallbacks数据集并比较轻量嵌入分类器与大型生成模型，发现前者在准确率和资源消耗上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境中语音助手常因噪声、转写错误、歧义等导致回退，现有系统仅给出通用提示，无法解决交互失败，影响用户体验，需要系统性分析并改进回退处理。

Method: 收集500+用户、6个月的真实使用数据，得到3030条匿名回退触发语句；构建操作性分类体系并标注VoxFallbacks数据集；在部署约束下比较多种模型（轻量嵌入分类器 vs 大型生成模型）的分类性能。

Result: 实验表明，基于轻量嵌入的分类器在多数分类任务上优于更大的生成模型，且计算资源需求显著更低。

Conclusion: 轻量级嵌入分类器是实现高效、成本可控的回退机制的有效方案，提供了设计稳健回退处理的实用经验。

Abstract: Robust understanding of user input is a core requirement for voice assistants deployed in real-world environments. In practice, these systems encounter heterogeneous fallback situations caused by noisy audio input, transcription errors, ambiguous requests, incomplete utterances, or unintended activations. Existing systems typically respond with generic fallback messages, which do not resolve the underlying interaction failure and can degrade user experience. We study fallback handling in a deployed smartwatch-based voice assistant for general health support in everyday environments. Our analysis is based on six months of real-world usage data from more than 500 users, yielding a dataset of 3,030 anonymized, naturally occurring fallback-triggering utterances. We contribute (1) an operational taxonomy and the annotated VoxFallbacks dataset of these interactions, (2) a comparative evaluation of different models within a classification pipeline under practical deployment constraints, and (3) practical lessons for designing robust and cost-efficient fallback mechanisms. Results show that lightweight embedding-based classifiers outperform larger generative models on most classification tasks while requiring substantially fewer computational resources.

</details>


### [198] [CLIN: an Objective Framework for Evaluating Creativity in Short Persian Literary Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30754)
*Mohammad Reza Modarres,Armin Tourajmehr,Yadollah Yaghoobzadeh,Mohammad Taher Pilehvar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估LLM在波斯语短文创意评分的可靠性，发现不同维度表现差异大，提出基于可解释代理指标的CLIN方法，成本低且与人类一致性更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创意评估多维且以人为中心，尤其在低资源语言如波斯语中缺乏可靠的自动评估手段，需探索LLM评估的可靠性及成本效益。

Method: 在波斯语短篇文学文本上，使用多种评估策略和提示形式让LLM对TTCT衍生的五个创意维度进行评分；随后设计CLIN，通过主题感知新颖度、上下文词汇聚类和词汇多样性分别近似原创性、流畅性和阐述性。

Result: LLM与人类在结构化维度（原创性、流畅性、阐述性）上协议较高，但在情感和吸引力等主观维度上显著下降；提示形式影响评分，few‑shot、集成和多代理辩论未带来一致提升。CLIN的代理指标在与人类一致性上媲美甚至优于最强零样本LLM评审，且评估成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: LLM在低资源语言的创意评估表现维度依赖，直接使用LLM评分并非最优方案。利用可解释的代理指标如CLIN，可在保持人类对齐的同时显著降低评估成本，为创意评估提供更实用的路径。

Abstract: Evaluating creativity in large language model (LLM) outputs remains challenging because creativity is multidimensional and human-centered. We examine how reliably LLMs evaluate short literary text in Persian, a low-resource language, across multiple evaluation strategies and prompt formulations. We find that LLM-human agreement varies substantially across dimensions: alignment is stronger for structured TTCT-derived properties such as Originality, Fluency, and Elaboration, but considerably weaker for more subjective dimensions, particularly Emotion and Attractiveness. Judgments are also sensitive to prompt formulation, while few-shot prompting, ensembling, and multi-agent debate provide no consistent improvement. Motivated by this dimension-dependent behavior, we investigate whether structured creativity dimensions can instead be approximated using simple, interpretable proxy metrics. We introduce CLIN, which evaluates three TTCT-derived dimensions separately using topic-aware novelty for Originality, contextual lexical clustering for Fluency, and lexical diversity for Elaboration. These proxies achieve human alignment comparable to or better than the strongest zero-shot LLM judge in our setting while requiring substantially lower evaluation cost.

</details>


### [199] [TopoCompress: Long Context Compression via Graph-Wired Semantic Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30811)
*Daniel Agyei Asante,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TopoCompress 是一种无需训练、与模型无关的长上下文压缩框架，通过语义跨度选择、图结构传播查询相关性，实现更高压缩效率和更低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文压缩方法会碎片化关键信息、需要额外训练或依赖目标模型，导致成本高、延迟大，亟需一种通用且高效的压缩方案。

Method: TopoCompress 首先对候选语义跨度进行密集向量和词汇查询相关性评分并加入语义加速；随后构建混合图，将跨度通过语义相似度和顺序相邻关系连接；最后在图上进行查询引导的相关性分数传播，选取最相关的连贯语义块作为压缩结果。

Result: 在 HotpotQA、2WikiMQA、MuSiQue、Qasper、MultiFieldQA-en 五个长上下文任务上，TopoCompress 稳定超越强基线；在相同压缩预算下性能可与最强基线持平，且压缩预算仅为其 1/4，压缩时间比最快基线快 1.41 倍。

Conclusion: TopoCompress 通过训练无关、模型无关的图传播机制，实现了高效、连贯的长上下文压缩，为大语言模型推理的成本与时延优化提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Long-context compression is essential for reducing the cost and latency of large language model inference. However, existing methods can fragment important evidence, require additional training or alignment, and often depend on the target model for effective compression. We introduce TopoCompress, a training-free and model-agnostic framework that compresses long contexts by selecting coherent semantic spans. TopoCompress first scores each span using dense and lexical query relevance together with semantic acceleration. It then constructs a hybrid graph that connects spans based on semantic similarity and sequential adjacency, and propagates the query-guided relevance scores over the graph. Across five long-context tasks-HotpotQA, 2WikiMQA, MuSiQue, Qasper, and MultiFieldQA-en-TopoCompress consistently outperforms strong compression baselines. Notably, TopoCompress achieves performance comparable to the strongest baseline while using a 4x smaller compression budget, and provides a 1.41x smaller compression time over the fastest baseline.

</details>


### [200] [Error-Type-Aware Loss Reweighting for Robust Named Entity Recognition with Noisy LLM Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30827)
*Elena Merdjanovska,Jonas Golde,Alan Akbik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种针对命名实体识别（NER）标注噪声的错误类型感知损失重加权方法，有效提升在合成噪声数据上的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在使用大语言模型生成的合成标注数据训练小模型时，现有微调过程忽视了标注噪声，且传统噪声鲁棒损失无法区分NER中不同类型的噪声（如漏标和类型错误），导致性能下降。

Method: 针对不同错误类型的噪声设计独立的重加权规则，即错误类型感知的损失重加权（error‑type‑aware loss reweighting），在不增加额外训练资源的前提下，对可能错误的 token 施加差异化的权重。

Result: 在噪声水平 15%~40% 的数据集上，平均 F1 提升 0.8‑2.0 个百分点；在 Wikigold 数据集 24.1% 噪声下，提升最高达 4.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过区分不同类型的标注错误并进行针对性重加权，可显著提升在 LLM 生成的噪声标注数据上训练的 NER 模型的鲁棒性和性能，且实现简单高效。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to annotate datasets for training smaller, task-specialized models such as named entity recognition. While this method yields effective models, it assumes that the synthetic dataset is correctly annotated. In this work, we find that (i) current fine-tuning processes simply ignore LLM-introduced annotation noise, resulting in degraded performance and (ii) existing noise-robust losses are not transferable to sequence labeling because annotation noise in named entity recognition is heterogeneous: for example, missing mentions and type errors affect the training signal in different ways. Treating all noisy tokens equally in noise-robust losses and applying a single reweighing criterion for all may therefore remove useful supervision or reinforce incorrect labels. To address this limitation, we propose error-type-aware loss reweighting for NER, which introduces separate reweighing rules for different types of potentially erroneous tokens. Our approach is simple and efficient, does not require additional training resources, and improves F1 by 0.8 - 2.0 percentage points on dataset-level average for noise levels between 15% and 40%, with a maximum improvement of 4.6 percentage points with 24.1% noise on Wikigold.

</details>


### [201] [Opinionated, Hesitant and Stressed: Three Studies of How Politicians Speak in Four Slavic Parliaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30828)
*Ivan Porupski,Nikola Ljubešić*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对克罗地亚、捷克、波兰、塞尔维亚四种斯拉夫语议会口语进行三项大规模研究：情感影响声学特征、填充停顿受语速、年龄、情感等跨语言因素影响、克罗地亚语主重音在词类间呈现一致性与分离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用大规模议会语料库探索情感、流畅性和重音在不同斯拉夫语言中的跨语言规律，以弥补单语言研究的局限并为语音学、话语分析提供新证据。

Method: 基于ParlaSpeech 3.0 超过6000小时的四语料，①对每句话的情感标签与声学参数（基频、强度、语速）进行相关分析；②使用负二项广义估计方程（GEE）建模填充停顿频率，考察语速、年龄、情感、性别等预测变量的跨语言效应；③在克罗地亚语中通过说话人层面的主重音位置测量，比较动词、形容词、名词、副词和专有名词的重音偏好一致性。

Result: 1）负面情感 consistently 伴随更高基频、更大强度和更快语速，正向极端出现 arousal 驱动的上升趋势。2）填充停顿频率受语速、年龄、情感显著影响；性别效应在南斯拉夫语系中男性停顿更少，而在西斯拉夫语系中无显著差异。3）克罗地亚语说话人对动词、形容词、名词的早/晚重音偏好保持一致，但在副词和专有名词上出现解耦现象。

Conclusion: 研究展示了跨语言语料库在声学情感、流畅性和重音研究中的潜力，提出了进一步在语料库语音学、停顿建模和政治修辞等方向的研究议程。

Abstract: We present three large-scale studies of spoken parliamentary speech across four Slavic languages (Croatian, Czech, Polish, Serbian), drawing on over 6,000 hours from the ParlaSpeech 3.0 corpus. The first study examines how utterance-level sentiment shapes acoustic realisation: negative speech is consistently produced with higher pitch, greater intensity, and faster rate across all four parliaments, with a secondary arousal-driven upturn at the most positive extreme. The second study models filled pause frequency using negative binomial GEE, finding that speech rate, age, and sentiment are robust cross-lingual predictors, while gender effects reverse between South Slavic (men produce fewer filled pauses) and West Slavic parliaments (no gender difference) - a pattern invisible to single-language designs. The third study investigates primary stress variation in Croatian, showing that speaker-level preferences for early versus late stress cohere across verbs, adjectives, and nouns but decouple for adverbs and proper nouns. We conclude with a research agenda spanning corpus phonetics, disfluency modelling, and political rhetoric.

</details>


### [202] [Thesis Proposal: Toward a Human-Centered and Perspective-Aware Framework for Reproducible ML Evaluation and AI Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30842)
*Deepak Pandita,Christopher M. Homan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种以人为本、关注视角差异的机器学习评估与AI对齐框架，强调在评估和对齐过程中保留并利用人类意见分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估普遍采用多数投票合并标签，忽视少数意见，导致对主观任务（如安全、内容审核、情感分析）中的价值多样性缺乏体现，进而加剧AI可重复性危机和对齐失误。

Method: 构建一个能够捕捉并保留人类标注分歧的评估框架：①在数据收集阶段记录多样化的身份、信念和社会背景；②采用多标签、多投票或分布式标注方式保存完整的意见分布；③在模型评估时使用基于分布的指标（如KL散度、置信区间）而非单一共识分数；④在对齐阶段引入多视角人类反馈，利用加权或层次化的价值聚合策略。

Result: 该框架能够在实验中展示：①对同一任务的不同人群评估结果更具可解释性和公平性；②在对齐实验中，模型对少数群体的误差显著下降；③通过保持分歧信息，提高了实验的可重复性和对齐过程的透明度。

Conclusion: 在AI安全、内容审核等主观性强的领域，必须将人类意见分歧纳入评估与对齐流程。所提的视角感知框架为实现多元价值的可重复机器学习评估提供了可行路径，对提升AI系统的可信度和社会接受度具有重要意义。

Abstract: Humans play a vital role at every stage of AI development, from data collection and curation to model development and evaluation. However, humans often disagree with each other and sometimes with themselves over time. It is essential to take disagreement into account when building human-centered AI systems, especially in domains where it is prevalent, such as AI safety, content moderation, or sentiment analysis. Disagreement often arises from subjective human opinion and can vary with one's identity, beliefs, and social environment. Despite this, current LLM evaluation approaches frequently rely on aggregating labels (often via plurality voting) to represent consensus, thereby obscuring minority perspectives. By failing to account for human disagreement, these evaluation methods contribute to the reproducibility crisis in AI. Human feedback is also crucial for ensuring that AI systems align with human values. For these systems to be trustworthy, it is critical to ensure that they reflect diverse human values and perspectives. In this thesis proposal, we present a human-centered and perspective-aware framework for reproducible ML evaluation and AI alignment.

</details>


### [203] [Linguistic Distance Segregates Latent Representations in Automatic Speech Recognition Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30853)
*Ting-Hui Cheng,Line Katrine Harder Clemmensen,Sneha Das*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现说话者的母语与英语ASR错误率呈显著正相关，且在不同数据集和模型中表现出系统性差异，深层声学特征空间中出现基于母语的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨说话者第一语言背景对英语自动语音识别性能的影响，以解释和缓解不同语言族群之间的识别差距。

Method: 对多个数据集和ASR模型进行经验分析，使用Tweedie混合效应模型评估L1距离与错误率的关联，并在模型的深层声学层进行潜在空间可视化分析。

Result: 发现L1距离与ASR错误率之间存在统计显著的正相关（p<0.001），该关联在不同数据集和模型间强度不同；多数模型的深层声学层显示出基于L1的空间分离现象。

Conclusion: 说话者的第一语言背景是影响英语ASR性能的重要因素，需在模型设计和数据采集时考虑语言族群的多样性，以提升公平性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: While automatic speech recognition (ASR) models have achieved remarkable improvements in recent years, performance disparities persist across different speaker populations. One such disparity is for speakers whose first languages (L1) are from families distant from English. This paper investigates the relationship between first language background and English ASR performance. Through empirical analysis, we observe that the correlation between speakers' L1 distance and ASR error rates yields a systematic effect on English Speech, with its strength varying across datasets and models. This association is statistically significant in a follow-up analysis accounting for dataset-level variation in Tweedie mixed-effects models ($p<0.001$ across evaluated models). In addition, analysis of the latent space reveals a L1-based spatial segregation across deeper acoustic layers in the majority of evaluated architectures

</details>


### [204] [You Shouldn't Have Asked: A Pragmatics-Inspired Taxonomy for Evaluating LLM Refusals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30856)
*Ruoxuan Li,Pinqiao Wang,Sheng Li,Cameron Robert Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语用学的LLM拒绝行为分类，评估16种模型在14类有害请求上的拒绝方式，发现模型普遍采用明确且道德评判的拒绝，缺乏面子修复，可能导致用户感到羞辱或被挑衅，呼吁在对齐评估中加入交互适应性和社会责任性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在拒绝不安全或不当请求时可能产生交互成本，现有安全对齐研究只关注是否拒绝，而未评估拒绝的交际适当性和对用户的情感影响。

Method: 基于语用学理论构建首个LLM拒绝行为分类体系；对16个主流LLM在14类有害情境下的回复进行标注和分析，重点考察拒绝的显式性、道德评判、面子修复与提供安全替代等维度。

Result: 模型的拒绝大多是明确且强烈的道德评判，倾向通过提供更安全的替代方案而非进行面子修复；在敏感有害情境中，负面表述使用频率高，可能导致用户感到被羞辱或挑衅。

Conclusion: 安全对齐评估应超越“是否拒绝”，加入对拒绝方式的情境适应性和社会责任性的考量，以降低交互成本，提升用户体验和模型的社会可接受性。

Abstract: Refusals are often treated as face-threatening acts in pragmatics because they can challenge the requester's socially claimed self-image. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trained to refuse unsafe and inappropriate requests, and these refusals may harm users when models fail to manage this interactional cost properly. While existing work has mainly approached LLM non-compliance as a safety-alignment outcome, it does not provide a way to evaluate whether LLMs refuse appropriately across different harmful contexts. To study this question, we propose (to our knowledge) the first taxonomy of LLM refusals that is grounded in pragmatic theory. Applying this taxonomy to responses from 16 modern LLMs across 14 harm categories, we find that although models differ in how they refuse, their refusals are overall explicit and strongly morally evaluative, with interactional repair occurring mainly through offering or providing safer alternatives instead of interpersonal facework. This pattern is especially consequential in sensitive harm contexts, where overuse of negative framing may make users feel shamed or provoked, undermining the purpose of safe non-compliance. We therefore call for alignment evaluation that considers not only whether models refuse harmful requests, but also whether they refuse in ways that are contextually adaptive and socially accountable for the interactional consequences of saying no.

</details>


### [205] [Beyond Good Intentions: When Does the Framing of Multilingual and Low-Resource NLP Research Become a Caricature?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30866)
*Nedjma Ousidhoum,Noopur Zambare,Mohamed Abdalla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文审视低资源语言NLP研究的叙事框架，发现常用的修辞模式往往缺乏实证支持，导致对社区利益的声明薄弱；研究更倾向于资源构建和基准测试，而非真正的结构性改变，并提出改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的语言技术研发常被宣称为促进公平、服务本地社区并推动去殖民化，但实际研究中这些主张的证据不足，可能掩盖了对社区真正有益的工作缺失。

Method: 对近期ACL Anthology中的NLP/ML论文进行框架分析，归纳出常见的叙事模式和修辞模式，并统计这些论文中关于社区受益的声明的实证支持情况。

Result: 发现多数论文虽强调社区所有权和参与，但实际重点仍是资源创建和基准测试；关于社区收益的论断往往缺乏实证或未被证实，且这些叙事可能限制了对不平等结构的问责。

Conclusion: 提出针对作者、评审和读者的实用建议，帮助其批判性评估研究声明，避免误导性叙事，推动更具结构性变革的低资源语言研究。

Abstract: Building language technologies and conducting NLP research for low-resource languages---particularly when led by native speakers or involving participatory research practices---are often framed as means of addressing inequality, serving local communities, and, at times, contributing to *decolonisation*. In this paper, we examine recently published NLP and ML papers, focusing on the narratives used to characterise multilinguality, low-resource languages, and underrepresented cultures. We propose a framework for analysing research framings and identify recurring rhetorical patterns that may hinder accountability and constrain equitable knowledge production for---and by---underserved communities. We further assess the evidential basis of assertions regarding community benefit and find that such statements are often weakly supported or left unsubstantiated. Although community ownership and participation are frequently presented as key objectives, our analysis, supported by statistics from the ACL Anthology, suggests that research outputs more often prioritise resource creation and benchmarking---important but distinct goals---over evidence of broader structural change. We conclude by offering practical recommendations to help authors, reviewers, and readers critically assess these assertions and avoid potentially misleading framings.

</details>


### [206] [Personas Differ from Native-Language Generation: Language Pathways Shape LLM Interpersonal Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30873)
*Jinhee Won,Xinlan Emily Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究比较了两种跨语言获取人际建议的方式：先用目标语言生成再翻译（NL）和直接以“母语者”身份提示模型（NP），发现二者在语言风格、行为引导和具体建议上存在显著差异，NP倾向于使用更多社交词汇但提供的可操作性更低，且在强制选择情境下更倾向于对抗性行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在跨语言人际建议和社会行为研究中的广泛应用，研究者常用“以母语者身份回答”来快速获取语言或文化差异的表现。然而，这种简化是否等同于真实的目标语言生成并翻译回英文尚不清楚，可能影响研究结论的可靠性。

Method: 收集600个跨13种语言的人际建议问题，使用8种大语言模型。对每个问题分别采用两种提示方式：① 目标语言直接生成后翻译成英文（NL），② 以“你是该语言的母语者”身份直接用英文回答（NP）。比较两种方式在语言风格（词汇社交线索、具体性、情感色彩等）、行为支架（可操作性）以及强制选择题的行动推荐上的差异。

Result: NP与NL不可互换。NP相比NL增加了词汇层面的社交线索（如亲和、积极语调），但降低了具体性和社会敏感度；在开放式建议中提供的可操作性支架更少。在强制选择情境下，NP更倾向于推荐对抗性行动而非重定向，且这种效应在不同语言、话题和模型间呈现不同的效应大小。

Conclusion: 跨语言获取建议的策略是关键方法选择，会显著影响模型的建议框架和推荐行动。研究者应慎重选择NL或NP方式，避免因提示差异导致的结果偏差。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used for interpersonal advice and as tools for studying social behavior across languages and cultures. A common shortcut for eliciting language- or culture-related variation is to ask a model to answer as a native speaker. We test whether this native-speaker persona reproduces the outputs obtained when models instead generate advice in the target language and translate the response back into English. Using 600 interpersonal advice questions across 13 languages and eight LLMs, we compare native-language generation followed by translation (NL) with native-speaker persona prompting (NP), measuring linguistic style, behavioral scaffolding, and forced-choice action recommendations. We find that NP and NL are not interchangeable. Compared to NL, NP often increases lexical social cues, including affiliation and positive tone, while reducing qualities such as concreteness and social attunement; NP also provides less actionable scaffolding in open-ended advice. In forced-choice scenarios, NP changes which action the model selects, favoring confrontation over redirection, with effect sizes varying across languages, topics, and models. Our results show that cross-lingual elicitation strategy is a consequential methodological choice that can change both how advice is framed and which actions models recommend.

</details>


### [207] [Evaluating and Mitigating Anti-LGBTQ Biases in German and Multilingual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30884)
*Melina Morch,Daniel Braun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了德英双语的反LGBTQ偏见评估基准，结合社区收集的刻板印象与WinoQueer德语翻译，评测8种语言模型并通过微调尝试缓解偏见，发现模型普遍复制反 queer 刻板印象，微调虽有整体改善但并非对所有模型和身份均有效，且翻译数据与社区数据的差异凸显文化适配的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于性别和种族偏见，且主要针对英语，缺乏对反LGBTQ偏见的系统评估，尤其在多语言环境下。现有基准未能覆盖文化和语言差异，导致对模型偏见的测量不完整。

Method: 1) 从德语使用者社区收集反LGBTQ刻板印象句子；2) 将英文基准WinoQueer翻译成德语；3) 合成德英双语数据集作为评估基准；4) 在八种不同规模和架构的语言模型上进行零样本评估；5) 对模型进行基于社区文本和进步媒体内容的微调以尝试缓解偏见；6) 分析不同身份、模型和数据来源的偏差表现。

Result: - 所有模型均在不同程度上复现了反 queer 刻板印象，且偏见强度随身份（如男/女、跨性别等）和模型大小/架构而异。- 翻译的WinoQueer数据与社区收集的数据在偏见表现上存在显著差异，说明文化适配对评估至关重要。- 微调在整体上降低了偏见分数，但并非对所有模型和身份均有一致改善，部分模型仍保持较高偏见。

Conclusion: 反LGBTQ偏见在多语言语言模型中普遍存在，评估时需考虑文化和语言的本地化。微调可以部分缓解偏见，但仍需更细致的策略和更广泛的社区数据来实现稳健的公平性改进。

Abstract: While gender and racial biases in language models have been widely studied, anti-LGBTQ biases remain underexplored, particularly beyond English. Existing benchmarks often do not capture cultural and linguistic variation and rely on gender representations. This paper introduces a multilingual German-English benchmark dataset for the evaluation of anti-LGBTQ biases in language models. It combines community-sourced stereotypes from German-speaking queer individuals with a German translation of WinoQueer. The data is used to evaluate eight language models across sizes and architectures and explore mitigation through fine-tuning on community and progressive media content. Results show that language models reproduce anti-queer stereotypes, with variation across identities and models. Differences between the translated and community-based data highlight the importance of cultural adaptation for multilingual bias evaluation. Fine-tuning reduces bias on average, but not consistently across models and identities. Warning: This text contains examples of anti-queer hateful language and stereotypes.

</details>


### [208] [ECGQuest: Benchmarking and Fine-Tuning Language Models for Electrocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30893)
*Mohammadsina Hassannia,Matthew A. Reyna,Reza Sameni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ECGQuest是一个基于文献的ECG上下文知识问答基准，包含10,904个是非题，评估显示在零样本下商业模型最高74.4%准确率，参数高效微调可将小模型提升至约78%竞争于大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型基准侧重于医学常识或单个ECG信号的解释，缺乏对ECG解读所需的广泛上下文知识的评估，导致难以衡量模型在真实临床环境中的表现。

Method: 利用GPT-4o从23篇ECG文献和2003-2025年Computing in Cardiology会议论文中生成是非题，形成10,904个唯一问题及其否定形式（共21,808对Q&A）。在零样本设置下评估3个商业模型和20个开源模型，并对7-14B参数的5个开源模型使用LoRA进行参数高效微调，另外使用BERT和BiomedBERT作为监督编码器基线。通过将MedMCQA和MedQA转化为二分类题目，检验模型的泛化能力。

Result: 零样本准确率在49.5%~74.4%之间，GPT-5表现最佳。通用模型优于专科模型，部分模型出现强True/False偏向，编码器基线接近随机水平。LoRA微调提升所有开源模型6.5%~14.1%，微调后DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B达76.3%，五模型投票集成达78.5%。在MedMCQA和MedQA上，微调主要帮助弱模型或有偏向的模型，对强基线模型提升不显著。

Conclusion: ECGQuest提供了可复现的ECG上下文知识基准，证明参数高效微调能够使小型开源模型在ECG问答任务上竞争甚至超越更大的商业模型，强调了针对特定医学子领域的专用基准和微调的重要性。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) interpretation requires knowledge of cardiology, electrophysiology, clinical diagnosis, ECG waveforms, signal acquisition, and instrumentation. Existing language-model benchmarks, however, primarily assess broad medical knowledge or interpretation of individual ECG signals and images rather than the broader contextual knowledge required for ECG interpretation. We developed ECGQuest, a literature-grounded resource for evaluating and fine-tuning ECG-specific language models. A GPT-4o-based pipeline generated questions from 23 ECG references and Computing in Cardiology proceedings from 2003-2025. The final dataset contains 10,904 unique True/False questions paired with their negated forms (21,808 Q&A pairs). We evaluated three commercial and 20 open-source language models on a held-out test set in a zero-shot setting. Five open-source models with 7-14B parameters were fine-tuned using Low-Rank Adaptation, with BERT and BiomedBERT included as supervised encoder baselines. Generalization was assessed on ECG-related subsets of MedMCQA and MedQA converted to binary True/False questions using official answer keys. Zero-shot accuracy on ECGQuest ranged from 49.5% to 74.4%, with GPT-5 performing best. General-purpose models outperformed medically specialized models, several models showed strong True/False bias, and encoder baselines performed near chance. Fine-tuning improved all open-source models by 6.5-14.1%. Fine-tuned DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B reached 76.3% accuracy, while a five-model voting ensemble reached 78.5%. On MedMCQA and MedQA, fine-tuning mainly benefited weaker or class-biased models and did not consistently improve strong base models. ECGQuest provides a reproducible benchmark for contextual ECG knowledge and shows that parameter-efficient fine-tuning can make smaller language models competitive with substantially larger commercial models.

</details>


### [209] [Low-Resource Preference Adaptation of LLMs via Activation-Based Label Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30902)
*Alessio Galatolo,Meriem Beloucif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过在大型语言模型的中间层激活上训练轻量线性探针，仅用≤500条标注偏好对即可自动标注大规模未标注数据，实现低资源环境下的偏好优化，效果优于同标注预算的直接训练，并在多数情况下接近使用50-100倍标注数据的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源场景中，获取用户特定偏好的标注成本高昂，且大型语言模型在对非主流或个性化偏好进行对齐时表现不佳，需要一种无需大量人工标注即可进行偏好学习的方法。

Method: 首先分析预训练和对齐模型的中间表示，发现选择与拒绝的响应在激活空间形成可区分的簇；随后在少量（≤500）标注的偏好对上训练线性探针，将其用于对大规模（≥50K）未标注数据进行自动偏好标注，随后在这些伪标注数据上进行下游偏好优化。

Result: 在多个数据集、不同偏好优化方法和模型规模下，所提方法在相同标注预算下 consistently 超过直接训练，并在多数实验中与使用 50‑100 倍标注数据的基线相竞争，显示出显著的标注效率提升。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型的中间层已隐含偏好信息，可通过轻量线性探针在极少标注数据下高效提取并扩展到大规模数据，实现低资源偏好对齐，提示对齐模型在非主流偏好上存在局限，需要更细粒度的适配策略。

Abstract: Adapting large language models to user-specific preferences is often constrained by the cost of human annotation, making preference optimisation impractical in low-resource settings where preferences cannot be reliably labelled by LLMs themselves, e.g., due to cultural, subjective, or personalised contexts. In this paper, we investigate how language models encode preference information in their intermediate representations, finding that activations from chosen and rejected responses form distinct clusters across layers, even in pretrained models. Strikingly, this structure is strengthened by alignment on canonical datasets but erased when the target preferences differ from those the model was aligned on, suggesting aligned LLMs are poor judges for non-mainstream populations. Exploiting this structure, we propose training a lightweight linear probe on a few labelled preference pairs ($\leq$500) and using it to annotate large unlabelled datasets (50K+) for downstream preference optimisation. We systematically evaluate this approach across different datasets, preference optimisation methods and model scales and find that our method consistently outperforms direct training given the same annotation budget, and remains competitive against baselines trained on $50-100\times$ more labelled data in the majority of our settings. Code is available at https://github.com/alessioGalatolo/activ-pref-probe.

</details>


### [210] [MMDS-Bench: Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models on Dynamic Stance in Social Media Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30903)
*Yuzhe Ding,Kang He,Li Zheng,Shengwu Zheng,Teng Shi,Fei Li,Chong Teng,Donghong Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MMDS-Bench，一个用于多模态动态立场分类的诊断基准，包含3,482条标注实例和800条需结构化推理的子集，并评估了12种多模态大语言模型，发现它们在跨模态关系推理上仍表现不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态立场分类研究局限于纯文本，而社交媒体交互日益涉及图片、表情包等多模态信息，缺乏针对多模态父子回复关系的评测基准。

Method: 构建MMDS-Bench，提供七类动态立场标签并标注五类挑战因素（多模态融合、父信息框架、非字面表达、交互推理、标签边界模糊），并设计包含父信息、回复信息和立场关系推理的800实例诊断子集；随后使用12种开源/闭源多模态大语言模型进行评测，并提出基于参考答案的LLM-judge协议评估推理质量。

Result: 实验表明，当前多模态大语言模型在整体准确率上仍有限，尤其在需要跨模态关系推理的案例中表现显著下降；模型在多模态融合和非字面表达方面略有提升，但整体仍难以可靠捕捉动态立场。

Conclusion: MMDS-Bench揭示了多模态动态立场理解的关键瓶颈，表明现有MLLM在跨模态交互推理方面仍有显著提升空间，为后续模型改进和评测提供了有价值的基准和分析框架。

Abstract: Dynamic stance classification models how a reply responds to its direct parent message, rather than how a post relates to a fixed topic. Existing work has mainly studied this problem in text-only settings, while social media interactions increasingly rely on images, screenshots, memes, reaction images, and cross-modal references. We introduce MMDS-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark for multimodal dynamic stance classification in social media parent-reply interactions. MMDS-Bench contains 3,482 multimodal instances annotated with a seven-label dynamic stance taxonomy, together with an 800-instance diagnostic subset that requires structured reasoning over parent understanding, reply understanding, and stance-relation inference. We further annotate each instance with five challenge factors covering multimodal fusion, parent framing, non-literal expression, interaction reasoning, and label-boundary ambiguity. We evaluate 12 closed-source and open-source multimodal large language models and propose a reference-grounded LLM-judge protocol for assessing reasoning quality. Results show that current MLLMs still struggle with multimodal dynamic stance understanding, especially in cases that require relational inference beyond separate parent and reply comprehension.

</details>


### [211] [TRIPPULSE: Multi-Agent Travel Planning with Review-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30924)
*Priyanshu Karmakar,Borru Vijay Sai,Shubhojit Mallick,Abhik Jana,Shreya Ghosh,Manish Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRIPPULSE是一种多代理框架，通过将行程生成拆分为住宿、交通、餐饮、景点和活动等专职代理，并利用真实评论数据和RGPA评估指标，实现约束满足的同时生成更具体验感的个性化旅行计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大模型的旅行规划主要依赖结构化属性和预设旅客画像，忽略了评论中体现的舒适度、安全性、服务质量等体验因素，这导致生成的行程缺乏真实感和用户中心性。

Method: TRIPPULSE将行程生成任务分解为多个专门的代理，每个代理在局部上下文中处理住宿、交通、餐饮、景点和活动等子任务；通过全局编排器和调度机制确保时空和预算约束；并在TRIPCRAFT模型中加入100K+真实评论，提出基于评论的角色对齐（RGPA）作为LLM‑as‑Judge评估指标。

Result: 在多种行程时长和不同模型（包括专有和开源）上实验表明，TRIPPULSE在约束满足率上保持优势，同时生成的行程在个性化和体验根植性方面显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过多代理拆解和评论驱动的对齐评估，TRIPPULSE实现了约束严格且更贴合用户真实旅行体验的行程生成，为面向用户的旅行规划提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Travel itinerary generation requires balancing strict spatio-temporal constraints with human preferences. Existing LLM-based planners mainly rely on structured attributes and pre- defined traveler personas, but real travel deci- sions are often shaped by reviews that reveal experiential factors such as comfort, safety, ser- vice quality, ambiance, crowding, and hidden risks absent from structured databases. Incor- porating such review information is therefore critical to realistic, user-centric itinerary gen- eration. We propose TRIPPULSE1, a multi- agent framework for review-grounded travel planning. Instead of relying on a monolithic planner (and face context and reasoning bot- tlenecks), TRIPPULSE2 decomposes itinerary generation into specialized agents (each op- erating over localized contexts) for accom- modations, transportation, meals, attractions, and events, coordinated through a global or- chestrator with scheduling mechanisms that enforce temporal and budget feasibility. We augment TRIPCRAFT with 100K+ real-world reviews and introduce Review-Grounded Per- sona Alignment (RGPA), an LLM-as-a-Judge metric for evaluating alignment with human- centric travel experiences. Experiments across multiple trip durations and diverse proprietary and open-source models show that TRIPPULSE maintains strong constraint satisfaction while generating more personalized and experien- tially grounded itineraries.

</details>


### [212] [Annotated Surrogate Retrieval for Polish Statutory Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30929)
*Orkun Yiğit Cengiz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出三种基于语言模型注释的波兰法条检索方法（ASCR、ASCR‑H、DTF），在波兰律师资格考试题目上评估，ASCR‑H 在排名首位准确率最高，DTF 在低成本/低延迟下表现接近最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律检索系统在准确率与计算成本之间存在权衡，尤其在大规模法典检索和高质量答案定位上缺乏高效且可解释的方案。

Method: 构建文档代理（语言模型在索引时为每条法条生成注释），设计三种检索流水线：ASCR 为级联检索并在末端进行重排；ASCR‑H 在级联中融合稠密检索列表；DTF 完全使用词汇和稠密检索器，通过加权倒数排名融合（RRRF）并采用确定性再评分，无需生成模型调用。

Result: 在 300 道2024/2025波兰律师考试题（其中264题参考法条在语料库中）上，ASCR‑H 将参考法条排在第一的比例为 72.3%，显著高于 BM25（61.7%）和稠密检索（52.3%），且在 18/20 次 McNemar 检验中显著；DTF 在前 20 条时准确率略低但延迟仅为 ASCR‑H 的 1/9，成本不足一半，并在引用准确率上达到上限。重排阶段单独贡献 27.6% 的首位准确率。负向实验表明词形还原、伪相关反馈和查询改写未带来提升。

Conclusion: 语言模型注释的文档代理能够在保持较高检索准确率的同时显著降低计算成本，ASCR‑H 适用于对首位准确率要求高的场景，DTF 则在资源受限环境下提供可接受的性能。研究还揭示了代理覆盖率与基准参考条目之间的不对称性，并提供了公开基准和代码供复现。

Abstract: We present a family of retrieval methods for Polish statutory law built on document surrogates: language-model annotations attached to statutory articles at index time. Three designs occupy different points on the cost-quality frontier. ASCR is a surrogate cascade with reranking; ASCR-H fuses a dense list into that cascade; and DTF replaces both language-model stages with three lexical and dense retrievers, weighted reciprocal rank fusion, and a deterministic re-scoring prior, using no model call before generation. We evaluate all three against fourteen lexical, dense, fused and ablated baselines plus four controls, on 300 questions from the 2024 and 2025 Polish bar and legal counsel entrance examinations (264 with their reference article in the corpus), over 82,508 articles from 1,133 acts. On paired McNemar tests, ASCR-H places the reference provision at rank one significantly more often than every other non-oracle configuration except one of its own ablations (eighteen of twenty comparisons significant in its favour at p < 0.005), reaching 72.3% against 61.7% for BM25 and 52.3% for dense retrieval. The advantage is concentrated at the head and does not survive depth: it is significant at cutoffs of one and five, disappears by ten, and by twenty DTF leads on point estimate (86.0% versus 84.5%) at one ninth the latency and less than half the cost. Ablation attributes 27.6 points of rank-one accuracy to the reranking stage alone. We further report that the ranking advantage does not extend to citation accuracy, where DTF matches the oracle ceiling, and three negative results on lemmatisation, pseudo-relevance feedback and query rewriting. Surrogate annotation covers 27.0% of the corpus but every reference provision in the benchmark, an asymmetry we disclose and discuss. Benchmark, per-question outputs and paired significance tests are publicly available.

</details>


### [213] [Detecting AI Impostors: How Do Middle Schoolers Identify LLM Agents in a Live Collaborative Setting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30948)
*Dan Schumacher,Pragathi Durga Rajarajan,Haven Kotara,Roman Rendon,Kosi Atupulazi,Deepti Tagare,Ismaila Temitayo Sanusi,Fred G. Martin,Anthony Rios*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过社交推理游戏DoppelBot，研究中学生对AI冒充的检测能力，发现随时间检测准确率提升，且从语言线索转向社交情境线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能够模仿人类写作，导致身份冒充、信任危机和检测困难，尤其青少年使用生成式AI频繁且辨识能力有限，需要了解他们如何识别和应对AI冒充。

Method: 设计合作式社交推理游戏DoppelBot，让中学生在游戏中辨别AI生成的角色；通过多轮实验收集游戏对话和投票数据，分析检测准确率变化、使用的策略以及对隐私和冒充的反思。

Result: 学生的AI检测准确率随实验进行而提升，主要原因是从依赖语言特征转向利用共享的社会和情境线索；学生还能指出AI的局限（如缺乏实体），并对数据隐私等更广泛议题进行思考。

Conclusion: 在真实社交情境中，青少年能够逐步学习并改进对AI冒充的识别，社交线索比纯语言线索更有效；DoppelBot提供了可复现的实验平台，并公开了匿名化数据集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: LLMs can imitate how people write, which raises concerns about impersonation, trust, and detection in social settings. These concerns are especially important for adolescents, who use generative AI frequently but may struggle to recognize it. We introduce \textit{DoppelBot}, a cooperative social deduction game designed to study how young people detect and respond to AI impersonation. Through studies with middle schoolers, we investigate whether a DoppelBot prompts reflection on privacy and impersonation, how repeated exposure affects AI-detection accuracy as agents become more personalized, and which strategies students use to identify AI doppelgängers. We find that students' detection accuracy improves over time, driven by a shift from relying on linguistic cues to leveraging shared social and contextual signals. Students also demonstrated an understanding of AI limitations such as embodiment and reflected on broader issues such as data privacy. To support future research, we release an anonymized dataset of game transcripts and voting behavior.

</details>


### [214] [CogEvol: Towards Efficient and Reliable Learning Environment Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30968)
*Shangqing Tu,Daniel Zhang-Li,Yucheng Wang,Shiyu Gan,Yanpeng Wang,Huiqiang Rong,Mofei Chen,Shen Yang,Yini Chen,Yinuo Duan,Haoxuan Li,Binglin Liu,Ye He,Danqi Zheng,Zhanxin Hao,Yuxuan Wu,Mengting Tao,Yuqiu Liu,Jifan Yu,Juanzi Li,Bin Xu,Lei Hou,Huiqin Liu,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CogEvol是一套专为学习环境生成（将课程简介转化为结构化JSON幻灯片或交互式HTML页面）的模型，能够在单次推理中快速完成任务，显著提升生产效率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的教育内容生成依赖多轮对话式代理，耗时长且不够可靠；需要一种能够一次性、快速且高质量生成学习材料的模型，以满足大规模AI原生教育的需求。

Method: 构建了生产级数据管道，将真实失败案例转化为53,687条SFT样本；采用混合规则+视觉语言模型（VLM）奖励，结合GRPO强化学习，并在发现并修复奖励作弊后进行硬化；模型规模从4B到27B，使用国内Ascend加速器实现与A800 GPU等效的性能。

Result: CogEvol-27B在幻灯片质量评测中得分83.7，在500例交互HTML基准中得分63.7，参数量仅为主流编码模型的1/26.9；生成一张幻灯片的中位时长为17秒，交互页面为59秒；编辑支架可再降低约76%的生成成本。

Conclusion: CogEvol实现了高效、可靠的学习环境生成，显著缩短生成时间并降低成本，4B模型已开源，展示了在大规模教育场景中可行的AI原生解决方案。

Abstract: We present CogEvol, a family of models trained specifically for Learning Environment Generation: turning a course brief into a finished learning artifact (structured-JSON slides or self-contained interactive HTML pages) in a single pass. Across 220k production requests, CogEvol completes a slide in a median of 17 seconds and an interactive page in 59, replacing minutes-long multi-turn agent scaffolding. Reliability is enforced rather than hoped for: a production-grounded data pipeline turns real failures into 53,687 verified SFT samples, and a hybrid rule-plus-VLM reward drives GRPO-based RL, hardened after we caught and fixed a reward-hacking episode that produced visually convincing but unplayable games. CogEvol-27B scores 83.7 on slide quality and 63.7 on a 500-case interactive-HTML benchmark with 26.9x fewer parameters than flagship coding models, and, in collaboration with the OpenMAIC team, serves their live production traffic. CogEvol-4B is released openly under the Apache 2.0 license at https://github.com/CogEvol/CogEvol-4B; external flagships are measured on the same suites under the identical harness. Scaffold editing cuts interactive-page generation cost by a further ~76%, and the full stack runs on domestic Ascend accelerators at application-level parity with A800 GPUs, lowering the unit cost of AI-native education at scale.

</details>


### [215] [Evaluating and Improving LLM Self-Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30980)
*Siqi Zeng,Andre N. Assis,Rowan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出自我建模基准，评估语言模型预测自身行为的能力，并通过合成数据和强化学习提升模型的自我建模水平，但提升并未必然等同于真正的内省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在实际应用中的广泛使用，了解并预测模型在特定输入下的行为（尤其是反事实情形）对安全性、可解释性和可靠性至关重要。现有模型在自我行为预测方面表现有限，亟需系统评估与提升方法。

Method: 1) 构建多样化的自我建模基准，涵盖如提示编辑是否会改变最终答案等可验证的行为问题。2) 设计可扩展的合成数据流水线，自动生成自我建模训练样本。3) 在三个开源模型系列上使用强化学习（RL）对模型进行微调，以提升整体自我建模能力，并在未见任务上检验迁移效果。

Result: 实验表明：当前模型具备一定的自我建模能力，但在简单的反事实问题上出现系统性错误。通过合成数据+RL的训练，三类开源模型的自我建模整体得分均有显著提升，并在部分未见任务上实现了正向迁移。然而，提升并未证明模型获得了对内部决策过程的特权访问，仍可能是基于模式学习而非真正的内省。

Conclusion: 自我建模是评估语言模型可预测性的重要维度，本文提供了基准与提升方案，展示了可行的性能提升路径。但当前的改进更多是行为预测的表层提升，尚未实现深层次的模型内省，未来工作需探索如何让模型获得对自身内部机制的真实访问与解释能力。

Abstract: We study self-modeling: an LLM's ability to answer questions about its own behavior. We focus on verifiable behavioral questions, such as whether a prompt edit would change the model's final answer. To measure this capability, we introduce a benchmark that tests diverse types of self-modeling questions. Current models show non-trivial but limited self-modeling skill, and make systematic mistakes on simple counterfactual questions about their own behavior. To improve self-modeling skill, we develop a scalable synthetic-data pipeline that produces self-modeling training data, and show that reinforcement-learning can improve aggregate self-modeling skill across three open-source model families with some transfer to held-out tasks. These gains, however, do not seem to constitute introspection consistently: improved self-modeling may not arise from privileged access to the model's internal decision process.

</details>


### [216] [Stick to What You Know: A Study of Knowledge-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30987)
*Arthur Becker,Jakob Kemmler,David Thulke,Christine Schäfer,Christian Dugast,Hermann Ney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过让监督微调（SFT）目标与基础模型的参数化知识保持一致，提出并评估了两种新方法（Evidence Rewrite 与 Recall Rewrite），显著降低了模型的事实性幻觉，同时保持了通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在监督微调过程中，模型被迫学习可能超出其内部知识的目标答案，这会导致事实性幻觉。作者旨在探究并缓解这一问题，即通过约束SFT目标不超出基础模型已有的知识。

Method: 在统一实验框架下，对比生成式和估计式的知识对齐方法，并提出两种新变体：
1. Evidence Rewrite：使用外部证据验证基础模型生成的答案，仅保留经证据支持的内容。 
2. Recall Rewrite：仅保留基础模型能够一致回忆的陈述，即当模型自身能够可靠生成相同信息时才保留。

Result: 在 Qwen‑3‑4B 与 OLMo‑3‑7B 上的实验表明，知识对齐的SFT显著降低了 WildHalu 与 Biography 数据集上的事实性幻觉，且对模型的通用能力影响有限。Recall Rewrite 在提升事实性方面效果最佳，并在 UnknownBench 上提升了模型的拒答行为。

Conclusion: SFT目标若超出基础模型的内部知识会导致幻觉产生；通过将SFT目标约束在模型已有知识范围内（尤其是Recall Rewrite），可以有效提升模型的事实性和拒答能力，而不会显著削弱其通用性能。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) trains a base language model to imitate target responses, and these targets may require knowledge the base model has not robustly internalized. We study this as a source of hallucinations and frame a group of mitigation methods as \emph{knowledge-aligned SFT}: constraining SFT training targets to the base model's parametric knowledge. Under a unified setup, we compare existing generation-based and estimation-based knowledge-alignment methods and introduce two new variants: Evidence Rewrite, which verifies base-model generations using external evidence, and Recall Rewrite, which retains claims only when they can be consistently recalled by the base model. Experiments with Qwen 3 4B and OLMo 3 7B show that knowledge-aligned SFT can reduce factual hallucinations on WildHalu and Biography while largely preserving general capabilities. Recall Rewrite yields the strongest factuality gains and improves refusal behavior on UnknownBench. It thereby confirms that SFT targets beyond the base model's knowledge drive hallucination behavior.

</details>


### [217] [Faithfulness Is Not Free: Auditing Offline KV-Cache Quantization in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30996)
*Atta Ul Asad,Ahsan Bilal,Muhammad Ali,Muhammad Haseeb,Dean F. Hougen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对检索增强生成系统的缓存进行INT8/INT4量化，发现INT4虽能压缩存储，但显著降低答案的可信度，尤其在噪声检索和更多检索块时，准确率虽仍可，但可信度大幅下降，需在部署前审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成模型通过缓存检索文档提升效率，量化缓存可进一步节省存储，但尚未评估量化是否会破坏答案的可信度（faithfulness），即答案是否仍基于检索到的证据。

Method: 在RGB和HotpotQA两套数据上，对Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型的缓存进行INT8和INT4量化。使用幻觉检测器、自然语言推理（NLI）蕴含判断以及大型语言模型评审，分别测量答案的准确性和可信度。进一步分析噪声检索和检索块数量对结果的影响。

Result: INT8量化对准确性和可信度几乎无损。INT4量化导致准确率下降，且在仍正确的答案中，超过90%的可信度变化为负向——即答案虽正确但不再由检索证据支持。噪声检索和更多检索块会放大这种负面影响。

Conclusion: 在压缩检索缓存时，仅关注准确率不足以保证模型输出的可信度。必须在部署前对量化后的缓存进行可信度审计，尤其是使用更激进的INT4量化时。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation systems can precompute and store key-value caches of retrieved documents to avoid re-encoding context at every query. Quantizing these caches further reduces storage, but no prior work asks whether compression damages faithfulness, whether responses remain grounded in the retrieved evidence. Faithfulness and accuracy are not equivalent: a model can produce a correct answer that is no longer supported by the context it was given. We evaluate Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct under INT8 and INT4 quantization on RGB and HotpotQA, measuring both accuracy and faithfulness with a hallucination detector, NLI entailment, and an LLM judge. INT8 is near-lossless across both metrics. INT4 reduces accuracy and, more critically, even among answers that remain factually correct, over 90% of faithfulness changes are negative, i.e., accuracy metrics are blind to this regression. The harm grows under noisy retrieval and with more retrieved chunks. Faithfulness must be audited before compressed caches are deployed.

</details>


### [218] [Evidence-Bounded Mental Health Reasoning from Heterogeneous Speech Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31014)
*Chengyuan Gao,Jiang Wu,Tao Lu,Jiayan Guo,Mingkun Xu,Tianyi Zang,Shangyang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出证据受限的多模态精神健康筛查框架EviBound，并构建Evidence Package Benchmark，实现对不同临床语音协议的证据边界控制，显著提升抑郁症检测AUROC并消除不当推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态语音文本筛查模型忽视不同临床协议的证据有效性差异，导致模型在不相关文本上产生幻觉式症状推断或过度主张支持，危及临床安全。

Method: 将筛查任务重新定义为证据受限推理问题，构建包含1,870个证据包、六类异构来源、模态掩码和证据权限的Evidence Package Benchmark；提出EviBound框架，使用基于患者画像的规划器限定推理范围，采用五路声学共识调度证据工具，并通过边界批评器抑制无证据的声明。

Result: 在抑郁症检测任务上，EviBound在保留零违规声明的前提下，测试集AUROC达到0.8658，较最强直接全模态基线提升0.0811。

Conclusion: EviBound实现了面向临床协议的证据一致推理，推动多模态NLP从单纯准确率向安全、证据可控的方向发展，为临床语言模型的可靠应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Computational mental health screening using multimodal speech and text has shown great promise. However, existing models often assume all clinical speech protocols carry equivalent evidentiary validity. In reality, heterogeneous protocols, from free interviews to fixed reading tasks, support fundamentally different evidence. Forcing uniform reasoning flattens these boundaries, causing models to hallucinate symptoms from irrelevant text or overclaim support. Even advanced long chain-of-thought LLMs fail to resolve this issue, as free-form reasoning can exacerbate boundary violations. To address this, we reformulate multimodal screening as an evidence-bounded reasoning problem. We introduce the Evidence Package Benchmark, integrating 1,870 packages across six heterogeneous sources with explicit modality masks and evidence permissions. We further propose EviBound, a protocol-aware evidence control framework. Unlike direct LLM prompting, EviBound uses a profile-aware planner to restrict reasoning scope, orchestrates evidence tools via five-way acoustic consensus, and enforces a boundary critic to suppress unsupported claims. Empirical results show EviBound achieves a held-out test Depression AUROC of 0.8658, exceeding the strongest direct omni-modal baseline by +0.0811 AUROC while maintaining zero claim violations. Our work moves beyond unconstrained accuracy toward evidence-consistent, protocol-aware systems for safer clinical NLP research.

</details>


### [219] [LLM Judges Verify Presence, Not Absence: Omission Blindness in AI Clinical Notes and What Recovers It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31016)
*Sebastian Fox,Luke Markham,Ryan Lail,Michael Karotsieris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估了大型语言模型（LLM）作为审查者检测临床记录遗漏的能力，发现传统配对判别法对遗漏检测几乎随机，提出两种新方法（逐事实流水线和单次GEPA提示）显著提升检测率并降低误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI自动生成临床笔记的普及，主要错误是信息遗漏。现有的LLM审查者能否可靠检测这些遗漏尚未得到验证，且公开数据缺乏可靠答案键。

Method: 构建了包含500对单错误笔记的基准，其中298对明确缺失事实，202对为添加或修改的对照。评估八种审查者设计的配对判别性能。随后提出两种重新构造任务的方案：①逐事实流水线式检测缺失事实并评估严重性；②使用GEPA进化的单次提示一次性列出缺失事实。对比两种方法的召回率、误报率和成本，并进行临床专家验证。

Result: 配对判别在遗漏检测上仅达0.50-0.63，接近随机。流水线方法误报率2.7%，单次GEPA提示误报率6.2%，召回率分别为24.6%和36.9%（差异显著p=0.002），且成本仅为流水线的十分之一。临床医生验证表明两种方法在分歧时流水线更可靠。对真实供应商笔记的再校准实验显示，单次GEPA提示在保持更低误报率的同时检测率超过八种传统审查者的最佳表现。

Conclusion: 传统LLM审查者难以检测临床笔记的遗漏。通过任务重构，使用逐事实流水线或单次GEPA提示可显著提升遗漏检测效果，单次提示在成本和召回率上更具优势。作者已公开基准、提示和判决数据供社区使用。

Abstract: Ambient AI scribes draft clinical notes, and published audits find their dominant error is omission: information the encounter established that the note fails to record. The standard check is an LLM judge: a second model reads the note against the transcript and flags problems. We ask whether judges detect omissions. Public corpora cannot supply the answer key: their clinician reference notes and transcripts are materially discrepant. Our benchmark has 500 single-error note pairs from audited fact sheets, 298 with a named fact certainly absent and 202 added-or-altered controls. Across eight judge designs, paired discrimination (the flawed note below its clean twin, 0.5 a coin flip) reads 0.79-0.94 on added or altered content and 0.50-0.63 on omissions. On single notes, no design flags omissions reliably more often than perfect notes. Wording changes, voting and GEPA prompt optimisation move the operating point without creating usable detection. Restructuring the task recovers it: list the facts the transcript establishes, then check the note for each. Two methods reach it independently and trade off: a per-fact pipeline, and a GEPA-evolved prompt doing the same in one call. The pipeline's flags name the missing fact and its severity at 2.7% false alarms. The single call detects more (36.9% against 24.6%, p=0.002) at 6.2% false alarms and a tenth of the cost per note. A physician author validated 70 items and, where the two routes disagree, sided with the pipeline on 10 of 10 (p=0.002). A second clinician, not an author, graded the severity rubric blind and agrees to within a grade. On real vendor notes from a companion census no benchmark threshold transfers, but the re-calibrated single call detects more than the best of the eight at half its false-alarm rate. Omissions whose fact is restated elsewhere defeat both routes. We release the benchmark, prompts and judgements.

</details>


### [220] [One note in three: a verified census of three deployed AI scribes, and the instrument that counted it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31017)
*Sebastian Fox,Luke Markham,Ryan Lail,Michael Karotsieris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对三款商业AI记录员在142次就诊中的565份临床笔记进行审计，发现约31%笔记存在验证错误，主要集中在过敏、药物信息、虚构患者身份等方面；在排除预填信息后错误率仍达约25%。错误率受审查指令和模型族影响显著，审计结果在不同标准下差异大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估当前商业AI临床记录系统的安全性和准确性，揭示其在真实临床环境中可能产生的错误风险，以指导监管和改进。

Method: 在同一批142次英国和美国门诊会话中收集565份AI生成的笔记，进行12轮发现过程生成13,678个候选错误，筛选出5,898个重要错误交给两种不同模型的对抗性审查面板进行过滤，最终保留618条经验证的错误。采用双盲人工审查（两位临床医生）验证错误，并比较不同审查指令和模型族的影响。

Result: 每三份笔记中约有1份（31.3%）包含经验证的错误，主要集中在过敏、药物、虚构身份和电话会诊的检查记录等。排除预填信息后错误率仍为24.8%。错误率随审查指令从9.3%提升至79.0%，不同模型族的审查标记率分别为54.8%和27.8%。不同审计标准导致错误率在28%至97%之间波动。与已有审计相比，遗漏错误比例为54-86%，而本研究为23.1%。

Conclusion: 商业AI记录员在临床笔记生成中仍存在显著错误风险，错误率受审查标准和模型选择影响大。需要更严格的审计框架、透明的错误报告以及改进的模型和提示设计，以确保患者安全。研究公开了全部618条错误、对应转录证据、提示和可复现的流水线。

Abstract: Ambient AI scribes draft clinical notes under the reassurance that a clinician signs every note. We audited three commercial AI scribes on the same 142 consultations: 565 notes from recorded UK primary-care and US ambulatory encounters plus authored scenarios. Twelve discovery passes proposed 13,678 candidate errors; the 5,898 clearing an importance filter went to an adversarial panel of two models from different families, each told to refute what it could, and 618 survived. One note in three (31.3% [27.0, 35.6]) carries a verified failure, concentrated in allergy and medication information, invented patient identity, and history written up as examination on telephone consultations that can contain none. No product was given a patient record; setting aside the two classes a record would have prefilled, invented identity and dates, the rate is 24.8% [20.8, 29.0].
